\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{8\cdot 2pL_{x}L_{\theta}\sqrt{2\log\left(\frac{2pMT_{t}}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)}\log\left(\frac{2p}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)}{\sqrt{MT_{t}}}.

(6)

Now we investigate
\|\bm{C}_{t}-\mathbb{E}[\bm{C}_{t}]\|
. Recall that in Eq. (
5
), for any
t>0
,
m\in[M]
,
j\in[T_{t}]
and
i\in[p]
,
|\tilde{\eta}_{t,m,j,i}|\leq R
. Then, we have
\|\tilde{\bm{C}}_{t,m,j}\|\leq\frac{1}{MT_{t}}\cdot pR^{2}
.

Recall that for any
t>0
,
m\in[M]
and
j\in[T_{t}]
, with probability at least
1-2p\exp(-\frac{R^{2}}{2})
,
|\eta_{t,m,j,i}|\leq R
for all
i\in[p]
. Thus, with probability at least
1-2p\exp(-\frac{R^{2}}{2})
,
\|\bm{C}_{t,m,j}\|\leq\frac{1}{MT_{t}}\cdot pR^{2}
. Then, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbb{E}[\bm{C}_{t,m,j}]-\mathbb{E}[\tilde{\bm{C}}_{t,m,j}]\right\|\leq

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbb{E}\left[\bm{C}_{t,m,j}\cdot\mathbbm{1}\left\{\left\|\bm{C}_{t,m,j}\right\|\geq\frac{pR^{2}}{MT_{t}}\right\}\right]\right\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\bm{C}_{t,m,j}\right\|\cdot\mathbbm{1}\left\{\left\|\bm{C}_{t,m,j}\right\|\geq\frac{pR^{2}}{MT_{t}}\right\}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{pR^{2}}{MT_{t}}\cdot\mathbbm{1}\left\{\left\|\bm{C}_{t,m,j}\right\|\geq\frac{pR^{2}}{MT_{t}}\right\}\right]+\left[\left(\left\|\bm{C}_{t,m,j}\right\|-\frac{pR^{2}}{MT_{t}}\right)\cdot\mathbbm{1}\left\{\left\|\bm{C}_{t,m,j}\right\|\geq\frac{pR^{2}}{MT_{t}}\right\}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{pR^{2}}{MT_{t}}\cdot\Pr\left[\left\|\bm{C}_{t,m,j}\right\|\geq\frac{pR^{2}}{MT_{t}}\right]+\int_{0}^{\infty}\Pr\left[\left\|\bm{C}_{t,m,j}\right\|-\frac{pR^{2}}{MT_{t}}>x\right]dx

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{pR^{2}}{MT_{t}}\cdot 2p\cdot\exp\left(-\frac{R^{2}}{2}\right)+\frac{2p}{MT_{t}}\int_{R}^{\infty}\bm{y}\cdot\Pr\left[\left\|\bm{C}_{t,m,j}\right\|>\frac{dy^{2}}{MT_{t}}\right]dy

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{pR^{2}}{MT_{t}}\cdot 2p\cdot\exp\left(-\frac{R^{2}}{2}\right)+\frac{2p}{MT_{t}}\int_{R}^{\infty}\bm{y}\cdot 2p\exp\left(-\frac{y^{2}}{2}\right)dy

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{pR^{2}}{MT_{t}}\cdot 2p\cdot\exp\left(-\frac{R^{2}}{2}\right)+\frac{2p}{MT_{t}}\cdot 2p\cdot\exp\left(-\frac{R^{2}}{2}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{p}{MT_{t}}\cdot 2p\cdot\left(R^{2}+2\right)\exp\left(-\frac{R^{2}}{2}\right).

Using the truncated matrix Bernstein inequality (Lemma
E.2
) with
n=MT_{t}
,
R=\sqrt{2\log\left(\frac{2pMT_{t}}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)}
,
n\Pr[\|\bm{C}_{t,m,j}\|\geq\frac{1}{MT_{t}}\cdot pR^{2}]\leq\delta^{\prime}
,
U=\frac{p\cdot 2\log\left(\frac{2pMT_{t}}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)}{MT_{t}}
,
\sigma^{2}=\frac{32p}{MT_{t}}
,
\tau=\frac{4\cdot p\cdot 2\log\left(\frac{2pMT_{t}}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)\log\left(\frac{2p}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)}{\sqrt{MT_{t}}}+\frac{4\cdot p\cdot 2\log\left(\frac{2pMT_{t}}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)\log\left(\frac{2p}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)}{MT_{t}}
and
\Delta=\frac{p\cdot 2\cdot 2\log\left(\frac{2pMT_{t}}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)}{MT_{t}}\cdot\frac{\delta^{\prime}}{MT_{t}}
, we have that with probability at least
1-2\delta^{\prime}
,

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{C}_{t}-\mathbb{E}\left[\bm{C}_{t}\right]\right\|\leq

\displaystyle\frac{4\cdot 2p\log\left(\frac{2pMT_{t}}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)\log\left(\frac{2p}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)}{\sqrt{MT_{t}}}+\frac{4\cdot 2p\log\left(\frac{2pMT_{t}}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)\log\left(\frac{2p}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)}{MT_{t}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{8\cdot 2p\log\left(\frac{2pMT_{t}}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)\log\left(\frac{2p}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)}{\sqrt{MT_{t}}}

(7)

Plugging Eqs. (
6
) and (
7
) into Eq. (
4
), we have that with probability at least
1-4\delta^{\prime}
,

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{Z}_{t}-\mathbb{E}[\bm{Z}_{t}]\right\|\leq

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{X}_{\textup{batch}}^{+}\right\|^{2}\left(\left\|\bm{A}_{t}-\mathbb{E}\left[\bm{A}_{t}\right]\right\|+\left\|\bm{C}_{t}-\mathbb{E}\left[\bm{C}_{t}\right]\right\|\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{X}_{\textup{batch}}^{+}\right\|^{2}\left(\frac{8\cdot 2pL_{x}L_{\theta}\sqrt{2\log\left(\frac{2pMT_{t}}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)}\log\left(\frac{2p}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)}{\sqrt{MT_{t}}}+\frac{8\cdot 2p\log\left(\frac{2pMT_{t}}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)\log\left(\frac{2p}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)}{\sqrt{MT_{t}}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{96\left\|\bm{X}_{\textup{batch}}^{+}\right\|^{2}pL_{x}L_{\theta}\log\left(\frac{2p}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)}{\sqrt{MT_{t}}}\log\left(\frac{2pMT_{t}}{\delta^{\prime}}\right).

Let
\delta^{\prime}=\frac{\delta_{t}}{8}
. Then, we obtain that with probability at least
1-\frac{\delta_{t}}{2}
,

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{Z}_{t}-\mathbb{E}[\bm{Z}_{t}]\right\|\leq

\displaystyle\frac{96\left\|\bm{X}_{\textup{batch}}^{+}\right\|^{2}pL_{x}L_{\theta}\log\left(\frac{16p}{\delta_{t}}\right)}{\sqrt{MT_{t}}}\log\left(\frac{16pMT_{t}}{\delta_{t}}\right),

which implies that

\Pr\left[\mathcal{E}_{t}\right]\geq 1-\frac{\delta_{t}}{2}
.

Taking a union bound over all phases
t\geq 1
and recalling
\delta_{t}:=\frac{\delta}{2t^{2}}
, we obtain

\displaystyle\Pr\left[\mathcal{E}\right]\geq

\displaystyle 1-\sum_{t=1}^{\infty}\Pr\left[\bar{\mathcal{E}_{t}}\right]

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle 1-\sum_{t=1}^{\infty}\frac{\delta_{t}}{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 1-\sum_{t=1}^{\infty}\frac{\delta}{4t^{2}}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle 1-\frac{\delta}{2}.

∎

For any matrix
\bm{A}\in\mathbb{R}^{m\times n}
with
m\geq n
, let
\sigma_{\max}(\bm{A})
and
\sigma_{\min}(\bm{A})
denote the maximum and minimum singular values of
\bm{A}
, respectively. For any
i\in[m]
, let
\sigma_{i}(\bm{A})
denote the
i
-th singular value of
\bm{A}
.

For any matrix
\bm{A}\in\mathbb{R}^{m\times n}
with
m\geq n
, let
\bm{A}_{\bot}
denote the orthogonal complement matrix of
\bm{A}
, where the columns of
\bm{A}_{\bot}
are the orthogonal complement of those of
\bm{A}
. Then, it holds that
\bm{A}\bm{A}^{\top}+\bm{A}_{\bot}\bm{A}_{\bot}^{\top}=\bm{I}_{m}
, where
\bm{I}_{m}
is the
m\times m
identity matrix.

According to Assumption
3.1
, there exists an absolute constant
c_{0}
which satisfies that
\sigma_{\min}(\frac{1}{M}\sum_{m=1}^{M}\bm{w}_{m}\bm{w}_{m}^{\top})=\sigma_{\min}(\frac{1}{M}\sum_{m=1}^{M}\bm{\theta}_{m}\bm{\theta}_{m}^{\top})\geq\frac{c_{0}}{k}
.

Lemma C.4

(Concentration of
\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}
)
.

Suppose that event
\mathcal{E}
holds. Then, for any phase
t>0
,

\displaystyle\left\|\hat{\bm{B}}_{t,\bot}^{\top}\bm{B}\right\|\leq\frac{192\left\|\bm{X}_{\textup{batch}}^{+}\right\|^{2}kpL_{x}L_{\theta}\log\left(\frac{16p}{\delta_{t}}\right)}{\sqrt{MT_{t}}}\log\left(\frac{16pMT_{t}}{\delta_{t}}\right).

Furthermore, for any phase
t>0
, if

\displaystyle T_{t}=\Bigg\lceil

\displaystyle\frac{68\cdot 192^{2}\cdot 8^{2}\left(1+\zeta\right)^{3}(\rho^{E})^{2}k^{4}L_{x}^{4}L_{\theta}^{2}L_{w}^{2}}{c_{0}^{2}M}\cdot\max\left\{2^{2t},\ \frac{L_{x}^{4}}{\omega^{2}}\right\}\cdot\log^{2}\left(\frac{16p}{\delta_{t}}\right)

\displaystyle\log^{2}\left(\frac{192\cdot 16\cdot 8\left(1+\zeta\right)^{\frac{3}{2}}\rho^{E}k^{2}pL_{x}^{2}L_{\theta}L_{w}}{c_{0}}\cdot\max\left\{2^{t},\ \frac{L_{x}^{2}}{\omega}\right\}\cdot\frac{1}{\delta_{t}}\cdot\log\left(\frac{16p}{\delta_{t}}\right)\right)\Bigg\rceil,

(8)

then

\displaystyle\left\|\hat{\bm{B}}_{t,\bot}^{\top}\bm{B}\right\|\leq\min\left\{\frac{1}{8kL_{x}L_{w}\cdot 2^{t}\sqrt{1+\zeta}},\ \frac{\omega}{6L_{x}^{2}}\right\}.

Proof of Lemma
C.4
.

From Assumption
3.1
,
\sigma_{k}(\mathbb{E}[\bm{Z}_{t}])-\sigma_{k+1}(\mathbb{E}[\bm{Z}_{t}])=\sigma_{\min}(\frac{1}{M}\sum_{m=1}^{M}\bm{\theta}_{m}\bm{\theta}_{m}^{\top})\geq\frac{c_{0}}{k}
. Using the Davis-Kahan sin
\theta
Theorem
(
Bhatia 2013
)
and letting
T_{t}
be large enough to satisfy
\left\|\bm{Z}_{t}-\mathbb{E}[\bm{Z}_{t}]\right\|\leq\frac{c_{0}}{2k}
, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\hat{\bm{B}}_{t,\bot}^{\top}\bm{B}\right\|\leq

\displaystyle\frac{\left\|\bm{Z}_{t}-\mathbb{E}[\bm{Z}_{t}]\right\|}{\sigma_{k}(\mathbb{E}[\bm{Z}_{t}])-\sigma_{k+1}(\mathbb{E}[\bm{Z}_{t}])-\left\|\bm{Z}_{t}-\mathbb{E}[\bm{Z}_{t}]\right\|}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{2k}{c_{0}}\left\|\bm{Z}_{t}-\mathbb{E}[\bm{Z}_{t}]\right\|

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{(a)}}{\leq}

\displaystyle\frac{192\left\|\bm{X}_{\textup{batch}}^{+}\right\|^{2}kpL_{x}L_{\theta}\log\left(\frac{16p}{\delta_{t}}\right)}{c_{0}\sqrt{MT_{t}}}\log\left(\frac{16pMT_{t}}{\delta_{t}}\right),.

where inequality (a) uses the definition of event
\mathcal{E}
.

Using Lemma
E.3
with
A=\frac{192\left\|\bm{X}_{\textup{batch}}^{+}\right\|^{2}kpL_{x}L_{\theta}}{c_{0}}\log\left(\frac{16p}{\delta_{t}}\right)
,
B=\frac{16p}{\delta_{t}}
and
\kappa=\min\{\frac{1}{8kL_{x}L_{w}\cdot 2^{t}\sqrt{1+\zeta}},\frac{\omega}{6L_{x}^{2}}\}
, we have that if

\displaystyle MT_{t}\geq

\displaystyle 68\left(\frac{192\left\|\bm{X}_{\textup{batch}}^{+}\right\|^{2}kpL_{x}L_{\theta}}{c_{0}}\log\left(\frac{16p}{\delta_{t}}\right)\right)^{2}\cdot\max\left\{\left(8kL_{x}L_{w}\cdot 2^{t}\sqrt{1+\zeta}\right)^{2},\ \frac{6^{2}L_{x}^{4}}{\omega^{2}}\right\}\cdot

\displaystyle\log^{2}\left(\frac{192\left\|\bm{X}_{\textup{batch}}^{+}\right\|^{2}kpL_{x}L_{\theta}}{c_{0}}\log\left(\frac{16p}{\delta_{t}}\right)\cdot\frac{16p}{\delta_{t}}\cdot\max\left\{8kL_{x}L_{w}\cdot 2^{t}\sqrt{1+\zeta},\ \frac{6L_{x}^{2}}{\omega}\right\}\right),

then
\left\|\hat{\bm{B}}_{t,\bot}^{\top}\bm{B}\right\|\leq\min\left\{\frac{1}{8kL_{x}L_{w}\cdot 2^{t}\sqrt{1+\zeta}},\ \frac{\omega}{6L_{x}^{2}}\right\}
.

According to Lemma
C.1
, we have
\|\bm{X}_{\textup{batch}}^{+}\|\leq\sqrt{\frac{(1+\zeta)\rho^{E}}{p}}
.

Then, further enlarging
MT_{t}
, we have that if

\displaystyle MT_{t}\geq

\displaystyle\frac{68\cdot 192^{2}\cdot 8^{2}\left(1+\zeta\right)^{3}(\rho^{E})^{2}k^{4}L_{x}^{4}L_{\theta}^{2}L_{w}^{2}}{c_{0}^{2}}\cdot\max\left\{2^{2t},\ \frac{L_{x}^{4}}{\omega^{2}}\right\}\cdot\log^{2}\left(\frac{16p}{\delta_{t}}\right)

\displaystyle\log^{2}\left(\frac{192\cdot 16\cdot 8\left(1+\zeta\right)^{\frac{3}{2}}\rho^{E}k^{2}pL_{x}^{2}L_{\theta}L_{w}}{c_{0}}\cdot\max\left\{2^{t},\ \frac{L_{x}^{2}}{\omega}\right\}\cdot\frac{1}{\delta_{t}}\cdot\log\left(\frac{16p}{\delta_{t}}\right)\right),

then

\displaystyle\left\|\hat{\bm{B}}_{t,\bot}^{\top}\bm{B}\right\|\leq\min\left\{\frac{1}{8kL_{x}L_{w}\cdot 2^{t}\sqrt{1+\zeta}},\ \frac{\omega}{6L_{x}^{2}}\right\}.

∎

C.3
Elimination with Low-dimensional Representations

For clarity of notation, we also add subscript
t
to the notations in subroutine
\mathtt{EliLowRep}
to denote the quantities generated in phase
t
. Specifically, we use the notations
\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}
,
\hat{\mathcal{X}}_{t,m}
,
\bm{\lambda}^{G}_{t,m}
,
\rho^{G}_{t,m}
,
N_{t,m}
,
\{\bm{z}_{t,m,i}\}_{i\in[N_{t,m}]}
,
\{r_{t,m,i}\}_{i\in[N_{t,m}]}
,
\hat{\bm{w}}_{t,m}
and
\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{t,m}
to denote the corresponding quantities used in
\mathtt{EliLowRep}
in phase
t
.

Before analyzing the sample complexity of
\mathtt{EliLowRep}
, we first prove that there exists a sample allocation
\bm{\lambda}\in\triangle_{\mathcal{X}}
such that
\sum_{i=1}^{n}\lambda(\bm{x}_{i})\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{x}_{i}\bm{x}_{i}^{\top}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}
is invertible, i.e., the G-optimal design optimization with
\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}
is non-vacuous (Line
2
in Algorithm
3
).

For any task
m\in[M]
, let

\displaystyle\bm{\lambda}^{*}_{m}:=

\displaystyle\operatornamewithlimits{argmin}_{\bm{\lambda}\in\triangle_{\mathcal{X}}}\max_{\bm{x}\in\mathcal{X}\setminus\{\bm{x}^{*}_{m}\}}\frac{\|\bm{B}^{\top}\bm{x}^{*}_{m}-\bm{B}^{\top}\bm{x}\|^{2}_{\left(\sum_{i=1}^{n}\lambda(\bm{x}_{i})\bm{B}^{\top}\bm{x}_{i}\bm{x}_{i}^{\top}\bm{B}\right)^{-1}}}{\left(({\bm{x}^{*}_{m}}-\bm{x})^{\top}\bm{\theta}_{m}\right)^{2}}.

\bm{\lambda}^{*}_{m}
is the optimal solution of the G-optimal design optimization with true feature extractor
\bm{B}
.

Lemma C.5
.

For any phase
t>0
and task
m\in[M]
, if
\|\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B}_{\bot}\|\leq\frac{\omega}{6L_{x}^{2}}
, we have

\displaystyle\sigma_{\min}\left(\sum_{i=1}^{n}\lambda^{*}_{m}(\bm{x}_{i})\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{x}_{i}\bm{x}_{i}^{\top}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}\right)>0.

Proof of Lemma
C.5
.

For any task
m\in[M]
, let
\bm{A}_{m}:=\sum_{i=1}^{n}\lambda^{*}_{m}(\bm{x}_{i})\bm{x}_{i}\bm{x}_{i}^{\top}
.
Then, for any phase
t>0
and task
m\in[M]
, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{n}\lambda^{*}_{m}(\bm{x}_{i})\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{x}_{i}\bm{x}_{i}^{\top}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}=

\displaystyle\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{A}_{m}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\left(\bm{B}\bm{B}^{\top}+\bm{B}_{\bot}\bm{B}_{\bot}^{\top}\right)\bm{A}_{m}\left(\bm{B}\bm{B}^{\top}+\bm{B}_{\bot}\bm{B}_{\bot}^{\top}\right)\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B}\bm{B}^{\top}\bm{A}_{m}\bm{B}\bm{B}^{\top}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}+\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B}\bm{B}^{\top}\bm{A}_{m}\bm{B}_{\bot}\bm{B}_{\bot}^{\top}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}

\displaystyle+\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B}_{\bot}\bm{B}_{\bot}^{\top}\bm{A}_{m}\bm{B}\bm{B}^{\top}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}+\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B}_{\bot}\bm{B}_{\bot}^{\top}\bm{A}_{m}\bm{B}_{\bot}\bm{B}_{\bot}^{\top}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}.

Hence, we have

\displaystyle\sigma_{\min}\left(\sum_{i=1}^{n}\lambda^{*}_{m}(\bm{x}_{i})\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{x}_{i}\bm{x}_{i}^{\top}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}\right)\geq

\displaystyle\sigma_{\min}\left(\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B}\bm{B}^{\top}\bm{A}_{m}\bm{B}\bm{B}^{\top}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}\right)-\sigma_{\max}\left(\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B}\bm{B}^{\top}\bm{A}_{m}\bm{B}_{\bot}\bm{B}_{\bot}^{\top}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}\right)

\displaystyle-\sigma_{\max}\left(\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B}_{\bot}\bm{B}_{\bot}^{\top}\bm{A}_{m}\bm{B}\bm{B}^{\top}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}\right)-\sigma_{\max}\left(\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B}_{\bot}\bm{B}_{\bot}^{\top}\bm{A}_{m}\bm{B}_{\bot}\bm{B}_{\bot}^{\top}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}\right)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\sigma_{\min}\left(\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B}\right)\sigma_{\min}\left(\bm{B}^{\top}\bm{A}_{m}\bm{B}\right)\sigma_{\min}\left(\bm{B}^{\top}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}\right)-\left\|\bm{B}_{\bot}^{\top}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}\right\|\left\|\bm{A}_{m}\right\|

\displaystyle-\left\|\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B}_{\bot}\right\|\left\|\bm{A}_{m}\right\|-\left\|\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B}_{\bot}\right\|\left\|\bm{A}_{m}\right\|

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\sigma^{2}_{\min}\left(\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B}\right)\sigma_{\min}\left(\bm{B}^{\top}\bm{A}_{m}\bm{B}\right)-3\left\|\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B}_{\bot}\right\|L_{x}^{2}

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{(a)}}{\geq}

\displaystyle\left(1-\left\|\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B}_{\bot}\right\|^{2}\right)\omega-3\left\|\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B}_{\bot}\right\|L_{x}^{2},

where inequality (a) uses the fact that
\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B}\bm{B}^{\top}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}+\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B}_{\bot}\bm{B}_{\bot}^{\top}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}=\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}(B\bm{B}^{\top}+\bm{B}_{\bot}\bm{B}_{\bot}^{\top})\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}=\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}=\bm{I}_{k}
, and thus,
\sigma^{2}_{\min}(\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B})=1-\|\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B}_{\bot}\|^{2}
.

Let
\|\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B}_{\bot}\|\leq\frac{\omega}{6L_{x}^{2}}
. Then, we have

\displaystyle\sigma_{\min}\left(\sum_{i=1}^{n}\lambda^{*}_{m}(\bm{x}_{i})\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{x}_{i}\bm{x}_{i}^{\top}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}\right)\geq

\displaystyle\left(1-\frac{\omega^{2}}{36L_{x}^{4}}\right)\omega-\frac{\omega}{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\omega}{2}-\frac{\omega^{3}}{36L_{x}^{4}}

\displaystyle>

\displaystyle 0,

where the last inequality is due to
\omega\leq L_{x}^{2}<\sqrt{18}L_{x}^{2}
.
∎

Next, we bound the optimal value
\rho^{G}_{t,m}
of the G-optimal design optimization with the estimated feature extractor
\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}
.

For any
\mathcal{Z}\subseteq\mathcal{X}
, let
\mathcal{Y}(\mathcal{Z}):=\{\bm{x}-\bm{x}^{\prime}:\ \forall\bm{x},\bm{x}^{\prime}\in\mathcal{Z},\ \bm{x}\neq\bm{x}^{\prime}\}
.
Recall that in Line
2
of Algorithm
3
, for any phase
t>0
and task
m\in[M]
,

\rho^{G}_{t,m}:=\min_{\bm{\lambda}\in\triangle_{\mathcal{X}}}\max_{\bm{y}\in\mathcal{Y}(\hat{\mathcal{X}}_{t,m})}\|\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{y}\|^{2}_{\left(\sum_{i=1}^{n}\lambda(\bm{x}_{i})\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{x}_{i}\bm{x}_{i}^{\top}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}\right)^{-1}}.

Lemma C.6
.

For any phase
t>0
and task
m\in[M]
,

\displaystyle\rho^{G}_{t,m}\leq 4k.

Proof of Lemma
C.6
.

For any phase
t>0
and task
m\in[M]
, we have that
\hat{\mathcal{X}}_{t,m}\subseteq\mathcal{X}
and
\mathcal{Y}(\hat{\mathcal{X}}_{t,m})\subseteq\mathcal{Y}(\mathcal{X})
.

For any fixed
\bm{\lambda}\in\triangle_{\mathcal{X}}
,

\displaystyle\max_{\bm{y}\in\mathcal{Y}(\hat{\mathcal{X}}_{t,m})}\|\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{y}\|^{2}_{\left(\sum_{i=1}^{n}\lambda(\bm{x}_{i})\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{x}_{i}\bm{x}_{i}^{\top}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}\right)^{-1}}\leq

\displaystyle\max_{\bm{y}\in\mathcal{Y}(\mathcal{X})}\|\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{y}\|^{2}_{\left(\sum_{i=1}^{n}\lambda(\bm{x}_{i})\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{x}_{i}\bm{x}_{i}^{\top}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}\right)^{-1}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\|\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}(\bm{x}^{\prime}_{1}-\bm{x}^{\prime}_{2})\|^{2}_{\left(\sum_{i=1}^{n}\lambda(\bm{x}_{i})\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{x}_{i}\bm{x}_{i}^{\top}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}\right)^{-1}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(\|\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{x}^{\prime}_{1}\|_{\left(\sum_{i=1}^{n}\lambda(\bm{x}_{i})\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{x}_{i}\bm{x}_{i}^{\top}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}\right)^{-1}}+\|\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{x}^{\prime}_{2}\|_{\left(\sum_{i=1}^{n}\lambda(\bm{x}_{i})\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{x}_{i}\bm{x}_{i}^{\top}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}\right)^{-1}}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\|\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{x}^{\prime}_{1}\|^{2}_{\left(\sum_{i=1}^{n}\lambda(\bm{x}_{i})\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{x}_{i}\bm{x}_{i}^{\top}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}\right)^{-1}}+2\|\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{x}^{\prime}_{2}\|^{2}_{\left(\sum_{i=1}^{n}\lambda(\bm{x}_{i})\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{x}_{i}\bm{x}_{i}^{\top}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}\right)^{-1}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 4\max_{\bm{x}\in\mathcal{X}}\|\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{x}\|^{2}_{\left(\sum_{i=1}^{n}\lambda(\bm{x}_{i})\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{x}_{i}\bm{x}_{i}^{\top}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}\right)^{-1}},

where
\bm{x}^{\prime}_{1}
and
\bm{x}^{\prime}_{2}
are the arms which satisfy that
\bm{y}=\bm{x}^{\prime}_{1}-\bm{x}^{\prime}_{2}
achieves the maximum value
\max_{\bm{y}\in\mathcal{Y}(\mathcal{X})}\|\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{y}\|^{2}_{\left(\sum_{i=1}^{n}\lambda(\bm{x}_{i})\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{x}_{i}\bm{x}_{i}^{\top}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}\right)^{-1}}
.

Since
\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{x}\in\mathbb{R}^{k}
, according to the Equivalence Theorem in
(
Kiefer & Wolfowitz 1960
)
, we have

\displaystyle\min_{\bm{\lambda}\in\triangle_{\mathcal{X}}}\max_{\bm{x}\in\mathcal{X}}\|\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{x}\|^{2}_{\left(\sum_{i=1}^{n}\lambda(\bm{x}_{i})\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{x}_{i}\bm{x}_{i}^{\top}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}\right)^{-1}}=k.

Therefore, we have

\displaystyle 4k=

\displaystyle 4\min_{\bm{\lambda}\in\triangle_{\mathcal{X}}}\max_{\bm{x}\in\mathcal{X}}\|\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{x}\|^{2}_{\left(\sum_{i=1}^{n}\lambda(\bm{x}_{i})\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{x}_{i}\bm{x}_{i}^{\top}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}\right)^{-1}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 4\max_{\bm{x}\in\mathcal{X}}\|\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{x}\|^{2}_{\left(\sum_{i=1}^{n}\lambda^{\prime}(\bm{x}_{i})\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{x}_{i}\bm{x}_{i}^{\top}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}\right)^{-1}}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\max_{\bm{y}\in\mathcal{Y}(\hat{\mathcal{X}}_{t,m})}\|\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{y}\|^{2}_{\left(\sum_{i=1}^{n}\lambda^{\prime}(\bm{x}_{i})\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{x}_{i}\bm{x}_{i}^{\top}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}\right)^{-1}}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\min_{\bm{\lambda}\in\triangle_{\mathcal{X}}}\max_{\bm{y}\in\mathcal{Y}(\hat{\mathcal{X}}_{t,m})}\|\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{y}\|^{2}_{\left(\sum_{i=1}^{n}\lambda(\bm{x}_{i})\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{x}_{i}\bm{x}_{i}^{\top}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}\right)^{-1}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\rho^{G}_{t,m},

where
\bm{\lambda}^{\prime}:=\operatornamewithlimits{argmin}_{\bm{\lambda}\in\triangle_{\mathcal{X}}}\max_{\bm{x}\in\mathcal{X}}\|\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{x}\|^{2}_{\left(\sum_{i=1}^{n}\lambda(\bm{x}_{i})\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{x}_{i}\bm{x}_{i}^{\top}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}\right)^{-1}}
.
∎

Now we analyze the estimation error of the estimated reward parameter
\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{t,m}=\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}\hat{\bm{w}}_{t,m}
in
\mathtt{EliLowRep}
.

For any phase
t>0
, task
m\in[M]
and arm
j\in[N_{t,m}]
, let
\xi_{t,m,j}
denote the noise of the sample on arm
\bm{z}_{t,m,j}
for task
m
, during the execution of
\mathtt{EliLowRep}
in phase
t
(Line
5
in Algorithm
3
).

For any phase
t>0
, define events

\displaystyle\mathcal{F}_{t}:=\Bigg\{

\displaystyle\bm{y}^{\top}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}\left(\sum_{j=1}^{N_{t,m}}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{z}_{t,m,j}{\bm{z}_{t,m,j}}^{\top}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}\right)^{-1}\sum_{j=1}^{N_{t,m}}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{z}_{t,m,j}\cdot\xi_{t,m,j}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{y}\right\|_{\left(\sum_{j=1}^{N_{t,m}}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{z}_{t,m,j}{\bm{z}_{t,m,j}}^{\top}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}\right)^{-1}}\sqrt{2\log\left(\frac{4n^{2}M}{\delta_{t}}\right)},\ \forall m\in[M],\ \forall\bm{y}\in\mathcal{Y}(\hat{\mathcal{X}}_{t,m})\Bigg\},

(9)

and

\displaystyle\mathcal{F}:=\cap_{t=1}^{\infty}\mathcal{F}_{t}.

Lemma C.7

(Concentration of the Variance Term)
.

It holds that

\displaystyle\Pr\left[\mathcal{F}\right]\geq 1-\frac{\delta}{2}.

Proof of Lemma
C.7
.

Let
\bm{\Sigma}_{t,m}:=\sum_{j=1}^{N_{t,m}}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{z}_{t,m,j}{\bm{z}_{t,m,j}}^{\top}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}
.
Then, we can write

\displaystyle\bm{y}^{\top}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}\left(\sum_{j=1}^{N_{t,m}}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{z}_{t,m,j}{\bm{z}_{t,m,j}}^{\top}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}\right)^{-1}\sum_{j=1}^{N_{t,m}}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{z}_{t,m,j}\cdot\xi_{t,m,j}=\sum_{j=1}^{N_{t,m}}\bm{y}^{\top}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}\bm{\Sigma}_{t,m}^{-1}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{z}_{t,m,j}\cdot\xi_{t,m,j}.

For any phase
t>0
, task
m\in[M]
and arm
j\in[N_{t,m}]
,
\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}
,
\bm{\Sigma}_{t,m}
and
\{\bm{z}_{t,m,j}\}_{j=1}^{N_{t,m}}
are fixed before the sampling in
\mathtt{EliLowRep}
, and the noise
\xi_{t,m,j}
is 1-sub-Gaussian (Line
5
in Algorithm
3
).
Thus, we have that for any
t>0
,
m\in[M]
and
j\in[N_{t,m}]
,
\bm{y}^{\top}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}\bm{\Sigma}_{t,m}^{-1}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{z}_{t,m,j}\cdot\xi_{t,m,j}
is
(\bm{y}^{\top}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}\bm{\Sigma}_{t,m}^{-1}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{z}_{t,m,j})
-sub-Gaussian.

Using Hoeffding’s inequality and taking a union bound over all
m\in[M]
and
\bm{y}\in\mathcal{Y}(\hat{\mathcal{X}}_{t,m})
, we have that with probability at least
1-\frac{\delta_{t}}{2}
,

\displaystyle\sum_{j=1}^{N_{t,m}}\bm{y}^{\top}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}\bm{\Sigma}_{t,m}^{-1}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{z}_{t,m,j}\cdot\xi_{t,m,j}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sqrt{2\sum_{j=1}^{N_{t,m}}\left(\bm{y}^{\top}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}\bm{\Sigma}_{t,m}^{-1}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{z}_{t,m,j}\right)^{2}\cdot\log\left(\frac{4n^{2}M}{\delta_{t}}\right)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sqrt{2\sum_{j=1}^{N_{t,m}}\bm{y}^{\top}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}\bm{\Sigma}_{t,m}^{-1}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{z}_{t,m,j}\cdot{\bm{z}_{t,m,j}}^{\top}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}\bm{\Sigma}_{t,m}^{-1}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{y}\cdot\log\left(\frac{4n^{2}M}{\delta_{t}}\right)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sqrt{2\bm{y}^{\top}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}\bm{\Sigma}_{t,m}^{-1}\left(\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\sum_{j=1}^{N_{t,m}}\bm{z}_{t,m,j}\cdot{\bm{z}_{t,m,j}}^{\top}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}\right)\bm{\Sigma}_{t,m}^{-1}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{y}\cdot\log\left(\frac{4n^{2}M}{\delta_{t}}\right)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sqrt{2\bm{y}^{\top}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}\bm{\Sigma}_{t,m}^{-1}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{y}\cdot\log\left(\frac{4n^{2}M}{\delta_{t}}\right)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\|\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{y}\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{t,m}^{-1}}\sqrt{2\log\left(\frac{4n^{2}M}{\delta_{t}}\right)},

which implies that

\displaystyle\Pr\left[\mathcal{F}_{t}\right]\geq 1-\frac{\delta_{t}}{2}.

Taking a union bound over all phases
t\geq 1
and recalling
\delta_{t}:=\frac{\delta}{2t^{2}}
, we obtain

\displaystyle\Pr\left[\mathcal{F}\right]\geq

\displaystyle 1-\sum_{t=1}^{\infty}\Pr\left[\bar{\mathcal{F}_{t}}\right]

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle 1-\sum_{t=1}^{\infty}\frac{\delta_{t}}{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 1-\sum_{t=1}^{\infty}\frac{\delta}{4t^{2}}

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle 1-\frac{\delta}{2}.

∎

Lemma C.8

(Concentration of
\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{t,m}
)
.

Suppose that event
\mathcal{E}\cap\mathcal{F}
holds. Then, for any phase
t>0
, task
m\in[M]
and
\bm{y}\in\mathcal{Y}(\hat{\mathcal{X}}_{t,m})
,

\displaystyle\left|\bm{y}^{\top}\left(\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{t,m}-\bm{\theta}_{m}\right)\right|\leq\frac{1}{2^{t}}.

Proof of Lemma
C.8
.

For any phase
t>0
, task
m\in[M]
and
\bm{y}\in\mathcal{Y}(\hat{\mathcal{X}}_{t,m})
,

\displaystyle\bm{y}^{\top}\left(\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{t,m}-\bm{\theta}_{m}\right)=

\displaystyle\bm{y}^{\top}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}\hat{\bm{w}}_{t,m}-\bm{y}^{\top}\left(\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}+\hat{\bm{B}}_{t,\bot}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t,\bot}^{\top}\right)\bm{\theta}_{m}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\bm{y}^{\top}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}\left(\hat{\bm{w}}_{t,m}-\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{\theta}_{m}\right)-\bm{y}^{\top}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t,\bot}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t,\bot}^{\top}\bm{\theta}_{m}.

(10)

Here,
\hat{\bm{w}}_{t,m}
can be written as

\displaystyle\hat{\bm{w}}_{t,m}=

\displaystyle\left(\sum_{j=1}^{N_{t,m}}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{z}_{t,m,j}{\bm{z}_{t,m,j}}^{\top}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}\right)^{-1}\sum_{j=1}^{N_{t,m}}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{z}_{t,m,j}\cdot r_{t,m,j}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(\sum_{j=1}^{N_{t,m}}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{z}_{t,m,j}{\bm{z}_{t,m,j}}^{\top}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}\right)^{-1}\sum_{j=1}^{N_{t,m}}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{z}_{t,m,j}\cdot\left({\bm{z}_{t,m,j}}^{\top}\bm{\theta}_{m}+\xi_{t,m,j}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(\sum_{j=1}^{N_{t,m}}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{z}_{t,m,j}{\bm{z}_{t,m,j}}^{\top}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}\right)^{-1}\sum_{j=1}^{N_{t,m}}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{z}_{t,m,j}\cdot\left({\bm{z}_{t,m,j}}^{\top}\left(\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}+\hat{\bm{B}}_{t,\bot}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t,\bot}^{\top}\right)\bm{\theta}_{m}+\xi_{t,m,j}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{\theta}_{m}+\left(\sum_{j=1}^{N_{t,m}}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{z}_{t,m,j}{\bm{z}_{t,m,j}}^{\top}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}\right)^{-1}\sum_{j=1}^{N_{t,m}}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{z}_{t,m,j}\cdot{\bm{z}_{t,m,j}}^{\top}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t,\bot}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t,\bot}^{\top}\bm{\theta}_{m}

\displaystyle+\left(\sum_{j=1}^{N_{t,m}}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{z}_{t,m,j}{\bm{z}_{t,m,j}}^{\top}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}\right)^{-1}\sum_{j=1}^{N_{t,m}}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{z}_{t,m,j}\cdot\xi_{t,m,j}.

(11)

Plugging Eq. (
11
) into Eq. (
10
), we can decompose the estimation error of
\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{t,m}
in
\mathtt{EliLowRep}
into three parts as

\displaystyle\bm{y}^{\top}\left(\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{t,m}-\bm{\theta}_{m}\right)=

\displaystyle\underbrace{\bm{y}^{\top}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}\left(\sum_{j=1}^{N_{t,m}}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{z}_{t,m,j}{\bm{z}_{t,m,j}}^{\top}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}\right)^{-1}\sum_{j=1}^{N_{t,m}}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{z}_{t,m,j}\cdot{\bm{z}_{t,m,j}}^{\top}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t,\bot}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t,\bot}^{\top}\bm{B}\bm{w}_{m}}_{\textup{Bias}}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\bm{y}^{\top}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}\left(\sum_{j=1}^{N_{t,m}}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{z}_{t,m,j}{\bm{z}_{t,m,j}}^{\top}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}\right)^{-1}\sum_{j=1}^{N_{t,m}}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{z}_{t,m,j}\cdot\xi_{t,m,j}}_{\textup{Variance}}-\underbrace{\bm{y}^{\top}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t,\bot}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t,\bot}^{\top}\bm{B}\bm{w}_{m}}_{\textup{Estimation error of $\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}$}}.

Taking the absolute value on both sides, and using the Cauchy–Schwarz inequality and definition of event
\mathcal{F}
(Eq. (
9
)), we have

\displaystyle\left|\bm{y}^{\top}\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{t,m}-\bm{y}^{\top}\bm{\theta}_{m}\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left\|\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{y}\right\|_{\left(\sum_{j=1}^{N_{t,m}}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{z}_{t,m,j}{\bm{z}_{t,m,j}}^{\top}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}\right)^{-1}}\cdot\left\|\sum_{j=1}^{N_{t,m}}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{z}_{t,m,j}\cdot{\bm{z}_{t,m,j}}^{\top}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t,\bot}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t,\bot}^{\top}\bm{B}\bm{w}_{m}\right\|_{\left(\sum_{j=1}^{N_{t,m}}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{z}_{t,m,j}{\bm{z}_{t,m,j}}^{\top}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}\right)^{-1}}

\displaystyle+\left\|\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{y}\right\|_{\left(\sum_{j=1}^{N_{t,m}}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{z}_{t,m,j}{\bm{z}_{t,m,j}}^{\top}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}\right)^{-1}}\sqrt{2\log\left(\frac{4n^{2}M}{\delta_{t}}\right)}+\left|\bm{y}^{\top}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t,\bot}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t,\bot}^{\top}\bm{B}\bm{w}_{m}\right|

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{(a)}}{\leq}

\displaystyle\frac{\sqrt{1+\zeta}\left\|\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{y}\right\|_{\left(\sum_{i=1}^{n}\lambda^{G}_{t,m}(\bm{x}_{i})\cdot\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{x}_{i}\bm{x}_{i}^{\top}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}\right)^{-1}}}{\sqrt{N_{t,m}}}\cdot L_{x}L_{w}\left\|\hat{\bm{B}}_{t,\bot}^{\top}\bm{B}\right\|\cdot\sum_{j=1}^{N_{t,m}}\left\|\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{z}_{t,m,j}\right\|_{\left(\sum_{j=1}^{N_{t,m}}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{z}_{t,m,j}{\bm{z}_{t,m,j}}^{\top}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}\right)^{-1}}

\displaystyle+\frac{\sqrt{1+\zeta}\left\|\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{y}\right\|_{\left(\sum_{i=1}^{n}\lambda^{G}_{t,m}(\bm{x}_{i})\cdot\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{x}_{i}\bm{x}_{i}^{\top}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}\right)^{-1}}}{\sqrt{N_{t,m}}}\cdot\sqrt{2\log\left(\frac{4n^{2}M}{\delta_{t}}\right)}+2L_{x}L_{w}\left\|\hat{\bm{B}}_{t,\bot}^{\top}\bm{B}\right\|

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{(b)}}{\leq}

\displaystyle\frac{\sqrt{1+\zeta}\left\|\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{y}\right\|_{\left(\sum_{i=1}^{n}\lambda^{G}_{t,m}(\bm{x}_{i})\cdot\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{x}_{i}\bm{x}_{i}^{\top}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}\right)^{-1}}}{\sqrt{N_{t,m}}}\cdot L_{x}L_{w}\left\|\hat{\bm{B}}_{t,\bot}^{\top}\bm{B}\right\|\cdot\sqrt{kN_{t,m}}

\displaystyle+\frac{\sqrt{1+\zeta}\left\|\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{y}\right\|_{\left(\sum_{i=1}^{n}\lambda^{G}_{t,m}(\bm{x}_{i})\cdot\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{x}_{i}\bm{x}_{i}^{\top}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}\right)^{-1}}}{\sqrt{N_{t,m}}}\cdot\sqrt{2\log\left(\frac{4n^{2}M}{\delta_{t}}\right)}+2L_{x}L_{w}\left\|\hat{\bm{B}}_{t,\bot}^{\top}\bm{B}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sqrt{1+\zeta}\left\|\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{y}\right\|_{\left(\sum_{i=1}^{n}\lambda^{G}_{t,m}(\bm{x}_{i})\cdot\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{x}_{i}\bm{x}_{i}^{\top}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}\right)^{-1}}\cdot L_{x}L_{w}\left\|\hat{\bm{B}}_{t,\bot}^{\top}\bm{B}\right\|\cdot\sqrt{k}

\displaystyle+\frac{\sqrt{1+\zeta}\left\|\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{y}\right\|_{\left(\sum_{i=1}^{n}\lambda^{G}_{t,m}(\bm{x}_{i})\cdot\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{x}_{i}\bm{x}_{i}^{\top}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}\right)^{-1}}}{\sqrt{N_{t,m}}}\cdot\sqrt{2\log\left(\frac{4n^{2}M}{\delta_{t}}\right)}+2L_{x}L_{w}\left\|\hat{\bm{B}}_{t,\bot}^{\top}\bm{B}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sqrt{(1+\zeta)\cdot k\cdot\rho^{G}_{t,m}}\cdot L_{x}L_{w}\left\|\hat{\bm{B}}_{t,\bot}^{\top}\bm{B}\right\|+\frac{\sqrt{(1+\zeta)\cdot\rho^{G}_{t,m}\cdot 2\log\left(\frac{4n^{2}M}{\delta_{t}}\right)}}{\sqrt{N_{t,m}}}+2L_{x}L_{w}\left\|\hat{\bm{B}}_{t,\bot}^{\top}\bm{B}\right\|

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{(c)}}{\leq}

\displaystyle\sqrt{(1+\zeta)\cdot 4k^{2}}\cdot L_{x}L_{w}\left\|\hat{\bm{B}}_{t,\bot}^{\top}\bm{B}\right\|+\frac{\sqrt{(1+\zeta)\cdot\rho^{G}_{t,m}\cdot 2\log\left(\frac{4n^{2}M}{\delta_{t}}\right)}}{\sqrt{N_{t,m}}}+2L_{x}L_{w}\left\|\hat{\bm{B}}_{t,\bot}^{\top}\bm{B}\right\|

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{(d)}}{\leq}

\displaystyle\sqrt{(1+\zeta)\cdot 4k^{2}}\cdot L_{x}L_{w}\cdot\frac{1}{8kL_{x}L_{w}\cdot 2^{t}\sqrt{1+\zeta}}+\frac{1}{4\cdot 2^{t}}+2L_{x}L_{w}\cdot\frac{1}{8kL_{x}L_{w}\cdot 2^{t}\sqrt{1+\zeta}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{4\cdot 2^{t}}+\frac{1}{4\cdot 2^{t}}+\frac{1}{4\cdot 2^{t}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2^{t}}.

Here inequality (a) is due to the guarantee of rounding procedure
\mathtt{ROUND}
and the triangle inequality. Inequality (b) uses Lemma
E.5
, and inequality (c) follows from Lemma
C.6
. Inequality (d) comes from Lemma
C.4
and
N_{t,m}:=\max\{\lceil 32\cdot 2^{2t}(1+\zeta)\rho^{G}_{t,m}\log(\frac{4n^{2}M}{\delta_{t}})\rceil,\ \frac{180k}{\zeta^{2}}\}
.
∎

For any task
m\in[M]
and arm
\bm{x}\in\mathcal{X}
, let
\Delta_{m}(\bm{x}):=({\bm{x}^{*}_{m}}-\bm{x})^{\top}\bm{\theta}_{m}
denote the reward gap between the optimal arm
\bm{x}^{*}_{m}
and arm
\bm{x}
in task
m
.
For any phase
t>0
and task
m\in[M]
, let
\mathcal{Z}_{t,m}:=\{\bm{x}\in\mathcal{X}:\Delta_{m}(\bm{x})\leq 4\cdot 2^{-t}\}
.

Lemma C.9
.

Suppose that event
\mathcal{E}\cap\mathcal{F}
holds. For any phase
t>0
and task
m\in[M]
,

\displaystyle\bm{x}^{*}_{m}\in\hat{\mathcal{X}}_{t,m},

and for any phase
t\geq 2
and task
m\in[M]
,

\displaystyle\hat{\mathcal{X}}_{t,m}\subseteq\mathcal{Z}_{t,m}.

Proof of Lemma
C.9
.

This proof follows a similar analytical procedure as that of Lemma 2 in
(
Fiez et al. 2019
)
.

First, we prove
\bm{x}^{*}_{m}\in\hat{\mathcal{X}}_{t,m}
for any phase
t>0
and task
m\in[M]
by contradiction.

Suppose that for some
t>0
and some
m\in[M]
,
\bm{x}^{*}_{m}
is eliminated from
\hat{\mathcal{X}}_{t,m}
in phase
t
.
Then, we have that there exists some
\bm{x}^{\prime}\in\hat{\mathcal{X}}_{t,m}
such that

\displaystyle(\bm{x}^{\prime}-\bm{x}^{*}_{m})^{\top}\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{t,m}>2^{-t}.

Then, we have

\displaystyle(\bm{x}^{\prime}-\bm{x}^{*}_{m})^{\top}\bm{\theta}_{m}=

\displaystyle(\bm{x}^{\prime}-\bm{x}^{*}_{m})^{\top}\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{t,m}-(\bm{x}^{\prime}-\bm{x}^{*}_{m})^{\top}\left(\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{t,m}-\bm{\theta}_{m}\right)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle(\bm{x}^{\prime}-\bm{x}^{*}_{m})^{\top}\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{t,m}-2^{-t}

\displaystyle>

\displaystyle 2^{-t}-2^{-t}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 0,

which contradicts the definition of
\bm{x}^{*}_{m}
. Thus, we obtain that
\bm{x}^{*}_{m}\in\hat{\mathcal{X}}_{t,m}
for any phase
t>0
and task
m\in[M]
.

Next, we prove
\hat{\mathcal{X}}_{t,m}\subseteq\mathcal{Z}_{t,m}
for any phase
t\geq 2
and task
m\in[M]
, i.e., each
\bm{x}\in\hat{\mathcal{X}}_{t,m}
satisfies that
\Delta_{m}(\bm{x})\leq 4\cdot 2^{-t}
.

Suppose that there exists some phase
t
, some task
m
and some
\bm{x}\in\hat{\mathcal{X}}_{t,m}
such that
\Delta_{m}(\bm{x})>4\cdot 2^{-t}
. Then, in phase
t-1\geq 1
, we have

\displaystyle(\bm{x}^{*}_{m}-\bm{x})^{\top}\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{t-1,m}=

\displaystyle(\bm{x}^{*}_{m}-\bm{x})^{\top}\bm{\theta}_{m}-(\bm{x}^{*}_{m}-\bm{x})^{\top}\left(\bm{\theta}_{m}-\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{t-1,m}\right)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle(\bm{x}^{*}_{m}-\bm{x})^{\top}\bm{\theta}_{m}-2^{-(t-1)}

\displaystyle>

\displaystyle 4\cdot 2^{-t}-2^{-(t-1)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2^{-(t-1)},

which implies that
x
should have been eliminated from
\hat{\mathcal{X}}_{t,m}
in phase
t-1
, and contradicts our supposition. Thus, we complete the proof.
∎

C.4
Proof of Theorem
4.1

Before proving Theorem
4.1
, we first introduce a useful lemma.

For any task
m\in[M]
, let

\displaystyle\bm{\lambda}^{*}_{m}:=

\displaystyle\operatornamewithlimits{argmin}_{\bm{\lambda}\in\triangle_{\mathcal{X}}}\max_{\bm{x}\in\mathcal{X}\setminus\{\bm{x}^{*}_{m}\}}\frac{\|\bm{B}^{\top}\bm{x}^{*}_{m}-\bm{B}^{\top}\bm{x}\|^{2}_{\left(\sum_{i=1}^{n}\lambda(\bm{x}_{i})\bm{B}^{\top}\bm{x}_{i}\bm{x}_{i}^{\top}\bm{B}\right)^{-1}}}{\left(({\bm{x}^{*}_{m}}-\bm{x})^{\top}\bm{\theta}_{m}\right)^{2}},

and

\displaystyle\rho^{*}_{m}:=

\displaystyle\min_{\bm{\lambda}\in\triangle_{\mathcal{X}}}\max_{\bm{x}\in\mathcal{X}\setminus\{\bm{x}^{*}_{m}\}}\frac{\|\bm{B}^{\top}\bm{x}^{*}_{m}-\bm{B}^{\top}\bm{x}\|^{2}_{\left(\sum_{i=1}^{n}\lambda(\bm{x}_{i})\bm{B}^{\top}\bm{x}_{i}\bm{x}_{i}^{\top}\bm{B}\right)^{-1}}}{\left(({\bm{x}^{*}_{m}}-\bm{x})^{\top}\bm{\theta}_{m}\right)^{2}}.

\bm{\lambda}^{*}_{m}
and
\rho^{*}_{m}
are the optimal solution and the optimal value of the G-optimal design optimization with true feature extractor
\bm{B}
, respectively.

Lemma C.10
.

Suppose that event
\mathcal{E}\cap\mathcal{F}
holds. For any task
m\in[M]
and
\bm{y}\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
,

\displaystyle\|\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{y}\|^{2}_{\left(\sum_{i=1}^{n}\lambda^{*}_{m}(\bm{x}_{i})\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{x}_{i}\bm{x}_{i}^{\top}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}\right)^{-1}}\leq\|\bm{B}^{\top}\bm{y}\|^{2}_{\left(\sum_{i=1}^{n}\lambda^{*}_{m}(\bm{x}_{i})\bm{B}^{\top}\bm{x}_{i}\bm{x}_{i}^{\top}\bm{B}\right)^{-1}}+\frac{11L_{x}^{4}}{k\omega^{2}\cdot 2^{t}}.

Proof of Lemma
C.10
.

We first handle the term
(\sum_{i=1}^{n}\lambda^{*}_{m}(\bm{x}_{i})\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{x}_{i}\bm{x}_{i}^{\top}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t})^{-1}
.

For any task
m\in[M]
, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{n}\lambda^{*}_{m}(\bm{x}_{i})\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{x}_{i}\bm{x}_{i}^{\top}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{n}\lambda^{*}_{m}(\bm{x}_{i})\left(\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B}\bm{B}^{\top}\bm{x}_{i}+\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B}_{\bot}\bm{B}_{\bot}^{\top}\bm{x}_{i}\right)\cdot\left(\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B}\bm{B}^{\top}\bm{x}_{i}+\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B}_{\bot}\bm{B}_{\bot}^{\top}\bm{x}_{i}\right)^{\top}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{n}\lambda^{*}_{m}(\bm{x}_{i})\Bigg(\left(\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B}\bm{B}^{\top}\bm{x}_{i}\right)\cdot\left(\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B}\bm{B}^{\top}\bm{x}_{i}\right)^{\top}+\left(\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B}\bm{B}^{\top}\bm{x}_{i}\right)\cdot\left(\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B}_{\bot}\bm{B}_{\bot}^{\top}\bm{x}_{i}\right)^{\top}

\displaystyle+\left(\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B}_{\bot}\bm{B}_{\bot}^{\top}\bm{x}_{i}\right)\cdot\left(\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B}\bm{B}^{\top}\bm{x}_{i}\right)^{\top}+\left(\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B}_{\bot}\bm{B}_{\bot}^{\top}\bm{x}_{i}\right)\cdot\left(\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B}_{\bot}\bm{B}_{\bot}^{\top}\bm{x}_{i}\right)^{\top}\Bigg)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{n}\lambda^{*}_{m}(\bm{x}_{i})\left(\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B}\bm{B}^{\top}\bm{x}_{i}\right)\cdot\left(\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B}\bm{B}^{\top}\bm{x}_{i}\right)^{\top}+\sum_{i=1}^{n}\lambda^{*}_{m}(\bm{x}_{i})\Bigg(\left(\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B}\bm{B}^{\top}\bm{x}_{i}\right)\cdot\left(\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B}_{\bot}\bm{B}_{\bot}^{\top}\bm{x}_{i}\right)^{\top}

\displaystyle+\left(\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B}_{\bot}\bm{B}_{\bot}^{\top}\bm{x}_{i}\right)\cdot\left(\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B}\bm{B}^{\top}\bm{x}_{i}\right)^{\top}+\left(\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B}_{\bot}\bm{B}_{\bot}^{\top}\bm{x}_{i}\right)\cdot\left(\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B}_{\bot}\bm{B}_{\bot}^{\top}\bm{x}_{i}\right)^{\top}\Bigg).

Let
\bm{P}_{t}:=\sum_{i=1}^{n}\lambda^{*}_{m}(\bm{x}_{i})(\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B}\bm{B}^{\top}\bm{x}_{i})\cdot(\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B}\bm{B}^{\top}\bm{x}_{i})^{\top}
.
Let
\bm{Q}_{t}:=\sum_{i=1}^{n}\lambda^{*}_{m}(\bm{x}_{i})((\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B}\bm{B}^{\top}\bm{x}_{i})\cdot(\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B}_{\bot}\bm{B}_{\bot}^{\top}\bm{x}_{i})^{\top}+(\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B}_{\bot}\bm{B}_{\bot}^{\top}\bm{x}_{i})\cdot(\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B}\bm{B}^{\top}\bm{x}_{i})^{\top}+(\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B}_{\bot}\bm{B}_{\bot}^{\top}\bm{x}_{i})\cdot(\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B}_{\bot}\bm{B}_{\bot}^{\top}\bm{x}_{i})^{\top})
.
Then, we have
\sum_{i=1}^{n}\lambda^{*}_{m}(\bm{x}_{i})\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{x}_{i}\bm{x}_{i}^{\top}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}=\bm{P}_{t}+\bm{Q}_{t}
.

From Assumption
3.2
, we have that for any task
m\in[M]
,
\sum_{i=1}^{n}\lambda^{*}_{m}(\bm{x}_{i})\bm{B}^{\top}\bm{x}_{i}\bm{x}_{i}^{\top}\bm{B}
is invertible.
Since
\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B}
is also invertible, we have that
\bm{P}_{t}
is invertible.
According to Lemmas
C.4
and
C.5
, we have that
\sum_{i=1}^{n}\lambda^{*}_{m}(\bm{x}_{i})\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{x}_{i}\bm{x}_{i}^{\top}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}
is also invertible.
Thus, we can write
(\sum_{i=1}^{n}\lambda^{*}_{m}(\bm{x}_{i})\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{x}_{i}\bm{x}_{i}^{\top}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t})^{-1}
as follows.

\displaystyle\left(\sum_{i=1}^{n}\lambda^{*}_{m}(\bm{x}_{i})\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{x}_{i}\bm{x}_{i}^{\top}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}\right)^{-1}=

\displaystyle\bm{P}_{t}^{-1}-\left(\bm{P}_{t}+\bm{Q}_{t}\right)^{-1}\bm{Q}_{t}\bm{P}_{t}^{-1}

Hence, for any task
m\in[M]
and
\bm{y}\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
, we have

\displaystyle\|\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{y}\|^{2}_{\left(\sum_{i=1}^{n}\lambda^{*}_{m}(\bm{x}_{i})\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{x}_{i}\bm{x}_{i}^{\top}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}\right)^{-1}}=

\displaystyle\left(\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{y}\right)^{\top}\left(\sum_{i=1}^{n}\lambda^{*}_{m}(\bm{x}_{i})\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{x}_{i}\bm{x}_{i}^{\top}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}\right)^{-1}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{y}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\underbrace{\left(\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{y}\right)^{\top}\bm{P}_{t}^{-1}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{y}}_{\textup{Term 1}}-\underbrace{\left(\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{y}\right)^{\top}\left(\bm{P}_{t}+\bm{Q}_{t}\right)^{-1}\bm{Q}_{t}\bm{P}_{t}^{-1}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{y}}_{\textup{Term 2}}.

(12)

From Lemma
C.4
, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\hat{\bm{B}}_{t,\bot}^{\top}\bm{B}\right\|\leq\min\left\{\frac{1}{8k\cdot 2^{t}\sqrt{1+\zeta}},\ \frac{\omega}{6L_{x}^{2}}\right\}\leq\min\left\{\frac{1}{8k\cdot 2^{t}},\ \frac{\omega}{6L_{x}^{2}}\right\}.

Since
\bm{B}^{\top}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B}+\bm{B}^{\top}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t,\bot}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t,\bot}^{\top}\bm{B}=\bm{B}^{\top}(\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}+\hat{\bm{B}}_{t,\bot}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t,\bot}^{\top})\bm{B}=\bm{B}^{\top}\bm{B}=\bm{I}_{k}
, we have
\sigma^{2}_{\min}(\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B})=1-\|\hat{\bm{B}}_{t,\bot}^{\top}\bm{B}\|^{2}
.

Thus, we have

\displaystyle\sigma_{\min}(\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B})=\sqrt{1-\left\|\hat{\bm{B}}_{t,\bot}^{\top}\bm{B}\right\|^{2}}\geq\sqrt{1-\min\left\{\frac{1}{64k^{2}\cdot 2^{2t}},\ \frac{\omega^{2}}{36L_{x}^{4}}\right\}}>0,

which implies that
\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B}
is invertible.

Now, we first analyze Term 1 in Eq. (
12
).

\displaystyle\textup{Term 1}=

\displaystyle\left(\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{y}\right)^{\top}\bm{P}_{t}^{-1}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{y}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B}\bm{B}^{\top}\bm{y}+\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B}_{\bot}\bm{B}_{\bot}^{\top}\bm{y}\right)^{\top}\bm{P}_{t}^{-1}\left(\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B}\bm{B}^{\top}\bm{y}+\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B}_{\bot}\bm{B}_{\bot}^{\top}\bm{y}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\underbrace{\left(\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B}\bm{B}^{\top}\bm{y}\right)^{\top}\bm{P}_{t}^{-1}\left(\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B}\bm{B}^{\top}\bm{y}\right)}_{\textup{Term 1-1}}+\underbrace{\left(\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B}\bm{B}^{\top}\bm{y}\right)^{\top}\bm{P}_{t}^{-1}\left(\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B}_{\bot}\bm{B}_{\bot}^{\top}\bm{y}\right)}_{\textup{Term 1-2}}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\left(\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B}_{\bot}\bm{B}_{\bot}^{\top}\bm{y}\right)^{\top}\bm{P}_{t}^{-1}\left(\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B}\bm{B}^{\top}\bm{y}\right)}_{\textup{Term 1-3}}+\underbrace{\left(\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}B_{\bot}\bm{B}_{\bot}^{\top}\bm{y}\right)^{\top}\bm{P}_{t}^{-1}\left(\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B}_{\bot}\bm{B}_{\bot}^{\top}\bm{y}\right)}_{\textup{Term 1-4}}.

In the following, we bound Terms 1-1, 1-2, 1-3 and 1-4, respectively.

First, we have

\displaystyle\textup{Term 1-1}=

\displaystyle\left(\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B}\bm{B}^{\top}\bm{y}\right)^{\top}\left(\sum_{i=1}^{n}\lambda^{*}_{m}(\bm{x}_{i})\left(\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B}\bm{B}^{\top}\bm{x}_{i}\right)\cdot\left(\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B}\bm{B}^{\top}\bm{x}_{i}\right)^{\top}\right)^{-1}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B}\bm{B}^{\top}\bm{y}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B}\bm{B}^{\top}\bm{y}\right)^{\top}\left(\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B}\left(\sum_{i=1}^{n}\lambda^{*}_{m}(\bm{x}_{i})\bm{B}^{\top}\bm{x}_{i}\bm{x}_{i}^{\top}\bm{B}\right)\left(\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B}\right)^{\top}\right)^{-1}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B}\bm{B}^{\top}\bm{y}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B}\bm{B}^{\top}\bm{y}\right)^{\top}\left(\left(\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B}\right)^{-1}\right)^{\top}\left(\sum_{i=1}^{n}\lambda^{*}_{m}(\bm{x}_{i})\bm{B}^{\top}\bm{x}_{i}\bm{x}_{i}^{\top}\bm{B}\right)^{-1}\left(\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B}\right)^{-1}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B}\bm{B}^{\top}\bm{y}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(\bm{B}^{\top}\bm{y}\right)^{\top}\left(\sum_{i=1}^{n}\lambda^{*}_{m}(\bm{x}_{i})\bm{B}^{\top}\bm{x}_{i}\bm{x}_{i}^{\top}\bm{B}\right)^{-1}\bm{B}^{\top}\bm{y}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{B}^{\top}\bm{y}\right\|^{2}_{\left(\sum_{i=1}^{n}\lambda^{*}_{m}(\bm{x}_{i})\bm{B}^{\top}\bm{x}_{i}\bm{x}_{i}^{\top}\bm{B}\right)^{-1}}.

We note that since
\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B}\bm{B}^{\top}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}+\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B}_{\bot}\bm{B}_{\bot}^{\top}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}=\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}(B\bm{B}^{\top}+\bm{B}_{\bot}\bm{B}_{\bot}^{\top})\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}=\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}=\bm{I}_{k}
,
\sigma^{2}_{\min}\left(\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B}\right)=1-\left\|\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B}_{\bot}\right\|^{2}
.
In addition,
\left\|\left(\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B}\right)^{-1}\right\|=\frac{1}{\sigma_{\min}(\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B})}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{1-\left\|\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B}_{\bot}\right\|^{2}}}
.

Then, second, we have

\displaystyle\textup{Term 1-2}=

\displaystyle\left(\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B}\bm{B}^{\top}\bm{y}\right)^{\top}\left(\sum_{i=1}^{n}\lambda^{*}_{m}(\bm{x}_{i})\left(\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B}\bm{B}^{\top}\bm{x}_{i}\right)\cdot\left(\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B}\bm{B}^{\top}\bm{x}_{i}\right)^{\top}\right)^{-1}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B}_{\bot}\bm{B}_{\bot}^{\top}\bm{y}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B}\bm{B}^{\top}\bm{y}\right)^{\top}\left(\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B}\left(\sum_{i=1}^{n}\lambda^{*}_{m}(\bm{x}_{i})\bm{B}^{\top}\bm{x}_{i}\bm{x}_{i}^{\top}\bm{B}\right)\left(\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B}\right)^{\top}\right)^{-1}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B}_{\bot}\bm{B}_{\bot}^{\top}\bm{y}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B}\bm{B}^{\top}\bm{y}\right)^{\top}\left(\left(\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B}\right)^{-1}\right)^{\top}\left(\sum_{i=1}^{n}\lambda^{*}_{m}(\bm{x}_{i})\bm{B}^{\top}\bm{x}_{i}\bm{x}_{i}^{\top}\bm{B}\right)^{-1}\left(\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B}\right)^{-1}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B}_{\bot}\bm{B}_{\bot}^{\top}\bm{y}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(\bm{B}^{\top}\bm{y}\right)^{\top}\left(\sum_{i=1}^{n}\lambda^{*}_{m}(\bm{x}_{i})\bm{B}^{\top}\bm{x}_{i}\bm{x}_{i}^{\top}\bm{B}\right)^{-1}\left(\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B}\right)^{-1}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B}_{\bot}\bm{B}_{\bot}^{\top}\bm{y}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2L_{x}\cdot\frac{1}{\omega}\cdot\frac{1}{\sqrt{1-\left\|\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B}_{\bot}\right\|^{2}}}\cdot\left\|\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B}_{\bot}\right\|\cdot 2L_{x}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 4L_{x}^{2}\cdot\frac{1}{\omega}\cdot\frac{1}{\sqrt{1-\left(\frac{1}{8k\cdot 2^{t}}\right)^{2}}}\cdot\frac{1}{8k\cdot 2^{t}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 4L_{x}^{2}\cdot\frac{1}{\omega}\cdot\frac{1}{\sqrt{1-\frac{3}{4}}}\cdot\frac{1}{8k\cdot 2^{t}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{L_{x}^{2}}{k\omega\cdot 2^{t}}.

Third, we have

\displaystyle\textup{Term 1-3}=

\displaystyle\left(\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B}_{\bot}\bm{B}_{\bot}^{\top}\bm{y}\right)^{\top}\left(\sum_{i=1}^{n}\lambda^{*}_{m}(\bm{x}_{i})\left(\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B}\bm{B}^{\top}\bm{x}_{i}\right)\cdot\left(\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B}\bm{B}^{\top}\bm{x}_{i}\right)^{\top}\right)^{-1}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B}\bm{B}^{\top}\bm{y}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B}_{\bot}\bm{B}_{\bot}^{\top}\bm{y}\right)^{\top}\left(\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B}\left(\sum_{i=1}^{n}\lambda^{*}_{m}(\bm{x}_{i})\bm{B}^{\top}\bm{x}_{i}\bm{x}_{i}^{\top}\bm{B}\right)\left(\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B}\right)^{\top}\right)^{-1}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B}\bm{B}^{\top}\bm{y}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B}_{\bot}\bm{B}_{\bot}^{\top}\bm{y}\right)^{\top}\left(\left(\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B}\right)^{-1}\right)^{\top}\left(\sum_{i=1}^{n}\lambda^{*}_{m}(\bm{x}_{i})\bm{B}^{\top}\bm{x}_{i}\bm{x}_{i}^{\top}\bm{B}\right)^{-1}\left(\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B}\right)^{-1}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B}\bm{B}^{\top}\bm{y}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(\left(\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B}\right)^{-1}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B}_{\bot}\bm{B}_{\bot}^{\top}\bm{y}\right)^{\top}\left(\sum_{i=1}^{n}\lambda^{*}_{m}(\bm{x}_{i})\bm{B}^{\top}\bm{x}_{i}\bm{x}_{i}^{\top}\bm{B}\right)^{-1}\bm{B}^{\top}\bm{y},

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{1-\left\|\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B}_{\bot}\right\|^{2}}}\cdot\left\|\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B}_{\bot}\right\|\cdot 2L_{x}\cdot\frac{1}{\omega}\cdot 2L_{x}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 4L_{x}^{2}\cdot\frac{1}{\omega}\cdot\frac{1}{\sqrt{1-\left(\frac{1}{8k\cdot 2^{t}}\right)^{2}}}\cdot\frac{1}{8k\cdot 2^{t}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 4L_{x}^{2}\cdot\frac{1}{\omega}\cdot\frac{1}{\sqrt{1-\frac{3}{4}}}\cdot\frac{1}{8k\cdot 2^{t}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{L_{x}^{2}}{k\omega\cdot 2^{t}}.

Finally, we have

\displaystyle\textup{Term 1-4}=

\displaystyle\left(\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B}_{\bot}\bm{B}_{\bot}^{\top}\bm{y}\right)^{\top}\left(\sum_{i=1}^{n}\lambda^{*}_{m}(\bm{x}_{i})\left(\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B}\bm{B}^{\top}\bm{x}_{i}\right)\cdot\left(\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B}\bm{B}^{\top}\bm{x}_{i}\right)^{\top}\right)^{-1}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B}_{\bot}\bm{B}_{\bot}^{\top}\bm{y}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B}_{\bot}\bm{B}_{\bot}^{\top}\bm{y}\right)^{\top}\left(\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B}\left(\sum_{i=1}^{n}\lambda^{*}_{m}(\bm{x}_{i})\bm{B}^{\top}\bm{x}_{i}\bm{x}_{i}^{\top}\bm{B}\right)\left(\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B}\right)^{\top}\right)^{-1}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B}_{\bot}\bm{B}_{\bot}^{\top}\bm{y}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B}_{\bot}\bm{B}_{\bot}^{\top}\bm{y}\right)^{\top}\left(\left(\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B}\right)^{-1}\right)^{\top}\left(\sum_{i=1}^{n}\lambda^{*}_{m}(\bm{x}_{i})\bm{B}^{\top}\bm{x}_{i}\bm{x}_{i}^{\top}\bm{B}\right)^{-1}\left(\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B}\right)^{-1}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B}_{\bot}\bm{B}_{\bot}^{\top}\bm{y}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(\left(\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B}\right)^{-1}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B}_{\bot}\bm{B}_{\bot}^{\top}\bm{y}\right)^{\top}\left(\sum_{i=1}^{n}\lambda^{*}_{m}(\bm{x}_{i})\bm{B}^{\top}\bm{x}_{i}\bm{x}_{i}^{\top}\bm{B}\right)^{-1}\left(\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B}\right)^{-1}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B}_{\bot}\bm{B}_{\bot}^{\top}\bm{y},

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{1-\left\|\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B}_{\bot}\right\|^{2}}}\cdot\left\|\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B}_{\bot}\right\|\cdot 2L_{x}\right)^{2}\cdot\frac{1}{\omega}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(2L_{x}\cdot\frac{1}{\sqrt{1-\left(\frac{1}{8k\cdot 2^{t}}\right)^{2}}}\cdot\frac{1}{8k\cdot 2^{t}}\right)^{2}\cdot\frac{1}{\omega}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(2L_{x}\cdot\frac{1}{\sqrt{1-\frac{3}{4}}}\cdot\frac{1}{8k\cdot 2^{t}}\right)^{2}\cdot\frac{1}{\omega}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{L_{x}^{2}}{4k^{2}\omega\cdot 2^{2t}}.

Thus, we have

\displaystyle\textup{Term 1}=

\displaystyle\left(\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{y}\right)^{\top}\bm{P}_{t}^{-1}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{y}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{B}^{\top}\bm{y}\right\|^{2}_{\left(\sum_{i=1}^{n}\lambda^{*}_{m}(\bm{x}_{i})\bm{B}^{\top}\bm{x}_{i}\bm{x}_{i}^{\top}\bm{B}\right)^{-1}}+\frac{2L_{x}^{2}}{k\omega\cdot 2^{t}}+\frac{L_{x}^{2}}{4k^{2}\omega\cdot 2^{2t}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{B}^{\top}\bm{y}\right\|^{2}_{\left(\sum_{i=1}^{n}\lambda^{*}_{m}(\bm{x}_{i})\bm{B}^{\top}\bm{x}_{i}\bm{x}_{i}^{\top}\bm{B}\right)^{-1}}+\frac{3L_{x}^{2}}{k\omega\cdot 2^{t}}.

(13)

Next, we investigate Term 2. In order to bound Term 2, we first bound the minimum singular value of
\bm{P}_{t}
and the maximum singular value of
\bm{Q}_{t}
.

Since
\bm{P}_{t}=\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B}(\sum_{i=1}^{n}\lambda^{*}(\bm{x}_{i})\bm{B}^{\top}\bm{x}_{i}\bm{x}_{i}^{\top}\bm{B})(\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B})^{\top}
, we have

\displaystyle\sigma_{\min}(\bm{P}_{t})\geq

\displaystyle\sigma^{2}_{\min}(\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B})\cdot\omega

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(1-\|\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B}_{\bot}\|^{2}\right)\omega

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\left(1-\frac{1}{8^{2}k^{2}\cdot 2^{2t}}\right)\omega

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\frac{3}{4}\omega.

Since
\bm{Q}_{t}=\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B}(\sum_{i=1}^{n}\lambda^{*}(\bm{x}_{i})\bm{B}^{\top}\bm{x}_{i}\bm{x}_{i}^{\top}\bm{B}_{\bot})(\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B}_{\bot})^{\top}+\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B}_{\bot}(\sum_{i=1}^{n}\lambda^{*}(\bm{x}_{i})\bm{B}^{\top}_{\bot}\bm{x}_{i}\bm{x}_{i}^{\top}\bm{B})(\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B})^{\top}+\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B}_{\bot}(\sum_{i=1}^{n}\lambda^{*}(\bm{x}_{i})\bm{B}^{\top}_{\bot}\bm{x}_{i}\bm{x}_{i}^{\top}\bm{B}_{\bot})(\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B}_{\bot})^{\top}
, we have

\displaystyle\sigma_{\max}(\bm{Q}_{t})\leq

\displaystyle 3L_{x}^{2}\left\|\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{B}_{\bot}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\min\left\{\frac{3L_{x}^{2}}{8k\cdot 2^{t}},\ \frac{\omega}{2}\right\}.

Then, we can bound Term 2 as

\displaystyle\textup{Term 2}=

\displaystyle\left(\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{y}\right)^{\top}\left(\bm{P}_{t}+\bm{Q}_{t}\right)^{-1}\bm{Q}_{t}\bm{P}_{t}^{-1}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{y}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left\|\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{y}\right\|^{2}\cdot\left\|\left(\bm{P}_{t}+\bm{Q}_{t}\right)^{-1}\right\|\cdot\left\|\bm{Q}_{t}\right\|\cdot\left\|\bm{P}_{t}^{-1}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{4L_{x}^{2}\cdot\sigma_{\max}\left(\bm{Q}_{t}\right)}{\sigma_{\min}\left(\bm{P}_{t}+\bm{Q}_{t}\right)\cdot\sigma_{\min}\left(\bm{P}_{t}\right)}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{4L_{x}^{2}\cdot\sigma_{\max}\left(\bm{Q}_{t}\right)}{\left(\sigma_{\min}\left(\bm{P}_{t}\right)-\sigma_{\max}\left(\bm{Q}_{t}\right)\right)\cdot\sigma_{\min}\left(\bm{P}_{t}\right)}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{4L_{x}^{2}\cdot\frac{3L_{x}^{2}}{8k\cdot 2^{t}}}{\left(\frac{3}{4}\omega-\frac{1}{2}\omega\right)\cdot\frac{3}{4}\omega}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{8L_{x}^{4}}{k\omega^{2}\cdot 2^{t}}.

(14)

Plugging Eqs. (
13
) and (
14
) into Eq. (
12
), we have

\displaystyle\|\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{y}\|^{2}_{\left(\sum_{i=1}^{n}\lambda^{*}_{m}(\bm{x}_{i})\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{x}_{i}\bm{x}_{i}^{\top}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}\right)^{-1}}\leq

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{B}^{\top}\bm{y}\right\|^{2}_{\left(\sum_{i=1}^{n}\lambda^{*}_{m}(\bm{x}_{i})\bm{B}^{\top}\bm{x}_{i}\bm{x}_{i}^{\top}\bm{B}\right)^{-1}}+\frac{3L_{x}^{2}}{k\omega\cdot 2^{t}}+\frac{8L_{x}^{4}}{k\omega^{2}\cdot 2^{t}}.

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{B}^{\top}\bm{y}\right\|^{2}_{\left(\sum_{i=1}^{n}\lambda^{*}_{m}(\bm{x}_{i})\bm{B}^{\top}\bm{x}_{i}\bm{x}_{i}^{\top}\bm{B}\right)^{-1}}+\frac{11L_{x}^{4}}{k\omega^{2}\cdot 2^{t}}.

∎

Below we prove the sample complexity for algorithm
\mathtt{DouExpDes}
(Theorem
4.1
).

Proof of Theorem
4.1
.

According to Lemmas
C.3
and
C.7
, we have
\Pr[\mathcal{E}\cap\mathcal{F}]\geq 1-\delta
. Below, supposing that event
\mathcal{E}\cap\mathcal{F}
holds, we prove the correctness and sample complexity.

We first prove the correctness.

For any task
m\in[M]
, let
t^{*}_{m}
denote the first phase which satisfies
|\hat{\mathcal{X}}_{t,m}|=1
. Let
t_{*}=\max_{m\in[M]}t^{*}_{m}
denote the total number of phases used.
For any task
m\in[M]
, let
\Delta_{m,\min}:=\min_{\bm{x}\in\mathcal{X}\setminus\{\bm{x}^{*}_{m}\}}(\bm{x}^{*}_{m}-\bm{x})^{\top}\bm{\theta}_{m}
denote the minimum reward gap for task
m
. Let
\Delta_{\min}:=\min_{m\in[M]}\Delta_{\min,m}
denote the minimum reward gap among all tasks.

From Lemma
C.9
, we can obtain the following facts: (i) For any task
m\in[M]
, the optimal arm
\bm{x}^{*}_{m}
will never be eliminated. (ii)
t^{*}_{m}\leq\lceil\log(\frac{4}{\Delta_{m,\min}})\rceil+1
, and thus,
t_{*}\leq\lceil\log(\frac{4}{\Delta_{\min}})\rceil+1
. Therefore, after at most
\lceil\log(\frac{4}{\Delta_{\min}})\rceil+1
phases, algorithm
\mathtt{DouExpDes}
will return the optimal arms
\bm{x}^{*}_{m}
for all tasks
m\in[M]
.

Now we prove the sample complexity.
In the following, we first prove that the sample complexity of algorithm
\mathtt{DouExpDes}
is bounded by
\tilde{O}(\frac{Mk}{\Delta_{\min}^{2}}\log(\delta^{-1})+(\rho^{E})^{2}dk^{4}L_{x}^{2}L_{w}^{2}D\log^{4}(\delta^{-1}))
.

Recall that
p=\frac{180d}{\zeta^{2}}
and
\zeta=\frac{1}{10}
. Then, summing the number of samples used in subroutines
\mathtt{FeatRecover}
and
\mathtt{EliLowRep}
in all phases (Line
4
in Algorithm
2
, Line
5
in Algorithm
3
), we have that the total number of samples is

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{t^{*}}pMT_{t}+\sum_{m=1}^{M}\sum_{t=1}^{t^{*}_{m}}N_{t,m}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{t^{*}}p\cdot O\Bigg(\left(1+\zeta\right)^{3}(\rho^{E})^{2}k^{4}L_{x}^{2}L_{\theta}^{2}\max\left\{2^{2t},\ \frac{L_{x}^{4}}{\omega^{2}}\right\}\log^{2}\left(\frac{p}{\delta_{t}}\right)\cdot

\displaystyle\hskip 50.00008pt\log^{2}\left(\left(1+\zeta\right)\rho^{E}kpL_{x}L_{\theta}\max\left\{2^{t},\ \frac{L_{x}}{\omega}\right\}\frac{1}{\delta_{t}}\log\left(\frac{p}{\delta_{t}}\right)\right)\Bigg)

\displaystyle+\sum_{m=1}^{M}\sum_{t=1}^{t^{*}_{m}}O\left(2^{2t}(1+\zeta)\rho^{G}_{t,m}\log\left(\frac{n^{2}M}{\delta_{t}}\right)+\frac{k}{\zeta^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{O(\log(\Delta_{\min}^{-1}))}O\Bigg((\rho^{E})^{2}k^{4}dL_{x}^{2}L_{\theta}^{2}\max\left\{2^{2t},\ \frac{L_{x}^{4}}{\omega^{2}}\right\}\log^{2}\left(\frac{d\log(\Delta_{\min}^{-1})}{\delta}\right)\cdot

\displaystyle\hskip 50.00008pt\log^{2}\left(\rho^{E}kdL_{x}L_{\theta}\max\left\{\Delta_{\min}^{-1},\ \frac{L_{x}}{\omega}\right\}\frac{\log(\Delta_{\min}^{-1})}{\delta}\log\left(\frac{d\log(\Delta_{\min}^{-1})}{\delta}\right)\right)\Bigg)

\displaystyle+\sum_{m=1}^{M}\sum_{t=1}^{O(\log(\Delta_{m,\min}^{-1}))}O\left(2^{2t}\rho^{G}_{t,m}\log\left(\frac{n^{2}M\log(\Delta_{m,\min}^{-1})}{\delta}\right)+k\right)

(15)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O\Bigg((\rho^{E})^{2}k^{4}dL_{x}^{2}L_{\theta}^{2}\max\left\{\Delta_{\min}^{-2},\ \frac{L_{x}^{4}\log(\Delta_{\min}^{-1})}{\omega^{2}}\right\}\log^{2}\left(\frac{d\log(\Delta_{\min}^{-1})}{\delta}\right)\cdot

\displaystyle\hskip 50.00008pt\log^{2}\left(\rho^{E}kdL_{x}L_{\theta}\max\left\{\Delta_{\min}^{-1},\ \frac{L_{x}}{\omega}\right\}\frac{\log(\Delta_{\min}^{-1})}{\delta}\log\left(\frac{d\log(\Delta_{\min}^{-1})}{\delta}\right)\right)\Bigg)

\displaystyle+O\left(Mk\Delta_{\min}^{-2}\log\left(\frac{n^{2}M\log(\Delta_{\min}^{-1})}{\delta}\right)\right).

∎

Next, we prove that the sample complexity of algorithm
\mathtt{DouExpDes}
is bounded by

\tilde{O}(\sum_{m=1}^{M}\min_{\bm{\lambda}\in\triangle_{\mathcal{X}}}\max_{\bm{x}\in\mathcal{X}\setminus\{\bm{x}^{*}_{m}\}}\frac{\|\bm{B}^{\top}(\bm{x}^{*}_{m}-\bm{x})\|^{2}_{\bm{A}(\bm{\lambda})^{-1}}}{((\bm{x}^{*}_{m}-\bm{x})^{\top}\bm{\theta}_{m})^{2}}\log(\delta^{-1})+(\rho^{E})^{2}dk^{4}L_{x}^{2}L_{w}^{2}D\log^{4}(\delta^{-1})).

From Eq. (
15
), we have that with probability
1-\delta
, the number of samples used by algorithm
\mathtt{DouExpDes}
is bounded by

\displaystyle\tilde{O}\Bigg(\sum_{t=1}^{\log(\Delta_{\min}^{-1})}(\rho^{E})^{2}k^{4}dL_{x}^{2}L_{\theta}^{2}\max\left\{2^{2t},\ \frac{L_{x}^{4}}{\omega^{2}}\right\}+\sum_{m=1}^{M}\sum_{t=1}^{\log(\Delta_{m,\min}^{-1})}2^{2t}\rho^{G}_{t,m}+Mk\Bigg).

(16)

For any
\mathcal{Z}\subseteq\mathcal{X}
,
\mathcal{Y}(\mathcal{Z}):=\{\bm{x}-\bm{x}^{\prime}:\ \forall\bm{x},\bm{x}^{\prime}\in\mathcal{Z},\ \bm{x}\neq\bm{x}^{\prime}\}
and
\mathcal{Y}^{*}_{m}(\mathcal{Z}):=\{\bm{x}^{*}_{m}-\bm{x}:\ \forall\bm{x}\in\mathcal{Z},\ \bm{x}\neq\bm{x}^{*}_{m}\}
.
Then, we have that for any task
m\in[M]
and phase
t\geq 2
,

\displaystyle\left(2^{t}\right)^{2}\rho^{G}_{t,m}=

\displaystyle\left(2^{t}\right)^{2}\min_{\bm{\lambda}\in\triangle_{\mathcal{X}}}\max_{\bm{y}\in\mathcal{Y}(\hat{\mathcal{X}}_{t,m})}\|\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{y}\|^{2}_{\left(\sum_{i=1}^{n}\lambda(\bm{x}_{i})\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{x}_{i}\bm{x}_{i}^{\top}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}\right)^{-1}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(2^{t}\right)^{2}\max_{\bm{y}\in\mathcal{Y}(\hat{\mathcal{X}}_{t,m})}\|\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{y}\|^{2}_{\left(\sum_{i=1}^{n}\lambda^{*}_{m}(\bm{x}_{i})\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{x}_{i}\bm{x}_{i}^{\top}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}\right)^{-1}}

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{(a)}}{\leq}

\displaystyle\left(2^{t}\right)^{2}\max_{\bm{y}\in\mathcal{Y}(\mathcal{Z}_{t,m})}\|\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{y}\|^{2}_{\left(\sum_{i=1}^{n}\lambda^{*}_{m}(\bm{x}_{i})\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{x}_{i}\bm{x}_{i}^{\top}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}\right)^{-1}}

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{(b)}}{\leq}

\displaystyle 4\left(2^{t}\right)^{2}\max_{\bm{y}\in\mathcal{Y}^{*}_{m}(\mathcal{Z}_{t,m})}\|\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{y}\|^{2}_{\left(\sum_{i=1}^{n}\lambda^{*}_{m}(\bm{x}_{i})\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}^{\top}\bm{x}_{i}\bm{x}_{i}^{\top}\hat{\bm{B}}_{t}\right)^{-1}}

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{(c)}}{\leq}

\displaystyle 4\left(2^{t}\right)^{2}\left(\max_{\bm{y}\in\mathcal{Y}^{*}_{m}(\mathcal{Z}_{t,m})}\|\bm{B}^{\top}\bm{y}\|^{2}_{\left(\sum_{i=1}^{n}\lambda^{*}_{m}(\bm{x}_{i})\bm{B}^{\top}\bm{x}_{i}\bm{x}_{i}^{\top}\bm{B}\right)^{-1}}+\frac{11L_{x}^{4}}{\omega^{2}k\cdot 2^{t}}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 4\left(\frac{16\max_{\bm{y}\in\mathcal{Y}^{*}_{m}(\mathcal{Z}_{t,m})}\|\bm{B}^{\top}\bm{y}\|^{2}_{\left(\sum_{i=1}^{n}\lambda^{*}_{m}(\bm{x}_{i})\bm{B}^{\top}\bm{x}_{i}\bm{x}_{i}^{\top}\bm{B}\right)^{-1}}}{\left(4\cdot 2^{-t}\right)^{2}}+\frac{11L_{x}^{4}\cdot 2^{t}}{\omega^{2}k}\right)

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{(d)}}{\leq}

\displaystyle 4\left(16\max_{\bm{y}\in\mathcal{Y}^{*}_{m}(\mathcal{Z}_{t,m})}\frac{\|\bm{B}^{\top}\bm{y}\|^{2}_{\left(\sum_{i=1}^{n}\lambda^{*}_{m}(\bm{x}_{i})\bm{B}^{\top}\bm{x}_{i}\bm{x}_{i}^{\top}\bm{B}\right)^{-1}}}{\left(\bm{y}^{\top}\bm{\theta}_{m}\right)^{2}}+\frac{11L_{x}^{4}\cdot 2^{t}}{\omega^{2}k}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 4\left(16\max_{\bm{y}\in\mathcal{Y}^{*}_{m}(\mathcal{X})}\frac{\|\bm{B}^{\top}\bm{y}\|^{2}_{\left(\sum_{i=1}^{n}\lambda^{*}_{m}(\bm{x}_{i})\bm{B}^{\top}\bm{x}_{i}\bm{x}_{i}^{\top}\bm{B}\right)^{-1}}}{\left(\bm{y}^{\top}\bm{\theta}_{m}\right)^{2}}+\frac{11L_{x}^{4}\cdot 2^{t}}{\omega^{2}k}\right)

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{(e)}}{=}

\displaystyle 4\left(16\min_{\bm{\lambda}\in\triangle_{\mathcal{X}}}\max_{\bm{y}\in\mathcal{Y}^{*}_{m}(\mathcal{X})}\frac{\|\bm{B}^{\top}\bm{y}\|^{2}_{\left(\sum_{i=1}^{n}\lambda(\bm{x}_{i})\bm{B}^{\top}\bm{x}_{i}\bm{x}_{i}^{\top}\bm{B}\right)^{-1}}}{\left(\bm{y}^{\top}\bm{\theta}_{m}\right)^{2}}+\frac{11L_{x}^{4}\cdot 2^{t}}{\omega^{2}k}\right).

(17)

Here inequality (a) is due to
\hat{\mathcal{X}}_{t,m}\subseteq\mathcal{Z}_{t,m}
(from Lemma
C.9
). Inequality (b) uses the fact that for any
\bm{y}=\bm{x}_{i}-\bm{x}_{j}\in\mathcal{Y}(\mathcal{Z}_{t,m})
, we can write
\bm{y}=(\bm{x}^{*}_{m}-\bm{x}_{j})-(\bm{x}^{*}_{m}-\bm{x}_{i})
, and the triangle inequality. Inequality (c) follows from Lemma
C.10
, and inequality (d) is due to that for any
\bm{y}\in\mathcal{Y}^{*}_{m}(\mathcal{Z}_{t,m})
,
\bm{y}^{\top}\bm{\theta}_{m}\leq 4\cdot 2^{-t}
(from the definition of
\mathcal{Z}_{t,m}
). Equality (e) comes from the definition of
\bm{\lambda}^{*}_{m}
.

Let
L:=\log^{2}(\frac{d\log(\Delta_{\min}^{-1})}{\delta})\cdot\log^{2}(\rho^{E}kdL_{x}L_{\theta}\max\{\Delta_{\min}^{-1},\ \frac{L_{x}}{\omega}\}\frac{\log(\Delta_{\min}^{-1})}{\delta}\log(\frac{d\log(\Delta_{\min}^{-1})}{\delta}))
.
Plugging Eq. (
17
) into Eq. (
16
), we have that with probability
1-\delta
, the number of samples used by algorithm
\mathtt{DouExpDes}
is bounded by

\displaystyle O\left(\sum_{m=1}^{M}\!\!\!\sum_{t=1}^{\log(\Delta_{m,\min}^{-1})}\!\!\!\!\!\!2^{2t}\rho^{G}_{t,m}\log\left(\frac{n^{2}M\log(\Delta_{m,\min}^{-1})}{\delta}\right)+Mk\log(\Delta_{\min}^{-1})+\sum_{t=1}^{\log(\Delta_{\min}^{-1})}(\rho^{E})^{2}k^{4}dL_{x}^{2}L_{\theta}^{2}\max\left\{2^{2t},\ \frac{L_{x}^{4}}{\omega^{2}}\right\}L\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O\Bigg(\sum_{m=1}^{M}\sum_{t=2}^{\log(\Delta_{m,\min}^{-1})}\Bigg(\min_{\bm{\lambda}\in\triangle_{\mathcal{X}}}\max_{\bm{y}\in\mathcal{Y}^{*}_{m}(\mathcal{X})}\frac{\|\bm{B}^{\top}\bm{y}\|^{2}_{\left(\sum_{i=1}^{n}\lambda(\bm{x}_{i})\bm{B}^{\top}\bm{x}_{i}\bm{x}_{i}^{\top}\bm{B}\right)^{-1}}}{\left(\bm{y}^{\top}\bm{\theta}_{m}\right)^{2}}+\frac{L_{x}^{4}\cdot 2^{t}}{\omega^{2}k}\Bigg)\cdot\log\left(\frac{n^{2}M\log(\Delta_{m,\min}^{-1})}{\delta}\right)

\displaystyle+\sum_{m=1}^{M}\rho^{*}_{1,m}\log\left(\frac{n^{2}M\log(\Delta_{m,\min}^{-1})}{\delta}\right)+Mk\log(\Delta_{\min}^{-1})+\sum_{t=1}^{\log(\Delta_{\min}^{-1})}(\rho^{E})^{2}k^{4}dL_{x}^{2}L_{\theta}^{2}\max\left\{2^{2t},\ \frac{L_{x}^{4}}{\omega^{2}}\right\}\cdot L\Bigg)

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{(a)}}{=}

\displaystyle O\Bigg(\sum_{m=1}^{M}\min_{\bm{\lambda}\in\triangle_{\mathcal{X}}}\max_{\bm{y}\in\mathcal{Y}^{*}_{m}(\mathcal{X})}\frac{\|\bm{B}^{\top}\bm{y}\|^{2}_{\left(\sum_{i=1}^{n}\lambda(\bm{x}_{i})\bm{B}^{\top}\bm{x}_{i}\bm{x}_{i}^{\top}\bm{B}\right)^{-1}}}{\left(\bm{y}^{\top}\bm{\theta}_{m}\right)^{2}}\cdot\log\left(\frac{n^{2}M\log(\Delta_{m,\min}^{-1})}{\delta}\right)\cdot\log(\Delta_{m,\min}^{-1})

\displaystyle+\frac{ML_{x}^{4}}{\omega^{2}k\cdot\Delta_{\min}}\cdot\log\left(\frac{n^{2}M\log(\Delta_{\min}^{-1})}{\delta}\right)+Mk\cdot\log\left(\frac{n^{2}M\log(\Delta_{\min}^{-1})}{\delta}\right)\cdot\log(\Delta_{\min}^{-1})

\displaystyle\qquad+(\rho^{E})^{2}k^{4}dL_{x}^{2}L_{\theta}^{2}\max\left\{\Delta_{\min}^{-2},\ \frac{L_{x}^{4}\cdot\log(\Delta_{\min}^{-1})}{\omega^{2}}\right\}\cdot L\Bigg),

where equality (a) uses Lemma
C.6
.

When
L_{x}=\omega=\Theta(1)
, we have that with probability
1-\delta
, the sample complexity of algorithm
\mathtt{DouExpDes}
is bounded by

\displaystyle\tilde{O}\Bigg(\sum_{m=1}^{M}\min_{\bm{\lambda}\in\triangle_{\mathcal{X}}}\max_{\bm{y}\in\mathcal{Y}^{*}_{m}(\mathcal{X})}\frac{\|\bm{B}^{\top}\bm{y}\|^{2}_{\left(\sum_{i=1}^{n}\lambda(\bm{x}_{i})\bm{B}^{\top}\bm{x}_{i}\bm{x}_{i}^{\top}\bm{B}\right)^{-1}}}{\left(\bm{y}^{\top}\bm{\theta}_{m}\right)^{2}}\log\left(\frac{1}{\delta}\right)+(\rho^{E})^{2}k^{4}dL_{x}^{2}L_{\theta}^{2}\max\left\{\Delta_{\min}^{-2},\ \frac{L_{x}^{4}}{\omega^{2}}\right\}\log^{4}\left(\frac{1}{\delta}\right)\Bigg).

Appendix D
Proofs for Algorithm
\mathtt{C\mathchar 45\relax DouExpDes}

In this section, we present the proofs for Algorithm
\mathtt{C\mathchar 45\relax DouExpDes}
.

D.1
Context Distribution Estimation and Sample Batch Planning

Define
\lambda_{\mathcal{D}}^{E}
and
\rho_{\mathcal{D}}^{E}
as the optimal solution and the optimal value of the following E-optimal design optimization:

\displaystyle\min_{\bm{\lambda}\in\triangle_{\mathcal{A}}}\left\|\left(\sum_{a\in\mathcal{A}}\lambda(a)\mathbb{E}_{s\sim\mathcal{D}}\left[\bm{\phi}(s,a)\bm{\phi}(s,a)^{\top}\right]\right)^{-1}\right\|.

(18)

Lemma D.1
.

It holds that

\displaystyle\rho_{\mathcal{D}}^{E}\leq\frac{1}{\nu}.

Proof of Lemma
D.1
.

The optimization in Eq. (
18
) is equivalent to maximize the minimum singular value of the matrix
\sum_{a\in\mathcal{A}}\lambda(a)\mathbb{E}_{s\sim\mathcal{D}}\left[\bm{\phi}(s,a)\bm{\phi}(s,a)^{\top}\right]
.

Thus,
\lambda_{\mathcal{D}}^{E}
is the optimal solution of the following optimization:

\displaystyle\max_{\bm{\lambda}\in\triangle_{\mathcal{A}}}\sigma_{\min}\left(\sum_{a\in\mathcal{A}}\lambda(a)\mathbb{E}_{s\sim\mathcal{D}}\left[\bm{\phi}(s,a)\bm{\phi}(s,a)^{\top}\right]\right).

Using Assumption
3.3
, we have

\displaystyle\sigma_{\min}\left(\sum_{a\in\mathcal{A}}\lambda_{\mathcal{D}}^{E}\mathbb{E}_{s\sim\mathcal{D}}\left[\bm{\phi}(s,a)\bm{\phi}(s,a)^{\top}\right]\right)\geq\nu.

Then, we have

\displaystyle\rho_{\mathcal{D}}^{E}=

\displaystyle\left\|\left(\sum_{a\in\mathcal{A}}\lambda_{\mathcal{D}}^{E}\mathbb{E}_{s\sim\mathcal{D}}\left[\bm{\phi}(s,a)\bm{\phi}(s,a)^{\top}\right]\right)^{-1}\right\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sigma_{\min}\left(\sum_{a\in\mathcal{A}}\lambda_{\mathcal{D}}^{E}\mathbb{E}_{s\sim\mathcal{D}}\left[\bm{\phi}(s,a)\bm{\phi}(s,a)^{\top}\right]\right)}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\nu}.

∎

Define event

\displaystyle\mathcal{K}:=\left\{\left\|\mathbb{E}_{s\sim\hat{\mathcal{D}}}\left[\bm{\phi}(s,a)\bm{\phi}(s,a)^{\top}\right]-\mathbb{E}_{s\sim\mathcal{D}}\left[\bm{\phi}(s,a)\bm{\phi}(s,a)^{\top}\right]\right\|\leq\frac{8L_{\phi}^{2}\log\left(\frac{20d|\mathcal{A}|}{\delta}\right)}{\sqrt{T_{0}}},\ \forall a\in\mathcal{A}\right\}.

Lemma D.2
.

It holds that

\displaystyle\Pr\left[\mathcal{K}\right]\geq 1-\frac{\delta}{5}.

Furthermore, if event
\mathcal{K}
holds and

\displaystyle T_{0}=\left\lceil\frac{32^{2}(1+\zeta)^{2}L_{\phi}^{4}}{\nu^{2}}\log^{2}\left(\frac{20d|\mathcal{A}|}{\delta}\right)\right\rceil,

we have that for any
a\in\mathcal{A}
,

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbb{E}_{s\sim\hat{\mathcal{D}}}\left[\bm{\phi}(s,a)\bm{\phi}(s,a)^{\top}\right]-\mathbb{E}_{s\sim\mathcal{D}}\left[\bm{\phi}(s,a)\bm{\phi}(s,a)^{\top}\right]\right\|\leq

\displaystyle\frac{\nu}{4(1+\zeta)}.

Proof of Lemma
D.2
.

For any
(s,a)\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{A}
,
\|\bm{\phi}(s,a)\bm{\phi}(s,a)^{\top}\|\leq L_{\phi}^{2}
. Then, using the matrix Bernstern inequality (Lemma
E.2
) and a union bound over
a\in\mathcal{A}
, we have that with probability
1-\frac{\delta}{5}
, for any
a\in\mathcal{A}
,

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbb{E}_{s\sim\hat{\mathcal{D}}}\left[\bm{\phi}(s,a)\bm{\phi}(s,a)^{\top}\right]-\mathbb{E}_{s\sim\mathcal{D}}\left[\bm{\phi}(s,a)\bm{\phi}(s,a)^{\top}\right]\right\|\leq

\displaystyle 4L_{\phi}^{2}\sqrt{\frac{\log\left(\frac{10\cdot 2d|\mathcal{A}|}{\delta}\right)}{T_{0}}}+\frac{4L_{\phi}^{2}\log\left(\frac{10\cdot 2d|\mathcal{A}|}{\delta}\right)}{T_{0}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{8L_{\phi}^{2}\log\left(\frac{20d|\mathcal{A}|}{\delta}\right)}{\sqrt{T_{0}}}.

If
T_{0}\geq 32^{2}(1+\zeta)^{2}\nu^{-2}L_{\phi}^{4}\log^{2}\left(\frac{20d|\mathcal{A}|}{\delta}\right)
, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbb{E}_{s\sim\hat{\mathcal{D}}}\left[\bm{\phi}(s,a)\bm{\phi}(s,a)^{\top}\right]-\mathbb{E}_{s\sim\mathcal{D}}\left[\bm{\phi}(s,a)\bm{\phi}(s,a)^{\top}\right]\right\|\leq\frac{\nu}{4(1+\zeta)},

which completes the proof.
∎

Define event

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}:=\Bigg\{

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{p}\bm{\phi}(s_{m,j,i}^{(\ell)},\bar{a}_{i})\bm{\phi}(s_{m,j,i}^{(\ell)},\bar{a}_{i})^{\top}-\sum_{i=1}^{p}\mathbb{E}_{s\sim\mathcal{D}}\left[\bm{\phi}(s,\bar{a}_{i})\bm{\phi}(s,\bar{a}_{i})^{\top}\right]\right\|\leq 8L_{\phi}^{2}\sqrt{p}\log\left(\frac{40dMT}{\delta}\right),

\displaystyle\forall m\in[M],\ \forall j\in[T],\ \forall\ell\in\{1,2\}\Bigg\}.

Lemma D.3
.

It holds that

\displaystyle\Pr\left[\mathcal{L}\right]\geq 1-\frac{\delta}{5}.

Furthermore, if event
\mathcal{L}
holds and

\displaystyle p=\left\lceil\frac{32^{2}(1+\zeta)^{2}L_{\phi}^{4}}{\nu^{2}}\log^{2}\left(\frac{40dMT}{\delta}\right)\right\rceil,

(19)

we have that for any
m\in[M]
,
j\in[T]
and
\ell\in\{1,2\}
,

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{p}\bm{\phi}(s_{m,j,i}^{(\ell)},\bar{a}_{i})\bm{\phi}(s_{m,j,i}^{(\ell)},\bar{a}_{i})^{\top}-\sum_{i=1}^{p}\mathbb{E}_{s\sim\mathcal{D}}\left[\bm{\phi}(s,\bar{a}_{i})\bm{\phi}(s,\bar{a}_{i})^{\top}\right]\right\|\leq

\displaystyle\frac{p\nu}{4(1+\zeta)}.

Here, the value of
T
is specified in Eq. (
29
).

Proof of Lemma
D.3
.

For any
(s,a)\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{A}
,
\|\bm{\phi}(s,a)\bm{\phi}(s,a)^{\top}\|\leq L_{\phi}^{2}
. Then, using the matrix Bernstern inequality (Lemma
E.2
) and a union bound over
m\in[M]
,
j\in[T]
and
\ell\in\{1,2\}
, we have that with probability
1-\frac{\delta}{5}
, for any
m\in[M]
,
j\in[T]
and
\ell\in\{1,2\}
,

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{p}\!\bm{\phi}(s_{m,j,i}^{(\ell)},\bar{a}_{i})\bm{\phi}(s_{m,j,i}^{(\ell)},\bar{a}_{i})^{\top}\!\!-\!\!\sum_{i=1}^{p}\mathbb{E}_{s\sim\mathcal{D}}\left[\bm{\phi}(s,\bar{a}_{i})\bm{\phi}(s,\bar{a}_{i})^{\top}\right]\right\|\leq

\displaystyle 4L_{\phi}^{2}\sqrt{p\log\left(\frac{10\cdot 4dMT}{\delta}\right)}\!+\!4L_{\phi}^{2}\log\left(\frac{10\cdot 4dMT}{\delta}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 8L_{\phi}^{2}\sqrt{p}\log\left(\frac{40dMT}{\delta}\right).

In addition, if
p\geq 32^{2}(1+\zeta)^{2}\nu^{-2}L_{\phi}^{4}\log^{2}\left(\frac{40dMT}{\delta}\right)
, we have that

\displaystyle 8L_{\phi}^{2}\sqrt{p}\log\left(\frac{40dMT}{\delta}\right)\leq\frac{p\nu}{4(1+\zeta)}

and thus,

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{p}\bm{\phi}(s_{m,j,i}^{(\ell)},\bar{a}_{i})\bm{\phi}(s_{m,j,i}^{(\ell)},\bar{a}_{i})^{\top}-\sum_{i=1}^{p}\mathbb{E}_{s\sim\mathcal{D}}\left[\bm{\phi}(s,\bar{a}_{i})\bm{\phi}(s,\bar{a}_{i})^{\top}\right]\right\|\leq\frac{p\nu}{4(1+\zeta)},

which completes the proof.
∎

For any task
m\in[M]
, round
j\in[T]
and
\ell\in\{1,2\}
, let

\bm{\Phi}_{m,j}^{(\ell)}=\begin{bmatrix}\bm{\phi}(s_{m,j,1}^{(\ell)},\bar{a}_{1})^{\top}\\
\dots\\
\bm{\phi}(s_{m,j,p}^{(\ell)},\bar{a}_{p})^{\top}\end{bmatrix},

and

(\bm{\Phi}_{m,j}^{(\ell)})^{+}=((\bm{\Phi}_{m,j}^{(\ell)})^{\top}\bm{\Phi}_{m,j}^{(\ell)})^{-1}(\bm{\Phi}_{m,j}^{(\ell)})^{\top}.

Lemma D.4
.

Suppose that event
\mathcal{K}\cap\mathcal{L}
holds. Then, for any
m\in[M]
,
j\in[T]
and
\ell\in\{1,2\}
,

\displaystyle\left\|(\bm{\Phi}_{m,j}^{(\ell)})^{+}\right\|\leq 2\sqrt{\frac{(1+\zeta)}{p\nu}}.

Proof of Lemma
D.4
.

We first assume that
(\bm{\Phi}_{m,j}^{(\ell)})^{\top}\bm{\Phi}_{m,j}^{(\ell)}
is invertible. In our later analysis, we will prove that as long as
T_{0}
and
p
are large enough,
(\bm{\Phi}_{m,j}^{(\ell)})^{\top}\bm{\Phi}_{m,j}^{(\ell)}
is invertible.

For any
m\in[M]
,
j\in[T]
and
\ell\in\{1,2\}
, we have

\displaystyle\left\|(\bm{\Phi}_{m,j}^{(\ell)})^{+}\right\|=

\displaystyle\left\|((\bm{\Phi}_{m,j}^{(\ell)})^{\top}\bm{\Phi}_{m,j}^{(\ell)})^{-1}(\bm{\Phi}_{m,j}^{(\ell)})^{\top}\right\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sqrt{\left\|((\bm{\Phi}_{m,j}^{(\ell)})^{\top}\bm{\Phi}_{m,j}^{(\ell)})^{-1}(\bm{\Phi}_{m,j}^{(\ell)})^{\top}\bm{\Phi}_{m,j}^{(\ell)}((\bm{\Phi}_{m,j}^{(\ell)})^{\top}\bm{\Phi}_{m,j}^{(\ell)})^{-1}\right\|}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sqrt{\left\|((\bm{\Phi}_{m,j}^{(\ell)})^{\top}\bm{\Phi}_{m,j}^{(\ell)})^{-1}\right\|}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{\sigma_{\min}\left((\bm{\Phi}_{m,j}^{(\ell)})^{\top}\bm{\Phi}_{m,j}^{(\ell)}\right)}}.

(20)

In addition, we have

\displaystyle\sigma_{\min}\left((\bm{\Phi}_{m,j}^{(\ell)})^{\top}\bm{\Phi}_{m,j}^{(\ell)}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sigma_{\min}\left(\sum_{i=1}^{p}\bm{\phi}(s_{m,j,i}^{(\ell)},\bar{a}_{i})\bm{\phi}(s_{m,j,i}^{(\ell)},\bar{a}_{i})^{\top}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sigma_{\min}\left(\sum_{i=1}^{p}\mathbb{E}_{s\sim\mathcal{D}}\left[\bm{\phi}(s,\bar{a}_{i})\bm{\phi}(s,\bar{a}_{i})^{\top}\right]+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\bm{\phi}(s_{m,j,i}^{(\ell)},\bar{a}_{i})\bm{\phi}(s_{m,j,i}^{(\ell)},\bar{a}_{i})^{\top}-\sum_{i=1}^{p}\mathbb{E}_{s\sim\mathcal{D}}\left[\bm{\phi}(s,\bar{a}_{i})\bm{\phi}(s,\bar{a}_{i})^{\top}\right]\right)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\sigma_{\min}\left(\sum_{i=1}^{p}\mathbb{E}_{s\sim\mathcal{D}}\left[\bm{\phi}(s,\bar{a}_{i})\bm{\phi}(s,\bar{a}_{i})^{\top}\right]\right)-\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{p}\bm{\phi}(s_{m,j,i}^{(\ell)},\bar{a}_{i})\bm{\phi}(s_{m,j,i}^{(\ell)},\bar{a}_{i})^{\top}-\sum_{i=1}^{p}\mathbb{E}_{s\sim\mathcal{D}}\left[\bm{\phi}(s,\bar{a}_{i})\bm{\phi}(s,\bar{a}_{i})^{\top}\right]\right\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sigma_{\min}\left(\sum_{i=1}^{p}\mathbb{E}_{s\sim\hat{\mathcal{D}}}\left[\bm{\phi}(s,\bar{a}_{i})\bm{\phi}(s,\bar{a}_{i})^{\top}\right]+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\mathbb{E}_{s\sim\mathcal{D}}\left[\bm{\phi}(s,\bar{a}_{i})\bm{\phi}(s,\bar{a}_{i})^{\top}\right]-\sum_{i=1}^{p}\mathbb{E}_{s\sim\hat{\mathcal{D}}}\left[\bm{\phi}(s,\bar{a}_{i})\bm{\phi}(s,\bar{a}_{i})^{\top}\right]\right)

\displaystyle-\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{p}\bm{\phi}(s_{m,j,i}^{(\ell)},\bar{a}_{i})\bm{\phi}(s_{m,j,i}^{(\ell)},\bar{a}_{i})^{\top}-\sum_{i=1}^{p}\mathbb{E}_{s\sim\mathcal{D}}\left[\bm{\phi}(s,\bar{a}_{i})\bm{\phi}(s,\bar{a}_{i})^{\top}\right]\right\|

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\sigma_{\min}\left(\sum_{i=1}^{p}\mathbb{E}_{s\sim\hat{\mathcal{D}}}\left[\bm{\phi}(s,\bar{a}_{i})\bm{\phi}(s,\bar{a}_{i})^{\top}\right]\right)-\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{p}\mathbb{E}_{s\sim\mathcal{D}}\left[\bm{\phi}(s,\bar{a}_{i})\bm{\phi}(s,\bar{a}_{i})^{\top}\right]-\sum_{i=1}^{p}\mathbb{E}_{s\sim\hat{\mathcal{D}}}\left[\bm{\phi}(s,\bar{a}_{i})\bm{\phi}(s,\bar{a}_{i})^{\top}\right]\right\|

\displaystyle-\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{p}\bm{\phi}(s_{m,j,i}^{(\ell)},\bar{a}_{i})\bm{\phi}(s_{m,j,i}^{(\ell)},\bar{a}_{i})^{\top}-\sum_{i=1}^{p}\mathbb{E}_{s\sim\mathcal{D}}\left[\bm{\phi}(s,\bar{a}_{i})\bm{\phi}(s,\bar{a}_{i})^{\top}\right]\right\|

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\sigma_{\min}\left(\sum_{i=1}^{p}\mathbb{E}_{s\sim\hat{\mathcal{D}}}\left[\bm{\phi}(s,\bar{a}_{i})\bm{\phi}(s,\bar{a}_{i})^{\top}\right]\right)-\sum_{i=1}^{p}\left\|\mathbb{E}_{s\sim\mathcal{D}}\left[\bm{\phi}(s,\bar{a}_{i})\bm{\phi}(s,\bar{a}_{i})^{\top}\right]-\mathbb{E}_{s\sim\hat{\mathcal{D}}}\left[\bm{\phi}(s,\bar{a}_{i})\bm{\phi}(s,\bar{a}_{i})^{\top}\right]\right\|

\displaystyle-\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{p}\bm{\phi}(s_{m,j,i}^{(\ell)},\bar{a}_{i})\bm{\phi}(s_{m,j,i}^{(\ell)},\bar{a}_{i})^{\top}-\sum_{i=1}^{p}\mathbb{E}_{s\sim\mathcal{D}}\left[\bm{\phi}(s,\bar{a}_{i})\bm{\phi}(s,\bar{a}_{i})^{\top}\right]\right\|

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\sigma_{\min}\left(\sum_{i=1}^{p}\mathbb{E}_{s\sim\hat{\mathcal{D}}}\left[\bm{\phi}(s,\bar{a}_{i})\bm{\phi}(s,\bar{a}_{i})^{\top}\right]\right)-\frac{p\nu}{4(1+\zeta)}-\frac{p\nu}{4(1+\zeta)},

(21)

where the last inequality uses Lemmas
D.2
and
D.3
.

In the following, we analyze
\sigma_{\min}(\sum_{i=1}^{p}\mathbb{E}_{s\sim\hat{\mathcal{D}}}\left[\bm{\phi}(s,\bar{a}_{i})\bm{\phi}(s,\bar{a}_{i})^{\top}\right])
.
According to the guarantee of the rounding procedure
\mathtt{ROUND}
, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\left(\sum_{i=1}^{p}\mathbb{E}_{s\sim\hat{\mathcal{D}}}\left[\bm{\phi}(s,\bar{a}_{i})\bm{\phi}(s,\bar{a}_{i})^{\top}\right]\right)^{-1}\right\|\leq

\displaystyle(1+\zeta)\left\|\left(p\sum_{a\in\mathcal{A}}\lambda^{E}_{\hat{\mathcal{D}}}(a)\mathbb{E}_{s\sim\hat{\mathcal{D}}}\left[\bm{\phi}(s,a)\bm{\phi}(s,a)^{\top}\right]\right)^{-1}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(1+\zeta)\left\|\left(p\sum_{a\in\mathcal{A}}\lambda^{E}(a)\mathbb{E}_{s\sim\hat{\mathcal{D}}}\left[\bm{\phi}(s,a)\bm{\phi}(s,a)^{\top}\right]\right)^{-1}\right\|,

which implies that

\displaystyle\sigma_{\min}\left(\sum_{i=1}^{p}\mathbb{E}_{s\sim\hat{\mathcal{D}}}\left[\bm{\phi}(s,\bar{a}_{i})\bm{\phi}(s,\bar{a}_{i})^{\top}\right]\right)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\frac{p}{1+\zeta}\sigma_{\min}\left(\sum_{a\in\mathcal{A}}\lambda^{E}(a)\mathbb{E}_{s\sim\hat{\mathcal{D}}}\left[\bm{\phi}(s,a)\bm{\phi}(s,a)^{\top}\right]\right)

\displaystyle\geq
