\displaystyle\frac{p}{1+\zeta}\sigma_{\min}\Bigg(\sum_{a\in\mathcal{A}}\lambda^{E}(a)\mathbb{E}_{s\sim\mathcal{D}}\left[\bm{\phi}(s,a)\bm{\phi}(s,a)^{\top}\right]

\displaystyle+\sum_{a\in\mathcal{A}}\lambda^{E}(a)\mathbb{E}_{s\sim\hat{\mathcal{D}}}\left[\bm{\phi}(s,a)\bm{\phi}(s,a)^{\top}\right]-\sum_{a\in\mathcal{A}}\lambda^{E}(a)\mathbb{E}_{s\sim\mathcal{D}}\left[\bm{\phi}(s,a)\bm{\phi}(s,a)^{\top}\right]\Bigg)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\frac{p}{1+\zeta}\Bigg(\sigma_{\min}\left(\sum_{a\in\mathcal{A}}\lambda^{E}(a)\mathbb{E}_{s\sim\mathcal{D}}\left[\bm{\phi}(s,a)\bm{\phi}(s,a)^{\top}\right]\right)

\displaystyle-\left\|\sum_{a\in\mathcal{A}}\lambda^{E}(a)\mathbb{E}_{s\sim\hat{\mathcal{D}}}\left[\bm{\phi}(s,a)\bm{\phi}(s,a)^{\top}\right]-\sum_{a\in\mathcal{A}}\lambda^{E}(a)\mathbb{E}_{s\sim\mathcal{D}}\left[\bm{\phi}(s,a)\bm{\phi}(s,a)^{\top}\right]\right\|\Bigg)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\frac{p}{1+\zeta}\Bigg(\frac{1}{\rho_{\mathcal{D}}^{E}}-\sum_{a\in\mathcal{A}}\lambda^{E}(a)\left\|\mathbb{E}_{s\sim\hat{\mathcal{D}}}\left[\bm{\phi}(s,a)\bm{\phi}(s,a)^{\top}\right]-\mathbb{E}_{s\sim\mathcal{D}}\left[\bm{\phi}(s,a)\bm{\phi}(s,a)^{\top}\right]\right\|\Bigg)

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{(a)}}{\geq}

\displaystyle\frac{p}{1+\zeta}\left(\nu-\frac{\nu}{4(1+\zeta)}\right)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\frac{3p\nu}{4(1+\zeta)},

(22)

where inequality (a) uses Lemmas
D.1
and
D.2
.

Plugging Eq. (
22
) into Eq. (
21
), we have

\displaystyle\sigma_{\min}\left((\bm{\Phi}_{m,j}^{(\ell)})^{\top}\bm{\Phi}_{m,j}^{(\ell)}\right)\geq

\displaystyle\frac{3p\nu}{4(1+\zeta)}-\frac{p\nu}{4(1+\zeta)}-\frac{p\nu}{4(1+\zeta)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{p\nu}{4(1+\zeta)}.

(23)

Equations (
21
) and (
23
) show that if
T_{0}
and
p
are large enough to satisfy that
\left\|\mathbb{E}_{s\sim\hat{\mathcal{D}}}\left[\bm{\phi}(s,a)\bm{\phi}(s,a)^{\top}\right]-\mathbb{E}_{s\sim\mathcal{D}}\left[\bm{\phi}(s,a)\bm{\phi}(s,a)^{\top}\right]\right\|\leq\frac{\nu}{4(1+\zeta)}
for any
a\in\mathcal{A}
and
\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{p}\bm{\phi}(s_{m,j,i}^{(\ell)},\bar{a}_{i})\bm{\phi}(s_{m,j,i}^{(\ell)},\bar{a}_{i})^{\top}-\sum_{i=1}^{p}\mathbb{E}_{s\sim\mathcal{D}}\left[\bm{\phi}(s,\bar{a}_{i})\bm{\phi}(s,\bar{a}_{i})^{\top}\right]\right\|\leq\frac{p\nu}{4(1+\zeta)}
for any
m\in[M]
,
j\in[T]
and
\ell\in\{1,2\}
, respectively, then we have that
(\bm{\Phi}_{m,j}^{(\ell)})^{\top}\bm{\Phi}_{m,j}^{(\ell)}
is invertible.

Continuing with Eq. (
20
), we have

\displaystyle\left\|(\bm{\Phi}_{m,j}^{(\ell)})^{+}\right\|\leq 2\sqrt{\frac{(1+\zeta)}{p\nu}}.

∎

D.2
Global Feature Extractor Recovery with Stochastic Contexts

In subroutine
\mathtt{C\mathchar 45\relax FeatRecover}
, for any
m\in[M]
,
j\in[T]
,
i\in[p]
and
\ell\in\{1,2\}
, let
s^{(\ell)}_{m,j,i}
and
\eta^{(\ell)}_{m,j,i}
denote the random context and noise of the
\ell
-th sample on action
\bar{a}_{i}
in the
j
-th round for task
m
, respectively. Here, the superscript
\ell\in\{1,2\}
refers to the first sample (Line
4
in Algorithm
5
) or the second sample (Line
5
in Algorithm
5
) on an action
\bar{a}_{i}
.

In
\mathtt{C\mathchar 45\relax FeatRecover}
, for any
m\in[M]
,
j\in[T]
,
i\in[p]
and
\ell\in\{1,2\}
, let
\bm{\alpha}^{(\ell)}_{m,j}\leftarrow[\alpha^{(\ell)}_{m,j,1},\dots,\alpha^{(\ell)}_{m,j,p}]^{\top}
, and then,
\tilde{\bm{\theta}}^{(\ell)}_{m,j}=(\bm{\Phi}_{m,j}^{(\ell)})^{+}\bm{\alpha}^{(\ell)}_{m,j}
. Recall that
\bm{Z}=\frac{1}{MT}\sum_{m=1}^{M}\sum_{j=1}^{T}\tilde{\bm{\theta}}^{(1)}_{m,j}(\tilde{\bm{\theta}}^{(2)}_{m,j})^{\top}
.

Lemma D.5

(Expectation of
\bm{Z}
)
.

It holds that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\bm{Z}\right]=\frac{1}{M}\sum_{m=1}^{M}\bm{\theta}_{m}\bm{\theta}_{m}^{\top}.

Proof of Lemma
D.5
.

\bm{Z}
can be written as

\displaystyle\bm{Z}=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{MT}\sum_{m=1}^{M}\sum_{j=1}^{T}\tilde{\bm{\theta}}^{(1)}_{m,j}(\tilde{\bm{\theta}}^{(2)}_{m,j})^{\top}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{MT}\sum_{m=1}^{M}\sum_{j=1}^{T}(\bm{\Phi}_{m,j}^{(1)})^{+}\begin{bmatrix}\alpha^{(1)}_{m,j,1}\\
\vdots\\
\alpha^{(1)}_{m,j,p}\end{bmatrix}\left[\alpha^{(2)}_{m,j,1},\dots,\alpha^{(2)}_{m,j,p}\right]((\bm{\Phi}_{m,j}^{(2)})^{+})^{\top}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{MT}\sum_{m=1}^{M}\sum_{j=1}^{T}(\bm{\Phi}_{m,j}^{(1)})^{+}\Bigg(\!\!\begin{bmatrix}&\!\!\!\!\!\!\left(\bm{\phi}(s^{(1)}_{m,j,1},\bar{a}_{1})^{\top}\bm{\theta}_{m}\right)\left(\bm{\phi}(s^{(2)}_{m,j,1},\bar{a}_{1})^{\top}\bm{\theta}_{m}\right)&\!\!\!\!\!\!\dots\!\!\!\!\!\!&\left(\bm{\phi}(s^{(1)}_{m,j,1},\bar{a}_{1})^{\top}\bm{\theta}_{m}\right)\left(\bm{\phi}(s^{(2)}_{m,j,p},\bar{a}_{p})^{\top}\bm{\theta}_{m}\right)\\
&\!\!\!\!\!\!\dots&\!\!\!\!\!\!\dots\!\!\!\!\!\!&\dots\\
&\!\!\!\!\!\!\left(\bm{\phi}(s^{(1)}_{m,j,p},\bar{a}_{p})^{\top}\bm{\theta}_{m}\right)\left(\bm{\phi}(s^{(2)}_{m,j,1},\bar{a}_{1})^{\top}\bm{\theta}_{m}\right)&\!\!\!\!\!\!\dots\!\!\!\!\!\!&\left(\bm{\phi}(s^{(1)}_{m,j,p},\bar{a}_{p})^{\top}\bm{\theta}_{m}\right)\left(\bm{\phi}(s^{(2)}_{m,j,p},\bar{a}_{p})^{\top}\bm{\theta}_{m}\right)\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle\hskip-20.00003pt+\begin{bmatrix}&\!\!\!\!\!\!\bm{\phi}(s^{(1)}_{m,j,1},\bar{a}_{1})^{\top}\bm{\theta}_{m}\cdot\eta^{(2)}_{m,j,1}+\eta^{(1)}_{m,j,1}\cdot\bm{\phi}(s^{(2)}_{m,j,1},\bar{a}_{1})^{\top}\bm{\theta}_{m}&\!\!\!\!\!\dots\!\!\!\!\!&\bm{\phi}(s^{(1)}_{m,j,1},\bar{a}_{1})^{\top}\bm{\theta}_{m}\cdot\eta^{(2)}_{m,j,p}+\eta^{(1)}_{m,j,1}\cdot\bm{\phi}(s^{(2)}_{m,j,p},\bar{a}_{p})^{\top}\bm{\theta}_{m}\\
&\!\!\!\!\!\!\dots&\!\!\!\!\!\dots\!\!\!\!\!&\dots\\
&\!\!\!\!\!\!\bm{\phi}(s^{(1)}_{m,j,p},\bar{a}_{p})^{\top}\bm{\theta}_{m}\cdot\eta^{(2)}_{m,j,1}+\eta^{(1)}_{m,j,p}\cdot\bm{\phi}(s^{(2)}_{m,j,1},\bar{a}_{1})^{\top}\bm{\theta}_{m}&\!\!\!\!\!\dots\!\!\!\!\!&\bm{\phi}(s^{(1)}_{m,j,p},\bar{a}_{p})^{\top}\bm{\theta}_{m}\cdot\eta^{(2)}_{m,j,p}+\eta^{(1)}_{m,j,p}\cdot\bm{\phi}(s^{(2)}_{m,j,p},\bar{a}_{p})^{\top}\bm{\theta}_{m}\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle\hskip-20.00003pt+\begin{bmatrix}&\!\!\!\!\!\!\eta^{(1)}_{m,j,1}\cdot\eta^{(2)}_{m,j,1}&\dots&\eta^{(1)}_{m,j,1}\cdot\eta^{(2)}_{m,j,p}\\
&\!\!\!\!\!\!\dots&\dots&\dots\\
&\!\!\!\!\!\!\eta^{(1)}_{m,j,p}\cdot\eta^{(2)}_{m,j,1}&\dots&\eta^{(1)}_{m,j,p}\cdot\eta^{(2)}_{m,j,p}\end{bmatrix}\Bigg)((\bm{\Phi}_{m,j}^{(2)})^{+})^{\top}.

(24)

For any task
m\in[M]
,
j\in[T]
,
i\in[p]
, the sample on action
a_{i}
in the first round (i.e.,
s^{(1)}_{m,j,i}
and
\eta^{(1)}_{m,j,i}
) is independent of that in the second round (i.e.,
s^{(2)}_{m,j,i}
and
\eta^{(2)}_{m,j,i}
).
Hence, taking the expectation on
\bm{Z}
, we obtain

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\bm{Z}]=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{MT}\sum_{m=1}^{M}\sum_{j=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}\Bigg[(\bm{\Phi}_{m,j}^{(1)})^{+}\cdot

\displaystyle\begin{bmatrix}&\left(\bm{\phi}(s^{(1)}_{m,j,1},\bar{a}_{1})^{\top}\bm{\theta}_{m}\right)\left(\bm{\phi}(s^{(2)}_{m,j,1},\bar{a}_{1})^{\top}\bm{\theta}_{m}\right)&\!\!\!\!\!\dots\!\!\!\!\!&\left(\bm{\phi}(s^{(1)}_{m,j,1},\bar{a}_{1})^{\top}\bm{\theta}_{m}\right)\left(\bm{\phi}(s^{(2)}_{m,j,p},\bar{a}_{p})^{\top}\bm{\theta}_{m}\right)\\
&\dots&\!\!\!\!\!\dots\!\!\!\!\!&\dots\\
&\left(\bm{\phi}(s^{(1)}_{m,j,p},\bar{a}_{p})^{\top}\bm{\theta}_{m}\right)\left(\bm{\phi}(s^{(2)}_{m,j,1},\bar{a}_{1})^{\top}\bm{\theta}_{m}\right)&\!\!\!\!\!\dots\!\!\!\!\!&\left(\bm{\phi}(s^{(1)}_{m,j,p},\bar{a}_{p})^{\top}\bm{\theta}_{m}\right)\left(\bm{\phi}(s^{(2)}_{m,j,p},\bar{a}_{p})^{\top}\bm{\theta}_{m}\right)\end{bmatrix}((\bm{\Phi}_{m,j}^{(2)})^{+})^{\top}\Bigg]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{MT}\sum_{m=1}^{M}\sum_{j=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}\Bigg[((\bm{\Phi}_{m,j}^{(1)})^{\top}\bm{\Phi}_{m,j}^{(1)})^{-1}(\bm{\Phi}_{m,j}^{(1)})^{\top}\begin{bmatrix}\bm{\phi}(s^{(1)}_{m,j,1},\bar{a}_{1})^{\top}\bm{\theta}_{m}\\
\vdots\\
\bm{\phi}(s^{(1)}_{m,j,p},\bar{a}_{p})^{\top}\bm{\theta}_{m}\end{bmatrix}\left[\bm{\phi}(s^{(2)}_{m,j,1},\bar{a}_{1})^{\top}\bm{\theta}_{m},\dots,\bm{\phi}(s^{(2)}_{m,j,p},\bar{a}_{p})^{\top}\bm{\theta}_{m}\right]\cdot

\displaystyle\bm{\Phi}_{m,j}^{(2)}((\bm{\Phi}_{m,j}^{(2)})^{\top}\bm{\Phi}_{m,j}^{(2)})^{-1}\Bigg]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{MT}\sum_{m=1}^{M}\sum_{j=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}\left[((\bm{\Phi}_{m,j}^{(1)})^{\top}\bm{\Phi}_{m,j}^{(1)})^{-1}(\bm{\Phi}_{m,j}^{(1)})^{\top}\cdot\bm{\Phi}_{m,j}^{(1)}\bm{\theta}_{m}(\bm{\theta}_{m})^{\top}(\bm{\Phi}_{m,j}^{(2)})^{\top}\cdot\bm{\Phi}_{m,j}^{(2)}((\bm{\Phi}_{m,j}^{(2)})^{\top}\bm{\Phi}_{m,j}^{(2)})^{-1}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{MT}\sum_{m=1}^{M}\sum_{j=1}^{T}\bm{\theta}_{m}(\bm{\theta}_{m})^{\top}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{M}\sum_{m=1}^{M}\bm{\theta}_{m}\bm{\theta}_{m}^{\top}.

∎

Define event

\displaystyle\mathcal{G}:=\left\{\left\|\bm{Z}-\mathbb{E}[\bm{Z}]\right\|\leq\frac{256(1+\zeta)L_{\phi}L_{\theta}\log\left(\frac{50d}{\delta}\right)}{\nu\sqrt{MT}}\log\left(\frac{100pMT}{\delta}\right)\right\}.

Lemma D.6

(Concentration of
Z
)
.

Suppose that
\mathcal{K}\cap\mathcal{L}
holds. Then, it holds that

\displaystyle\Pr\left[\mathcal{G}\right]\geq 1-\frac{\delta}{5}.

Proof of Lemma
D.6
.

Define the following matrices:

\displaystyle\bm{D}_{m,j}

\displaystyle:=\frac{1}{MT}(\bm{\Phi}_{m,j}^{(1)})^{+}\cdot

\displaystyle\begin{bmatrix}&\left(\bm{\phi}(s^{(1)}_{m,j,1},\bar{a}_{1})^{\top}\bm{\theta}_{m}\right)\left(\bm{\phi}(s^{(2)}_{m,j,1},\bar{a}_{1})^{\top}\bm{\theta}_{m}\right)&\dots&\left(\bm{\phi}(s^{(1)}_{m,j,1},\bar{a}_{1})^{\top}\bm{\theta}_{m}\right)\left(\bm{\phi}(s^{(2)}_{m,j,p},\bar{a}_{p})^{\top}\bm{\theta}_{m}\right)\\
&\dots&\dots&\dots\\
&\left(\bm{\phi}(s^{(1)}_{m,j,p},\bar{a}_{p})^{\top}\bm{\theta}_{m}\right)\left(\bm{\phi}(s^{(2)}_{m,j,1},\bar{a}_{1})^{\top}\bm{\theta}_{m}\right)&\dots&\left(\bm{\phi}(s^{(1)}_{m,j,p},\bar{a}_{p})^{\top}\bm{\theta}_{m}\right)\left(\bm{\phi}(s^{(2)}_{m,j,p},\bar{a}_{p})^{\top}\bm{\theta}_{m}\right)\end{bmatrix}((\bm{\Phi}_{m,j}^{(2)})^{+})^{\top}

\displaystyle\bm{D}

\displaystyle:=\sum_{m=1}^{M}\sum_{j=1}^{T}\bm{D}_{m,j}

\displaystyle\bm{E}_{m,j}

\displaystyle:=\frac{1}{MT}(\bm{\Phi}_{m,j}^{(1)})^{+}\cdot

\displaystyle\hskip-18.00005pt\begin{bmatrix}&\!\!\!\!\!\bm{\phi}(s^{(1)}_{m,j,1},\bar{a}_{1})^{\top}\bm{\theta}_{m}\cdot\eta^{(2)}_{m,j,1}+\eta^{(1)}_{m,j,1}\cdot\bm{\phi}(s^{(2)}_{m,j,1},\bar{a}_{1})^{\top}\bm{\theta}_{m}&\!\!\!\!\!\dots\!\!\!\!\!&\bm{\phi}(s^{(1)}_{m,j,1},\bar{a}_{1})^{\top}\bm{\theta}_{m}\cdot\eta^{(2)}_{m,j,p}+\eta^{(1)}_{m,j,1}\cdot\bm{\phi}(s^{(2)}_{m,j,p},\bar{a}_{p})^{\top}\bm{\theta}_{m}\\
&\!\!\!\!\!\dots&\!\!\!\!\!\dots\!\!\!\!\!&\dots\\
&\!\!\!\!\!\bm{\phi}(s^{(1)}_{m,j,p},\bar{a}_{p})^{\top}\bm{\theta}_{m}\cdot\eta^{(2)}_{m,j,1}+\eta^{(1)}_{m,j,p}\cdot\bm{\phi}(s^{(2)}_{m,j,1},\bar{a}_{1})^{\top}\bm{\theta}_{m}&\!\!\!\!\!\dots\!\!\!\!\!&\bm{\phi}(s^{(1)}_{m,j,p},\bar{a}_{p})^{\top}\bm{\theta}_{m}\cdot\eta^{(2)}_{m,j,p}+\eta^{(1)}_{m,j,p}\cdot\bm{\phi}(s^{(2)}_{m,j,p},\bar{a}_{p})^{\top}\bm{\theta}_{m}\end{bmatrix}\!\cdot

\displaystyle((\bm{\Phi}_{m,j}^{(2)})^{+})^{\top}

\displaystyle\bm{E}

\displaystyle:=\sum_{m=1}^{M}\sum_{j=1}^{T}\bm{E}_{m,j}

\displaystyle\bm{F}_{m,j}

\displaystyle:=\frac{1}{MT}(\bm{\Phi}_{m,j}^{(1)})^{+}\begin{bmatrix}&\eta^{(1)}_{m,j,1}\cdot\eta^{(2)}_{m,j,1}&\dots&\eta^{(1)}_{m,j,1}\cdot\eta^{(2)}_{m,j,p}\\
&\dots&\dots&\dots\\
&\eta^{(1)}_{m,j,p}\cdot\eta^{(2)}_{m,j,1}&\dots&\eta^{(1)}_{m,j,p}\cdot\eta^{(2)}_{m,j,p}\end{bmatrix}((\bm{\Phi}_{m,j}^{(2)})^{+})^{\top}

\displaystyle\bm{F}

\displaystyle:=\sum_{m=1}^{M}\sum_{j=1}^{T}\bm{F}_{m,j}

From Eq. (
24
), we can bound
\|\bm{Z}-\mathbb{E}[\bm{Z}]\|
as

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{Z}-\mathbb{E}[\bm{Z}]\right\|\leq

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{D}-\mathbb{E}[\bm{D}]\right\|+\left\|\bm{E}-\mathbb{E}[\bm{E}]\right\|+\left\|\bm{F}-\mathbb{E}[\bm{F}]\right\|.

(25)

Similar to the proof of Lemma
C.3
, in order to use the truncated matrix Bernstein inequality (Lemma
E.2
), we define the truncated noise and some truncated matrices as follows.

Let
R>0
be a truncation parameter of noises which will be chosen later. For any
m\in[M]
,
j\in[T]
,
i\in[p]
and
\ell\in\{1,2\}
, let
\tilde{\eta}^{(\ell)}_{m,j,i}=\eta^{(\ell)}_{m,j,i}\mathbbm{1}\{|\eta^{(\ell)}_{m,j,i}|\leq R\}
denote the truncated noise. Furthermore, we define the following matrices with truncated noises:

\displaystyle\tilde{\bm{E}}_{m,j}

\displaystyle:=\frac{1}{MT}(\bm{\Phi}_{m,j}^{(1)})^{+}\cdot

\displaystyle\hskip-18.00005pt\begin{bmatrix}&\!\!\!\!\!\bm{\phi}(s^{(1)}_{m,j,1},\bar{a}_{1})^{\top}\bm{\theta}_{m}\cdot\tilde{\eta}^{(2)}_{m,j,1}+\tilde{\eta}^{(1)}_{m,j,1}\cdot\bm{\phi}(s^{(2)}_{m,j,1},\bar{a}_{1})^{\top}\bm{\theta}_{m}&\!\!\!\!\!\dots\!\!\!\!\!&\bm{\phi}(s^{(1)}_{m,j,1},\bar{a}_{1})^{\top}\bm{\theta}_{m}\cdot\tilde{\eta}^{(2)}_{m,j,p}+\tilde{\eta}^{(1)}_{m,j,1}\cdot\bm{\phi}(s^{(2)}_{m,j,p},\bar{a}_{p})^{\top}\bm{\theta}_{m}\\
&\!\!\!\!\!\dots&\!\!\!\!\!\dots\!\!\!\!\!&\dots\\
&\!\!\!\!\!\bm{\phi}(s^{(1)}_{m,j,p},\bar{a}_{p})^{\top}\bm{\theta}_{m}\cdot\tilde{\eta}^{(2)}_{m,j,1}+\tilde{\eta}^{(1)}_{m,j,p}\cdot\bm{\phi}(s^{(2)}_{m,j,1},\bar{a}_{1})^{\top}\bm{\theta}_{m}&\!\!\!\!\!\dots\!\!\!\!\!&\bm{\phi}(s^{(1)}_{m,j,p},\bar{a}_{p})^{\top}\bm{\theta}_{m}\cdot\tilde{\eta}^{(2)}_{m,j,p}+\tilde{\eta}^{(1)}_{m,j,p}\cdot\bm{\phi}(s^{(2)}_{m,j,p},\bar{a}_{p})^{\top}\bm{\theta}_{m}\end{bmatrix}\!\cdot

\displaystyle((\bm{\Phi}_{m,j}^{(2)})^{+})^{\top}

\displaystyle\tilde{\bm{E}}

\displaystyle:=\sum_{m=1}^{M}\sum_{j=1}^{T}\tilde{\bm{E}}_{m,j}

\displaystyle\tilde{\bm{F}}_{m,j}

\displaystyle:=\frac{1}{MT}(\bm{\Phi}_{m,j}^{(1)})^{+}\begin{bmatrix}&\tilde{\eta}^{(1)}_{m,j,1}\cdot\tilde{\eta}^{(2)}_{m,j,1}&\dots&\tilde{\eta}^{(1)}_{m,j,1}\cdot\tilde{\eta}^{(2)}_{m,j,p}\\
&\dots&\dots&\dots\\
&\tilde{\eta}^{(1)}_{m,j,p}\cdot\tilde{\eta}^{(2)}_{m,j,1}&\dots&\tilde{\eta}^{(1)}_{m,j,p}\cdot\tilde{\eta}^{(2)}_{m,j,p}\end{bmatrix}((\bm{\Phi}_{m,j}^{(2)})^{+})^{\top}

\displaystyle\tilde{\bm{F}}

\displaystyle:=\sum_{m=1}^{M}\sum_{j=1}^{T}\tilde{\bm{F}}_{m,j}

Recall that from Lemma
D.4
, we have that for any
m\in[M]
,
j\in[T]
and
\ell\in\{1,2\}
,
\|(\bm{\Phi}_{m,j}^{(\ell)})^{+}\|\leq 2\sqrt{\frac{(1+\zeta)}{p\nu}}
. Let
B_{\Phi}:=2\sqrt{\frac{(1+\zeta)}{p\nu}}
.

We first analyze
\|\bm{D}-\mathbb{E}[\bm{D}]\|
. Since
|\bm{\phi}(s^{(\ell)}_{m,j,i},\bar{a}_{i})^{\top}\bm{\theta}_{m}|\leq L_{\phi}L_{\theta}
for any
m\in[M]
,
j\in[T]
,
i\in[p]
and
\ell\in\{1,2\}
, we have that
\|\bm{D}_{m,j}\|\leq\frac{1}{MT}\cdot pL_{\phi}L_{\theta}B_{\Phi}^{2}
and
\|\sum_{m=1}^{M}\sum_{j=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}[\bm{D}^{2}_{m,j}]\|\leq MT\cdot\frac{1}{M^{2}T^{2}}\cdot p^{2}L_{\phi}^{2}L_{\theta}^{2}B_{\Phi}^{4}=\frac{1}{MT}\cdot p^{2}L_{\phi}^{2}L_{\theta}^{2}B_{\Phi}^{4}
for any
m\in[M]
and
j\in[T]
.

Let
\delta^{\prime}\in(0,1)
be a confidence parameter which will be chosen later.
Using the matrix Bernstein inequality (Lemma
E.2
), we have that with probability at least
1-\delta^{\prime}
,

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{D}-\mathbb{E}[\bm{D}]\right\|\leq

\displaystyle 4\sqrt{\frac{p^{2}L_{\phi}^{2}L_{\theta}^{2}B_{\Phi}^{4}\log\left(\frac{2d}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)}{MT}}+\frac{4pL_{\phi}L_{\theta}B_{\Phi}^{2}\log\left(\frac{2d}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)}{MT}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{8\cdot 4pL_{\phi}L_{\theta}B_{\Phi}^{2}\log\left(\frac{2d}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)}{\sqrt{MT}}.

(26)

Next, we bound
\|\bm{E}-\mathbb{E}[\bm{E}]\|
.
Since
|\bm{\phi}(s^{(\ell)}_{m,j,i},\bar{a}_{i})^{\top}\bm{\theta}_{m}|\leq L_{\phi}L_{\theta}
and
|\tilde{\eta}^{(\ell)}_{m,j,i}|\leq R
for any
m\in[M]
,
j\in[T]
,
i\in[p]
and
\ell\in\{1,2\}
, we have that
\|\tilde{\bm{E}}_{m,j}\|\leq\frac{1}{MT}\cdot 2pRL_{\phi}L_{\theta}B_{\Phi}^{2}
and
\left\|\sum_{m=1}^{M}\sum_{j=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}[\tilde{\bm{E}}_{m,j}^{2}]\right\|\leq\frac{1}{MT}\cdot 4p^{2}R^{2}L_{\phi}^{2}L_{\theta}^{2}B_{\Phi}^{4}
for any
m\in[M]
and
j\in[T]
.

Since
\eta^{(\ell)}_{m,j,i}
is 1-sub-Gaussian for any
m\in[M]
,
j\in[T]
,
i\in[p]
and
\ell\in\{1,2\}
, using a union bound over
i\in[p]
and
\ell\in\{1,2\}
, we have that for any
m\in[M]
and
j\in[T]
, with probability at least
1-4p\exp(-\frac{R^{2}}{2})
,
|\eta^{(\ell)}_{m,j,i}|\leq R
for all
i\in[p]
and
\ell\in\{1,2\}
, and thus,
\|\bm{E}_{m,j}\|\leq\frac{1}{MT}\cdot 2pRL_{\phi}L_{\theta}B_{\Phi}^{2}
. Then, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbb{E}[\bm{E}_{m,j}]-\mathbb{E}[\tilde{\bm{E}}_{m,j}]\right\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbb{E}\left[\bm{E}_{m,j}\cdot\mathbbm{1}\left\{\left\|\bm{E}_{m,j}\right\|\geq\frac{2pRL_{\phi}L_{\theta}B_{\Phi}^{2}}{MT}\right\}\right]\right\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\bm{E}_{m,j}\right\|\cdot\mathbbm{1}\left\{\left\|\bm{E}_{m,j}\right\|\geq\frac{2pRL_{\phi}L_{\theta}B_{\Phi}^{2}}{MT}\right\}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{2pRL_{\phi}L_{\theta}B_{\Phi}^{2}}{MT}\!\cdot\!\mathbbm{1}\left\{\left\|\bm{E}_{m,j}\right\|\!\geq\!\frac{2pRL_{\phi}L_{\theta}B_{\Phi}^{2}}{MT}\right\}\right]\!+\!\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\left\|\bm{E}_{m,j}\right\|\!-\!\frac{2pRL_{\phi}L_{\theta}B_{\Phi}^{2}}{MT}\right)\!\cdot\!\mathbbm{1}\left\{\left\|\bm{E}_{m,j}\right\|\!\geq\!\frac{2pRL_{\phi}L_{\theta}B_{\Phi}^{2}}{MT}\right\}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{2pRL_{\phi}L_{\theta}B_{\Phi}^{2}}{MT}\cdot\Pr\left[\left\|\bm{E}_{m,j}\right\|\geq\frac{2pRL_{\phi}L_{\theta}B_{\Phi}^{2}}{MT}\right]+\int_{0}^{\infty}\Pr\left[\left\|\bm{E}_{m,j}\right\|-\frac{2pRL_{\phi}L_{\theta}B_{\Phi}^{2}}{MT}>x\right]dx

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{2pRL_{\phi}L_{\theta}B_{\Phi}^{2}}{MT}\cdot 4p\cdot\exp\left(-\frac{R^{2}}{2}\right)+\frac{2pL_{\phi}L_{\theta}B_{\Phi}^{2}}{MT}\int_{R}^{\infty}\Pr\left[\left\|\bm{E}_{m,j}\right\|>\frac{2pL_{\phi}L_{\theta}B_{\Phi}^{2}y}{MT}\right]dy

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{2pRL_{\phi}L_{\theta}B_{\Phi}^{2}}{MT}\cdot 4p\cdot\exp\left(-\frac{R^{2}}{2}\right)+\frac{2pL_{\phi}L_{\theta}B_{\Phi}^{2}}{MT}\int_{R}^{\infty}4p\exp\left(-\frac{y^{2}}{2}\right)dy

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{2pRL_{\phi}L_{\theta}B_{\Phi}^{2}}{MT}\cdot 4p\cdot\exp\left(-\frac{R^{2}}{2}\right)+\frac{2pL_{\phi}L_{\theta}B_{\Phi}^{2}}{MT}\cdot 4p\cdot\frac{1}{R}\cdot\exp\left(-\frac{R^{2}}{2}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{2pL_{\phi}L_{\theta}B_{\Phi}^{2}}{MT}\cdot 4p\cdot\left(R+\frac{1}{R}\right)\exp\left(-\frac{R^{2}}{2}\right).

Using the truncated matrix Bernstein inequality (Lemma
E.2
) with
n=MT
,
R=\sqrt{2\log\left(\frac{4pMT}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)}
,
U=\frac{2pL_{\phi}L_{\theta}B_{\Phi}^{2}\sqrt{2\log\left(\frac{4pMT}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)}}{MT}
,
\sigma^{2}=\frac{(2pL_{\phi}L_{\theta}B_{\Phi}^{2}\sqrt{2\log\left(\frac{4pMT}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)})^{2}}{MT}
,
\tau=4\sqrt{\frac{(2pL_{\phi}L_{\theta}B_{\Phi}^{2}\sqrt{2\log\left(\frac{4pMT}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)})^{2}\cdot\log\left(\frac{2d}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)}{MT}}+\frac{4\cdot 2pL_{\phi}L_{\theta}B_{\Phi}^{2}\sqrt{2\log\left(\frac{4pMT}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)}\cdot\log\left(\frac{2d}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)}{MT}
and
\Delta=\frac{2pL_{\phi}L_{\theta}B_{\Phi}^{2}\cdot 2\sqrt{2\log\left(\frac{4pMT}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)}}{MT}\cdot\frac{\delta^{\prime}}{MT}
, we have that with probability at least
1-2\delta^{\prime}
,

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{E}-\mathbb{E}[\bm{E}]\right\|\leq\frac{8\cdot 2pL_{\phi}L_{\theta}B_{\Phi}^{2}\sqrt{2\log\left(\frac{4pMT}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)}\cdot\log\left(\frac{2d}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)}{\sqrt{MT}}.

(27)

Now we investigate
\left\|\bm{F}-\mathbb{E}[\bm{F}]\right\|
. Since
|\tilde{\eta}^{(\ell)}_{m,j,i}|\leq R
for any
m\in[M]
,
j\in[T]
,
i\in[p]
and
\ell\in\{1,2\}
, we have that
\|\tilde{\bm{F}}_{m,j}\|\leq\frac{1}{MT}\cdot pR^{2}B_{\Phi}^{2}
and
\left\|\sum_{m=1}^{M}\sum_{j=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}\left[\tilde{\bm{F}}_{m,j}^{2}\right]\right\|\leq\frac{1}{MT}\cdot p^{2}R^{4}B_{\Phi}^{4}
.

Recall that for any
m\in[M]
and
j\in[T]
, with probability at least
1-4p\exp(-\frac{R^{2}}{2})
,
|\eta^{(\ell)}_{m,j,i}|\leq R
for all
i\in[p]
and
\ell\in\{1,2\}
, and thus,
\|\bm{F}_{m,j}\|\leq\frac{1}{MT}\cdot pB_{\Phi}^{2}R^{2}
. Then, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbb{E}[\bm{F}_{m,j}]-\mathbb{E}[\tilde{\bm{F}}_{m,j}]\right\|\leq

\displaystyle\left\|\mathbb{E}\left[\bm{F}_{m,j}\cdot\mathbbm{1}\left\{\left\|\bm{F}_{m,j}\right\|\geq\frac{pB_{\Phi}^{2}R^{2}}{MT}\right\}\right]\right\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\bm{F}_{m,j}\right\|\cdot\mathbbm{1}\left\{\left\|\bm{F}_{m,j}\right\|\geq\frac{pB_{\Phi}^{2}R^{2}}{MT}\right\}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{pB_{\Phi}^{2}R^{2}}{MT}\cdot\mathbbm{1}\left\{\left\|\bm{F}_{m,j}\right\|\geq\frac{pB_{\Phi}^{2}R^{2}}{MT}\right\}\right]+\left[\left(\left\|\bm{F}_{m,j}\right\|-\frac{pB_{\Phi}^{2}R^{2}}{MT}\right)\cdot\mathbbm{1}\left\{\left\|\bm{F}_{m,j}\right\|\geq\frac{pB_{\Phi}^{2}R^{2}}{MT}\right\}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{pB_{\Phi}^{2}R^{2}}{MT}\cdot\Pr\left[\left\|\bm{F}_{m,j}\right\|\geq\frac{pB_{\Phi}^{2}R^{2}}{MT}\right]+\int_{0}^{\infty}\Pr\left[\left\|\bm{F}_{m,j}\right\|-\frac{pB_{\Phi}^{2}R^{2}}{MT}>x\right]dx

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{pB_{\Phi}^{2}R^{2}}{MT}\cdot 4p\cdot\exp\left(-\frac{R^{2}}{2}\right)+\frac{2pB_{\Phi}^{2}}{MT}\int_{R}^{\infty}\bm{y}\cdot\Pr\left[\left\|\bm{F}_{m,j}\right\|>\frac{pB_{\Phi}^{2}y^{2}}{MT}\right]dy

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{pB_{\Phi}^{2}R^{2}}{MT}\cdot 4p\cdot\exp\left(-\frac{R^{2}}{2}\right)+\frac{2pB_{\Phi}^{2}}{MT}\int_{R}^{\infty}\bm{y}\cdot 4p\exp\left(-\frac{y^{2}}{2}\right)dy

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{pB_{\Phi}^{2}R^{2}}{MT}\cdot 4p\cdot\exp\left(-\frac{R^{2}}{2}\right)+\frac{2pB_{\Phi}^{2}}{MT}\cdot 4p\cdot\exp\left(-\frac{R^{2}}{2}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{pB_{\Phi}^{2}}{MT}\cdot 4p\cdot\left(R^{2}+2\right)\exp\left(-\frac{R^{2}}{2}\right).

Using the truncated matrix Bernstein inequality (Lemma
E.2
) with
n=MT
,
R=\sqrt{2\log\left(\frac{4pMT}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)}
,
U=\frac{pB_{\Phi}^{2}\cdot 2\log\left(\frac{4pMT}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)}{MT}
,
\sigma^{2}=\frac{(pB_{\Phi}^{2}\cdot 2\log\left(\frac{4pMT}{\delta^{\prime}}\right))^{2}}{MT}
,
\tau=4\sqrt{\frac{(pB_{\Phi}^{2}\cdot 2\log\left(\frac{4pMT}{\delta^{\prime}}\right))^{2}\cdot\log\left(\frac{2d}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)}{MT}}+\frac{4\cdot pB_{\Phi}^{2}\cdot 2\log\left(\frac{4pMT}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)\cdot\log\left(\frac{2d}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)}{MT}
and
\Delta=\frac{pB_{\Phi}^{2}\cdot 2\cdot 2\log\left(\frac{4pMT}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)}{MT}\cdot\frac{\delta^{\prime}}{MT}
, we have that with probability at least
1-2\delta^{\prime}
,

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{F}-\mathbb{E}\left[\bm{F}\right]\right\|\leq\frac{8\cdot pB_{\Phi}^{2}\cdot 2\log\left(\frac{4pMT}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)\cdot\log\left(\frac{2d}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)}{\sqrt{MT}}.

(28)

Plugging Eqs. (
26
)-(
28
) into Eq. (
25
), we have that with probability at least
1-5\delta^{\prime}
,

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{Z}-\mathbb{E}[\bm{Z}]\right\|\leq

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{D}-\mathbb{E}\left[\bm{D}\right]\right\|+\left\|\bm{E}-\mathbb{E}\left[\bm{E}\right]\right\|+\left\|\bm{F}-\mathbb{E}\left[\bm{F}\right]\right\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{64pL_{\phi}L_{\theta}B_{\Phi}^{2}\log\left(\frac{4pMT}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)\log\left(\frac{2d}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)}{\sqrt{MT}}.

Let
\delta^{\prime}=\frac{\delta}{25}
. Recall that
B_{\Phi}:=2\sqrt{\frac{(1+\zeta)}{p\nu}}
. Then, we obtain that with probability at least
1-\frac{\delta}{5}
,

\displaystyle\left\|\bm{Z}-\mathbb{E}[\bm{Z}]\right\|\leq\frac{256(1+\zeta)L_{\phi}L_{\theta}\log\left(\frac{50d}{\delta}\right)}{\nu\sqrt{MT}}\log\left(\frac{100pMT}{\delta}\right),

which implies that

\Pr[\mathcal{G}]\geq 1-\frac{\delta}{5}
.
∎

According to Assumption
3.1
, there exists an absolute constant
c_{0}
which satisfies that
\sigma_{\min}(\frac{1}{M}\sum_{m=1}^{M}\bm{w}_{m}\bm{w}_{m}^{\top})=\sigma_{\min}(\frac{1}{M}\sum_{m=1}^{M}\bm{\theta}_{m}\bm{\theta}_{m}^{\top})\geq\frac{c_{0}}{k}
.

Lemma D.7

(Concentration of
\hat{\bm{B}}
)
.

Suppose that event
\mathcal{G}
holds. Then,

\displaystyle\left\|\hat{\bm{B}}_{\bot}^{\top}\bm{B}\right\|\leq\frac{2048(1+\zeta)kL_{\phi}L_{\theta}\log\left(\frac{50d}{\delta}\right)}{c_{0}\nu\sqrt{MT}}\log\left(\frac{135(1+\zeta)dL_{\phi}MT}{\nu\delta}\right).

Furthermore, if

\displaystyle T=\left\lceil\frac{68\cdot 2048^{2}\cdot 96^{2}(1+\zeta)^{2}k^{4}L_{\phi}^{4}L_{\theta}^{2}L_{w}^{2}}{c_{0}^{2}\nu^{2}\varepsilon^{2}M}\log^{6}\left(\frac{2048\cdot 135\cdot 96\cdot 50\cdot 5(1+\zeta)^{2}k^{2}d^{2}L_{\phi}^{3}L_{\theta}L_{w}N}{c_{0}\nu^{2}\delta^{3}\varepsilon}\right)\right\rceil,

(29)

we have

\displaystyle\left\|\hat{\bm{B}}_{t,\bot}^{\top}\bm{B}\right\|\leq\frac{\varepsilon}{96k\log\left(\frac{5N}{\delta}\right)L_{\phi}L_{w}}.

Proof of Lemma
D.7
.

First, we have that
\sigma_{k}(\mathbb{E}[\bm{Z}])-\sigma_{k+1}(\mathbb{E}[\bm{Z}])=\sigma_{\min}(\frac{1}{M}\sum_{m=1}^{M}\bm{\theta}_{m}\bm{\theta}_{m}^{\top})\geq\frac{c_{0}}{k}
. Let
p:=\lceil 32^{2}(1+\zeta)^{2}\nu^{-2}L_{\phi}^{4}\log^{2}\left(\frac{40dMT}{\delta}\right)\rceil
.
Then, using the Davis-Kahan sin
\theta
Theorem
(
Bhatia 2013
)
and letting
T_{t}
be large enough to satisfy that
\left\|\bm{Z}-\mathbb{E}[\bm{Z}]\right\|\leq\frac{c_{0}}{2k}
, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\hat{\bm{B}}_{t,\bot}^{\top}\bm{B}\right\|\leq

\displaystyle\frac{\left\|\bm{Z}-\mathbb{E}[\bm{Z}]\right\|}{\sigma_{k}(\mathbb{E}[\bm{Z}])-\sigma_{k+1}(\mathbb{E}[\bm{Z}])-\left\|\bm{Z}-\mathbb{E}[\bm{Z}]\right\|}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{2k}{c_{0}}\left\|\bm{Z}-\mathbb{E}[\bm{Z}]\right\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{512(1+\zeta)kL_{\phi}L_{\theta}\log\left(\frac{50d}{\delta}\right)}{c_{0}\nu\sqrt{MT}}\log\left(\frac{100pMT}{\delta}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{512(1+\zeta)kL_{\phi}L_{\theta}\log\left(\frac{50d}{\delta}\right)}{c_{0}\nu\sqrt{MT}}\log\left(\frac{100MT}{\delta}\cdot\frac{2\cdot 32^{2}(1+\zeta)^{2}L_{\phi}^{4}}{\nu^{2}}\log^{2}\left(\frac{40dMT}{\delta}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{512(1+\zeta)kL_{\phi}L_{\theta}\log\left(\frac{50d}{\delta}\right)}{c_{0}\nu\sqrt{MT}}\log\left(\frac{2\cdot 100\cdot 32^{2}\cdot 40^{2}(1+\zeta)^{2}d^{2}L_{\phi}^{4}M^{3}T^{3}}{\nu^{2}\delta^{3}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{2048(1+\zeta)kL_{\phi}L_{\theta}\log\left(\frac{50d}{\delta}\right)}{c_{0}\nu\sqrt{MT}}\log\left(\frac{135(1+\zeta)dL_{\phi}MT}{\nu\delta}\right).

Using Lemma
E.3
with
A=2048(1+\zeta)kc_{0}^{-1}\nu^{-1}L_{\phi}L_{\theta}\log\left(\frac{50d}{\delta}\right)
,
B=\frac{135(1+\zeta)dL_{\phi}}{\nu\delta}
and
\kappa=\frac{\varepsilon}{96k\log\left(\frac{5N}{\delta}\right)L_{\phi}L_{w}}
, we have that if

\displaystyle MT\geq

\displaystyle\frac{68\cdot 2048^{2}\cdot 96^{2}(1+\zeta)^{2}k^{4}L_{\phi}^{4}L_{\theta}^{2}L_{w}^{2}}{c_{0}^{2}\nu^{2}\varepsilon^{2}}\cdot

\displaystyle\log^{2}\left(\frac{50d}{\delta}\right)\log^{2}\left(\frac{5N}{\delta}\right)\log^{2}\left(\frac{2048\cdot 135\cdot 96(1+\zeta)^{2}k^{2}dL_{\phi}^{3}L_{\theta}L_{w}}{c_{0}\nu^{2}\delta\varepsilon}\log\left(\frac{50d}{\delta}\right)\log\left(\frac{5N}{\delta}\right)\right),

then
\left\|\hat{\bm{B}}_{t,\bot}^{\top}\bm{B}\right\|\leq\frac{\varepsilon}{96k\log\left(\frac{5N}{\delta}\right)L_{\phi}L_{w}}
.

Further enlarging
MT
, if

\displaystyle MT\geq\frac{68\cdot 2048^{2}\cdot 96^{2}(1+\zeta)^{2}k^{4}L_{\phi}^{4}L_{\theta}^{2}L_{w}^{2}}{c_{0}^{2}\nu^{2}\varepsilon^{2}}\log^{6}\left(\frac{2048\cdot 135\cdot 96\cdot 50\cdot 5(1+\zeta)^{2}k^{2}d^{2}L_{\phi}^{3}L_{\theta}L_{w}N}{c_{0}\nu^{2}\delta^{3}\varepsilon}\right),

then

\displaystyle\left\|\hat{\bm{B}}_{t,\bot}^{\top}\bm{B}\right\|\leq\frac{\varepsilon}{96k\log\left(\frac{5N}{\delta}\right)L_{\phi}L_{w}}.

∎

D.3
Estimation with Low-dimensional Representations

Lemma D.8
.

In subroutine
\mathtt{EstLowRep}
(Algorithm
6
), for any
m\in[M]
and
t>0
, we have

\displaystyle\log\left(\frac{\det\left(\gamma I+\sum_{\tau=1}^{t}\hat{\bm{B}}^{\top}\bm{\phi}(s_{m,\tau},a_{m,\tau})\bm{\phi}(s_{m,\tau},a_{m,\tau})^{\top}\hat{\bm{B}}\right)}{\det\left(\gamma I\right)}\right)\leq k\log\left(1+\frac{t}{\gamma k}\right).

Proof of Lemma
D.8
.

This proof uses a similar idea as Lemma 11 in
(
Abbasi-Yadkori et al. 2011
)
.

It holds that

\displaystyle\log\left(\frac{\det\left(\gamma I+\sum_{\tau=1}^{t}\hat{\bm{B}}^{\top}\bm{\phi}(s_{m,\tau},a_{m,\tau})\bm{\phi}(s_{m,\tau},a_{m,\tau})^{\top}\hat{\bm{B}}\right)}{\det\left(\gamma I\right)}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\log\left(\frac{\left(\frac{\textup{Trace}\left(\gamma I+\sum_{\tau=1}^{t}\hat{\bm{B}}^{\top}\bm{\phi}(s_{m,\tau},a_{m,\tau})\bm{\phi}(s_{m,\tau},a_{m,\tau})^{\top}\hat{\bm{B}}\right)}{k}\right)^{k}}{\gamma^{k}}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle k\log\left(\frac{\textup{Trace}\left(\gamma I\right)+\sum_{\tau=1}^{t}\textup{Trace}\left(\hat{\bm{B}}^{\top}\bm{\phi}(s_{m,\tau},a_{m,\tau})\bm{\phi}(s_{m,\tau},a_{m,\tau})^{\top}\hat{\bm{B}}\right)}{\gamma k}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle k\log\left(\frac{\gamma k+\sum_{\tau=1}^{t}\left\|\hat{\bm{B}}^{\top}\bm{\phi}(s_{m,\tau},a_{m,\tau})\right\|^{2}}{\gamma k}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle k\log\left(1+\frac{t}{\gamma k}\right).

∎

Lemma D.9
.

In subroutine
\mathtt{EstLowRep}
(Algorithm
6
), for any
m\in[M]
and
t\geq 0
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{s\sim\mathcal{D}}\left[\max_{a\in\mathcal{A}}\left\|\hat{\bm{B}}^{\top}\bm{\phi}(s,a)\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{m,t}^{-1}}\right]\geq\mathbb{E}_{s\sim\mathcal{D}}\left[\max_{a\in\mathcal{A}}\left\|\hat{\bm{B}}^{\top}\bm{\phi}(s,a)\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{m,t+1}^{-1}}\right].

Proof of Lemma
D.9
.

This proof is similar to that of Lemma 6 in
(
Zanette et al. 2021
)
.

For any
m\in[M]
and
t\geq 0
, since
\bm{\Sigma}_{m,t+1}\succeq\bm{\Sigma}_{m,t}
, we have
\bm{\Sigma}_{m,t}^{-1}\succeq\bm{\Sigma}_{m,t+1}^{-1}
. Hence, for any
m\in[M]
,
t\geq 0
,
s\in\mathcal{S}
and
a\in\mathcal{A}
, we have

\displaystyle\bm{\phi}(s,a)^{\top}\hat{\bm{B}}\bm{\Sigma}_{m,t}^{-1}\hat{\bm{B}}^{\top}\bm{\phi}(s,a)\geq\bm{\phi}(s,a)^{\top}\hat{\bm{B}}\bm{\Sigma}_{m,t+1}^{-1}\hat{\bm{B}}^{\top}\bm{\phi}(s,a),

which implies that

\displaystyle\left\|\hat{\bm{B}}^{\top}\bm{\phi}(s,a)\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{m,t}^{-1}}\geq\left\|\hat{\bm{B}}^{\top}\bm{\phi}(s,a)\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{m,t+1}^{-1}}.

Therefore, for any
m\in[M]
and
t\geq 0
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{s\sim\mathcal{D}}\left[\max_{a\in\mathcal{A}}\left\|\hat{\bm{B}}^{\top}\bm{\phi}(s,a)\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{m,t}^{-1}}\right]\geq\mathbb{E}_{s\sim\mathcal{D}}\left[\max_{a\in\mathcal{A}}\left\|\hat{\bm{B}}^{\top}\bm{\phi}(s,a)\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{m,t+1}^{-1}}\right].

∎

In subroutine
\mathtt{EstLowRep}
, for any
m\in[M]
and
t>0
, let
\xi_{m,t}
denote the noise of the sample at timestep
t
for task
m
(Line
6
in Algorithm
6
).

Define event

\displaystyle\mathcal{H}:=\Bigg\{

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{\tau=1}^{t}\hat{\bm{B}}^{\top}\bm{\phi}(s_{m,\tau},a_{m,\tau})\xi_{m,\tau}\right\|_{\left(\gamma I+\sum_{\tau=1}^{t}\hat{\bm{B}}^{\top}\bm{\phi}(s_{m,\tau},a_{m,\tau})\bm{\phi}(s_{m,\tau},a_{m,\tau})^{\top}\hat{\bm{B}}\right)^{-1}}\leq

\displaystyle k\log\left(1+\frac{t}{\gamma k}\right)+2\log\left(\frac{5}{\delta}\right),\ \forall m\in[M],\ \forall t>0\Bigg\}.

Lemma D.10

(Martingale Concentration of the Variance Term)
.

It holds that

\displaystyle\Pr\left[\mathcal{H}\right]\geq 1-\frac{\delta}{5}.

Proof of Lemma
D.10
.

Let
\delta^{\prime}
be a confidence parameter which will be chosen later.
Since
\hat{\bm{B}}
is fixed before sampling
(s_{m,\tau},a_{m,\tau})
for all
m\in[M]
and
\tau>0
, using Lemma
E.7
, we have that with probability at least
1-\delta^{\prime}
, for any task
m\in[M]
and
t>0
,

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{\tau=1}^{t}\hat{\bm{B}}^{\top}\bm{\phi}(s_{m,\tau},a_{m,\tau})\xi_{m,j}\right\|_{\left(\gamma I+\sum_{\tau=1}^{t}\hat{\bm{B}}^{\top}\bm{\phi}(s_{m,\tau},a_{m,\tau})\bm{\phi}(s_{m,\tau},a_{m,\tau})^{\top}\hat{\bm{B}}\right)^{-1}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\log\left(\frac{\det\left(\gamma I+\sum_{\tau=1}^{t}\hat{\bm{B}}^{\top}\bm{\phi}(s_{m,\tau},a_{m,\tau})\bm{\phi}(s_{m,\tau},a_{m,\tau})^{\top}\hat{\bm{B}}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\det\left(\gamma I\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\cdot\delta^{\prime}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\log\left(\frac{\det\left(\gamma I+\sum_{\tau=1}^{t}\hat{\bm{B}}^{\top}\bm{\phi}(s_{m,\tau},a_{m,\tau})\bm{\phi}(s_{m,\tau},a_{m,\tau})^{\top}\hat{\bm{B}}\right)}{\det\left(\gamma I\right)}\right)+2\log\left(\frac{1}{\delta^{\prime}}\right)

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{(a)}}{\leq}

\displaystyle k\log\left(1+\frac{t}{\gamma k}\right)+2\log\left(\frac{1}{\delta^{\prime}}\right),

where inequality (a) uses Lemma
D.8
.

Letting
\delta^{\prime}=\frac{\delta}{5}
, we obtain this lemma.
∎

Define event

\displaystyle\mathcal{J}:=\Bigg\{

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{N}\mathbb{E}_{s\sim\mathcal{D}}\left[\max_{a\in\mathcal{A}}\left\|\hat{\bm{B}}^{\top}\bm{\phi}(s,a)\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{t-1}^{-1}}\right]\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{4}\left(2\sqrt{\log\left(\frac{5}{\delta}\right)}+\sqrt{4\log\left(\frac{5}{\delta}\right)+4\left(\sum_{t=1}^{N}\max_{a\in\mathcal{A}}\left\|\hat{\bm{B}}^{\top}\bm{\phi}(s_{t},a)\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{t-1}^{-1}}+2\log\left(\frac{5}{\delta}\right)\right)}\right)^{2}\Bigg\}.

Lemma D.11
.

It holds that

\displaystyle\Pr\left[\mathcal{J}\right]\geq 1-\frac{\delta}{5}.

Proof of Lemma
D.11
.

Using Lemma
E.8
, we can obtain this lemma.
∎

Lemma D.12
.

Suppose that event
\mathcal{K}\cap\mathcal{L}\cap\mathcal{G}\cap\mathcal{H}\cap\mathcal{J}
holds. For any task
m\in[M]
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{s\sim\mathcal{D}}

\displaystyle\left[\max_{a\in\mathcal{A}}\left|\bm{\phi}(s,a)^{\top}\left(\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{m,N}-\bm{\theta}_{m}\right)\right|\right]\leq\left(2\sqrt{\frac{2k\log\left(1+\frac{N}{\gamma k}\right)}{N}}+\frac{8\log\left(\frac{5}{\delta}\right)}{N}\right)\cdot

\displaystyle\left(\left\|\hat{\bm{B}}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{B}\right\|\sqrt{Nk}+\sqrt{k\log\left(1+\frac{N}{\gamma k}\right)+2\log\left(\frac{5}{\delta}\right)}+\sqrt{\gamma}\right)+\left\|\hat{\bm{B}}_{\bot}^{\top}\bm{B}\right\|.

Furthermore, if

\displaystyle N=\left\lceil\frac{4^{2}\cdot 26^{4}\cdot 24^{2}\cdot 2\left(k^{2}+k\gamma L_{\theta}^{2}\right)\log^{4}\big({\frac{240(k+\sqrt{k\gamma}L_{\theta})}{\varepsilon\delta}}\big)}{\varepsilon^{2}}\right\rceil,

then

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{s\sim\mathcal{D}}\left[\max_{a\in\mathcal{A}}\left|\bm{\phi}(s,a)^{\top}\left(\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{m,N}-\bm{\theta}_{m}\right)\right|\right]\leq\frac{\varepsilon}{2}

Proof of Lemma
D.12
.

For any task
m\in[M]
and
t\in[N]
,

\displaystyle\hat{\bm{w}}_{m,t}=

\displaystyle\bm{\Sigma}_{m,t}^{-1}\sum_{\tau=1}^{t}\hat{\bm{B}}^{\top}\bm{\phi}(s_{m,\tau},a_{m,\tau})r_{m,\tau}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(\gamma I+\sum_{\tau=1}^{t}\hat{\bm{B}}^{\top}\bm{\phi}(s_{m,\tau},a_{m,\tau})\bm{\phi}(s_{m,\tau},a_{m,\tau})^{\top}\hat{\bm{B}}\right)^{-1}\sum_{\tau=1}^{t}\hat{\bm{B}}^{\top}\bm{\phi}(s_{m,\tau},a_{m,\tau})\left(\bm{\phi}(s_{m,\tau},a_{m,\tau})^{\top}\bm{\theta}_{m}+\xi_{m,j}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(\gamma I+\sum_{\tau=1}^{t}\hat{\bm{B}}^{\top}\bm{\phi}(s_{m,\tau},a_{m,\tau})\bm{\phi}(s_{m,\tau},a_{m,\tau})^{\top}\hat{\bm{B}}\right)^{-1}\sum_{\tau=1}^{t}\hat{\bm{B}}^{\top}\bm{\phi}(s_{m,\tau},a_{m,\tau})\cdot

\displaystyle\left(\bm{\phi}(s_{m,\tau},a_{m,\tau})^{\top}\hat{\bm{B}}\hat{\bm{B}}^{\top}\bm{\theta}_{m}+\bm{\phi}(s_{m,\tau},a_{m,\tau})^{\top}\hat{\bm{B}}_{\perp}\hat{\bm{B}}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\theta}_{m}+\xi_{m,j}\right)

\displaystyle+\gamma\left(\gamma I+\sum_{\tau=1}^{t}\hat{\bm{B}}^{\top}\bm{\phi}(s_{m,\tau},a_{m,\tau})\bm{\phi}(s_{m,\tau},a_{m,\tau})^{\top}\hat{\bm{B}}\right)^{-1}\hat{\bm{B}}^{\top}\bm{\theta}_{m}

\displaystyle-\gamma\left(\gamma I+\sum_{\tau=1}^{t}\hat{\bm{B}}^{\top}\bm{\phi}(s_{m,\tau},a_{m,\tau})\bm{\phi}(s_{m,\tau},a_{m,\tau})^{\top}\hat{\bm{B}}\right)^{-1}\hat{\bm{B}}^{\top}\bm{\theta}_{m}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\hat{\bm{B}}^{\top}\bm{\theta}_{m}+\left(\gamma I+\sum_{\tau=1}^{t}\hat{\bm{B}}^{\top}\bm{\phi}(s_{m,\tau},a_{m,\tau})\bm{\phi}(s_{m,\tau},a_{m,\tau})^{\top}\hat{\bm{B}}\right)^{-1}\cdot

\displaystyle\sum_{\tau=1}^{t}\hat{\bm{B}}^{\top}\bm{\phi}(s_{m,\tau},a_{m,\tau})\bm{\phi}(s_{m,\tau},a_{m,\tau})^{\top}\hat{\bm{B}}_{\perp}\hat{\bm{B}}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{B}\bm{w}_{m}

\displaystyle+\left(\gamma I+\sum_{\tau=1}^{t}\hat{\bm{B}}^{\top}\bm{\phi}(s_{m,\tau},a_{m,\tau})\bm{\phi}(s_{m,\tau},a_{m,\tau})^{\top}\hat{\bm{B}}\right)^{-1}\sum_{\tau=1}^{t}\hat{\bm{B}}^{\top}\bm{\phi}(s_{m,\tau},a_{m,\tau})\xi_{m,j}

\displaystyle-\gamma\left(\gamma I+\sum_{\tau=1}^{t}\hat{\bm{B}}^{\top}\bm{\phi}(s_{m,\tau},a_{m,\tau})\bm{\phi}(s_{m,\tau},a_{m,\tau})^{\top}\hat{\bm{B}}\right)^{-1}\hat{\bm{B}}^{\top}\bm{\theta}_{m}.

Hence, for any task
m\in[M]
,
t\in[N]
and
(s,a)\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{A}
,

\displaystyle\bm{\phi}(s,a)^{\top}\left(\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{m,t}-\bm{\theta}_{m}\right)=

\displaystyle\bm{\phi}(s,a)^{\top}\hat{\bm{B}}\hat{\bm{w}}_{m,t}-\bm{\phi}(s,a)^{\top}\left(\hat{\bm{B}}\hat{\bm{B}}^{\top}+\hat{\bm{B}}_{\bot}\hat{\bm{B}}_{\bot}^{\top}\right)\bm{\theta}_{m}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\bm{\phi}(s,a)^{\top}\hat{\bm{B}}\left(\hat{\bm{w}}_{m,t}-\hat{\bm{B}}^{\top}\bm{\theta}_{m}\right)-\bm{\phi}(s,a)^{\top}\hat{\bm{B}}_{\bot}\hat{\bm{B}}_{\bot}^{\top}\bm{\theta}_{m}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\bm{\phi}(s,a)^{\top}\hat{\bm{B}}\left(\gamma I+\sum_{\tau=1}^{t}\hat{\bm{B}}^{\top}\bm{\phi}(s_{m,\tau},a_{m,\tau})\bm{\phi}(s_{m,\tau},a_{m,\tau})^{\top}\hat{\bm{B}}\right)^{-1}\cdot

\displaystyle\sum_{\tau=1}^{t}\hat{\bm{B}}^{\top}\bm{\phi}(s_{m,\tau},a_{m,\tau})\bm{\phi}(s_{m,\tau},a_{m,\tau})^{\top}\hat{\bm{B}}_{\perp}\hat{\bm{B}}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{B}\bm{w}_{m}

\displaystyle+\bm{\phi}(s,a)^{\top}\hat{\bm{B}}\left(\gamma I+\sum_{\tau=1}^{t}\hat{\bm{B}}^{\top}\bm{\phi}(s_{m,\tau},a_{m,\tau})\bm{\phi}(s_{m,\tau},a_{m,\tau})^{\top}\hat{\bm{B}}\right)^{-1}\sum_{\tau=1}^{t}\hat{\bm{B}}^{\top}\bm{\phi}(s_{m,\tau},a_{m,\tau})\xi_{m,j}

\displaystyle-\!\gamma\bm{\phi}(s,a)^{\!\top}\!\hat{\bm{B}}\left(\!\gamma I\!+\!\sum_{\tau=1}^{t}\hat{\bm{B}}^{\!\top}\bm{\phi}(s_{m,\tau},a_{m,\tau})\bm{\phi}(s_{m,\tau},a_{m,\tau})^{\!\top}\hat{\bm{B}}\!\right)^{\!\!-1}\!\!\!\!\!\!\hat{\bm{B}}^{\top}\!\bm{\theta}_{m}\!-\!\bm{\phi}(s,a)^{\!\top}\!\hat{\bm{B}}_{\bot}\hat{\bm{B}}_{\bot}^{\!\top}\bm{B}\bm{w}_{m}.

For any
m\in[M]
, let
\bm{\Sigma}_{m,0}:=\gamma I
. For any
m\in[M]
and
t\geq 1
, let
\bm{\Sigma}_{m,t}:=\gamma I+\sum_{\tau=1}^{t}\hat{\bm{B}}^{\top}\bm{\phi}(s_{m,\tau},a_{m,\tau})\bm{\phi}(s_{m,\tau},a_{m,\tau})^{\top}\hat{\bm{B}}
.

Taking the absolute value on both sides and using the Cauchy–Schwarz inequality, we obtain that for any
m\in[M]
,
t\in[N]
and
(s,a)\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{A}
,

\displaystyle\left|\bm{\phi}(s,a)^{\top}\left(\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{m,t}-\bm{\theta}_{m}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left\|\hat{\bm{B}}^{\top}\bm{\phi}(s,a)\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{m,t}^{-1}}\left\|\sum_{\tau=1}^{t}\hat{\bm{B}}^{\top}\bm{\phi}(s_{m,\tau},a_{m,\tau})\bm{\phi}(s_{m,\tau},a_{m,\tau})^{\top}\hat{\bm{B}}_{\perp}\hat{\bm{B}}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{B}\bm{w}_{m}\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{m,t}^{-1}}

\displaystyle+\left\|\hat{\bm{B}}^{\top}\bm{\phi}(s,a)\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{m,t}^{-1}}\left\|\sum_{\tau=1}^{t}\hat{\bm{B}}^{\top}\bm{\phi}(s_{m,\tau},a_{m,\tau})\xi_{m,j}\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{m,t}^{-1}}

\displaystyle+\gamma\left\|\hat{\bm{B}}^{\top}\bm{\phi}(s,a)\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{m,t}^{-1}}\left\|\hat{\bm{B}}^{\top}\bm{\theta}_{m}\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{m,t}^{-1}}

\displaystyle+\left|\bm{\phi}(s,a)^{\top}\hat{\bm{B}}_{\bot}\hat{\bm{B}}_{\bot}^{\top}\bm{B}\bm{w}_{m}\right|

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{(a)}}{\leq}

\displaystyle\left\|\hat{\bm{B}}^{\top}\bm{\phi}(s,a)\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{m,t}^{-1}}\sum_{\tau=1}^{t}\left|\bm{\phi}(s_{m,\tau},a_{m,\tau})^{\top}\hat{\bm{B}}_{\perp}\hat{\bm{B}}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{B}\bm{w}_{m}\right|\cdot\left\|\hat{\bm{B}}^{\top}\bm{\phi}(s_{m,\tau},a_{m,\tau})\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{m,t}^{-1}}

\displaystyle+\left\|\hat{\bm{B}}^{\top}\bm{\phi}(s,a)\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{m,t}^{-1}}\sqrt{k\log\left(1+\frac{t}{\gamma k}\right)+2\log\left(\frac{5}{\delta}\right)}

\displaystyle+\gamma\left\|\hat{\bm{B}}^{\top}\bm{\phi}(s,a)\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{m,t}^{-1}}\cdot\frac{1}{\sqrt{\gamma}}\cdot\left\|\hat{\bm{B}}^{\top}\bm{\theta}_{m}\right\|+\left\|\hat{\bm{B}}_{\bot}^{\top}\bm{B}\right\|L_{\phi}L_{w}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left\|\hat{\bm{B}}^{\top}\bm{\phi}(s,a)\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{m,t}^{-1}}\cdot\left\|\hat{\bm{B}}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{B}\right\|L_{\phi}L_{w}\cdot\sum_{\tau=1}^{t}\left\|\hat{\bm{B}}^{\top}\bm{\phi}(s_{m,\tau},a_{m,\tau})\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{m,t}^{-1}}

\displaystyle+\left\|\hat{\bm{B}}^{\top}\bm{\phi}(s,a)\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{m,t}^{-1}}\sqrt{k\log\left(1+\frac{t}{\gamma k}\right)+2\log\left(\frac{5}{\delta}\right)}

\displaystyle+\sqrt{\gamma}L_{\theta}\left\|\hat{\bm{B}}^{\top}\bm{\phi}(s,a)\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{m,t}^{-1}}+\left\|\hat{\bm{B}}_{\bot}^{\top}\bm{B}\right\|L_{\phi}L_{w}

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{(b)}}{\leq}

\displaystyle\left\|\hat{\bm{B}}^{\top}\bm{\phi}(s,a)\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{m,t}^{-1}}\cdot\left\|\hat{\bm{B}}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{B}\right\|L_{\phi}L_{w}\cdot\sqrt{tk}

\displaystyle+\left\|\hat{\bm{B}}^{\top}\bm{\phi}(s,a)\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{m,t}^{-1}}\sqrt{k\log\left(1+\frac{t}{\gamma k}\right)+2\log\left(\frac{5}{\delta}\right)}

\displaystyle+\sqrt{\gamma}L_{\theta}\left\|\hat{\bm{B}}^{\top}\bm{\phi}(s,a)\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{m,t}^{-1}}+\left\|\hat{\bm{B}}_{\bot}^{\top}\bm{B}\right\|L_{\phi}L_{w}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\|\hat{\bm{B}}^{\top}\bm{\phi}(s,a)\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{m,t}^{-1}}\left(\left\|\hat{\bm{B}}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{B}\right\|L_{\phi}L_{w}\cdot\sqrt{tk}+\sqrt{k\log\left(1+\frac{t}{\gamma k}\right)+2\log\left(\frac{5}{\delta}\right)}+\sqrt{\gamma}L_{\theta}\right)

\displaystyle+\left\|\hat{\bm{B}}_{\bot}^{\top}\bm{B}\right\|L_{\phi}L_{w},

where inequality (a) uses the triangle inequality and the definition of event
\mathcal{H}
, and inequality (b) is due to Lemma
E.6
.

Taking the maximum over
a\in\mathcal{A}
and taking the expectation on
s\sim\mathcal{D}
, we have that for any task
m\in[M]
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{s\sim\mathcal{D}}\left[\max_{a\in\mathcal{A}}\left|\bm{\phi}(s,a)^{\top}\left(\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{m,N}-\bm{\theta}_{m}\right)\right|\right]\leq

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{s\sim\mathcal{D}}\left[\max_{a\in\mathcal{A}}\left\|\hat{\bm{B}}^{\top}\bm{\phi}(s,a)\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{N}^{-1}}\right]\cdot

\displaystyle\left(\left\|\hat{\bm{B}}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{B}\right\|L_{\phi}L_{w}\cdot\sqrt{Nk}+\sqrt{k\log\left(1+\frac{N}{\gamma k}\right)+2\log\left(\frac{5}{\delta}\right)}+\sqrt{\gamma}L_{\theta}\right)

\displaystyle+\left\|\hat{\bm{B}}_{\bot}^{\top}\bm{B}\right\|L_{\phi}L_{w}.

(30)

According to Lemma
D.9
,
\mathbb{E}_{s\sim\mathcal{D}}\left[\max_{a\in\mathcal{A}}\left\|\hat{\bm{B}}^{\top}\bm{\phi}(s,a)\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{t}^{-1}}\right]
is non-increasing with respect to
t
. Hence, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{s\sim\mathcal{D}}\left[\max_{a\in\mathcal{A}}\left\|\hat{\bm{B}}^{\top}\bm{\phi}(s,a)\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{N}^{-1}}\right]\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N}\sum_{t=1}^{N}\mathbb{E}_{s\sim\mathcal{D}}\left[\max_{a\in\mathcal{A}}\left\|\hat{\bm{B}}^{\top}\bm{\phi}(s,a)\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{t}^{-1}}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N}\sum_{t=1}^{N}\mathbb{E}_{s\sim\mathcal{D}}\left[\max_{a\in\mathcal{A}}\left\|\hat{\bm{B}}^{\top}\bm{\phi}(s,a)\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{t-1}^{-1}}\right]

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{(a)}}{\leq}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{4N}\Bigg(2\sqrt{\log\left(\frac{5}{\delta}\right)}

\displaystyle+\sqrt{4\log\left(\frac{5}{\delta}\right)+4\left(\sum_{t=1}^{N}\max_{a\in\mathcal{A}}\left\|\hat{\bm{B}}^{\top}\bm{\phi}(s_{t},a)\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{t-1}^{-1}}+2\log\left(\frac{5}{\delta}\right)\right)}\Bigg)^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{4N}\left(2\sqrt{\log\left(\frac{5}{\delta}\right)}+\sqrt{4\log\left(\frac{5}{\delta}\right)+4\left(\sum_{t=1}^{N}\left\|\hat{\bm{B}}^{\top}\bm{\phi}(s_{t},a_{t})\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{t-1}^{-1}}+2\log\left(\frac{5}{\delta}\right)\right)}\right)^{\!\!\!2}\!\!,

(31)

where inequality (a) is due to the definition of event
\mathcal{J}
.

In addition, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{N}\left\|\hat{\bm{B}}^{\top}\bm{\phi}(s_{t},a_{t})\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{t-1}^{-1}}\leq

\displaystyle\sqrt{N\cdot\sum_{t=1}^{N}\left\|\hat{\bm{B}}^{\top}\bm{\phi}(s_{t},a_{t})\right\|^{2}_{\bm{\Sigma}_{t-1}^{-1}}}

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{(a)}}{\leq}

\displaystyle\sqrt{2N\log\left(\frac{\det\left(\gamma I+\sum_{\tau=1}^{N}\hat{\bm{B}}^{\top}\bm{\phi}(s_{m,\tau},a_{m,\tau})\bm{\phi}(s_{m,\tau},a_{m,\tau})^{\top}\hat{\bm{B}}\right)}{\det\left(\gamma I\right)}\right)}

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{(b)}}{\leq}

\displaystyle\sqrt{2Nk\log\left(1+\frac{N}{\gamma k}\right)},

(32)

where inequality (a) uses Lemma
E.9
, and inequality (b) is due to Lemma
D.8
.

Combining Eqs. (
31
) and (
32
), we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{s\sim\mathcal{D}}\left[\max_{a\in\mathcal{A}}\left\|\hat{\bm{B}}^{\top}\bm{\phi}(s,a)\right\|_{\bm{\Sigma}_{N}^{-1}}\right]\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{4N}\left(2\sqrt{\log\left(\frac{5}{\delta}\right)}+\sqrt{4\log\left(\frac{5}{\delta}\right)+4\left(\sqrt{2Nk\log\left(1+\frac{N}{\gamma k}\right)}+2\log\left(\frac{5}{\delta}\right)\right)}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{(a)}}{\leq}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2N}\left(4\log\left(\frac{5}{\delta}\right)+4\log\left(\frac{5}{\delta}\right)+4\left(\sqrt{2Nk\log\left(1+\frac{N}{\gamma k}\right)}+2\log\left(\frac{5}{\delta}\right)\right)\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{N}\left(2\sqrt{2Nk\log\left(1+\frac{N}{\gamma k}\right)}+8\log\left(\frac{5}{\delta}\right)\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2\sqrt{\frac{2k\log\left(1+\frac{N}{\gamma k}\right)}{N}}+\frac{8\log\left(\frac{5}{\delta}\right)}{N},

(33)

where inequality (a) uses the Cauchy–Schwarz inequality.

Furthermore, plugging Eq. (
33
) into Eq. (
30
) and using
\gamma\geq 1
, we have that for
N\geq 1
and
\sqrt{k}\log(2N)\geq 1
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{s\sim\mathcal{D}}\left[\max_{a\in\mathcal{A}}\left|\bm{\phi}(s,a)^{\top}\left(\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{m,N}-\bm{\theta}_{m}\right)\right|\right]

(34)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(2\sqrt{\frac{2k\log\left(1+\frac{N}{\gamma k}\right)}{N}}+\frac{8\log\left(\frac{5}{\delta}\right)}{N}\right)\cdot

\displaystyle\left(\left\|\hat{\bm{B}}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{B}\right\|L_{\phi}L_{w}\sqrt{Nk}+\sqrt{k\log\left(1+\frac{N}{\gamma k}\right)+2\log\left(\frac{5}{\delta}\right)}+\sqrt{\gamma}L_{\theta}\right)+\left\|\hat{\bm{B}}_{\bot}^{\top}\bm{B}\right\|L_{\phi}L_{w}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{12\sqrt{k}\log\left(\frac{5N}{\delta}\right)}{\sqrt{N}}\left(\left\|\hat{\bm{B}}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{B}\right\|L_{\phi}L_{w}\sqrt{Nk}+2\sqrt{k}\log\left(\frac{5N}{\delta}\right)+\sqrt{\gamma}L_{\theta}\right)+\left\|\hat{\bm{B}}_{\bot}^{\top}\bm{B}\right\|L_{\phi}L_{w}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{\left(24k+12\sqrt{k\gamma}L_{\theta}\right)\log^{2}\left(\frac{5N}{\delta}\right)}{\sqrt{N}}+24k\log\left(\frac{5N}{\delta}\right)\left\|\hat{\bm{B}}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{B}\right\|L_{\phi}L_{w}.

(35)

Using Lemma
E.4
with
A=24k+12\sqrt{k\gamma}L_{\theta}
,
B=\frac{5}{\delta}
and
\kappa=\frac{\varepsilon}{4}
, we have that if

\displaystyle N\geq\frac{26^{4}\left(24k+12\sqrt{k\gamma}L_{\theta}\right)^{2}\log^{4}\big({\frac{2\cdot 5(24k+12\sqrt{k\gamma}L_{\theta})}{\varepsilon\delta}}\big)}{\left(\frac{\varepsilon}{4}\right)^{2}},

then
\frac{\left(24k+12\sqrt{k\gamma}L_{\theta}\right)\log^{2}\left(\frac{5N}{\delta}\right)}{\sqrt{N}}\leq\frac{\varepsilon}{4}
.

Further enlarging
N
, if

\displaystyle N\geq\frac{4^{2}\cdot 26^{4}\cdot 24^{2}\cdot 2\left(k^{2}+k\gamma L_{\theta}^{2}\right)\log^{4}\big({\frac{240(k+\sqrt{k\gamma}L_{\theta})}{\varepsilon\delta}}\big)}{\varepsilon^{2}},

(36)

then

\displaystyle\frac{\left(24k+12\sqrt{k\gamma}L_{\theta}\right)\log^{2}\left(\frac{5N}{\delta}\right)}{\sqrt{N}}\leq\frac{\varepsilon}{4}.

According to Lemma
D.7
, we have
\left\|\hat{\bm{B}}_{t,\bot}^{\top}\bm{B}\right\|\leq\frac{\varepsilon}{96k\log\left(\frac{5N}{\delta}\right)L_{\phi}L_{w}}
.

Thus, setting
N
as the value in Eq. (
36
), and continuing with Eq. (
35
), we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{s\sim\mathcal{D}}\left[\max_{a\in\mathcal{A}}\left|\bm{\phi}(s,a)^{\top}\left(\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{m,N}-\bm{\theta}_{m}\right)\right|\right]\leq\frac{\varepsilon}{4}+\frac{\varepsilon}{4}=\frac{\varepsilon}{2}.

∎

D.4
Proof of Theorem
5.1

Proof of Theorem
5.1
.

Combining Lemmas
D.2
,
D.3
,
D.6
,
D.10
and
D.11
, we have that
Pr[\mathcal{K}\cap\mathcal{L}\cap\mathcal{G}\cap\mathcal{H}\cap\mathcal{J}]\geq 1-\delta
.
Suppose that event
\mathcal{K}\cap\mathcal{L}\cap\mathcal{G}\cap\mathcal{H}\cap\mathcal{J}
holds.

First, we uses a similar analytical procedure as that
in
(
Zanette et al. 2021
)
to prove the correctness.

Using Lemma
D.12
, we have that for any task
m\in[M]
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{s\sim\mathcal{D}}\left[\max_{a\in\mathcal{A}}\left|\bm{\phi}(s,a)^{\top}\left(\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{m,N}-\bm{\theta}_{m}\right)\right|\right]\leq\frac{\varepsilon}{2}.

For any
m\in[M]
and
s\in\mathcal{S}
, let
\beta_{m}(s):=\max_{a\in\mathcal{A}}|\bm{\phi}(s,a)^{\top}(\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{m,N}-\bm{\theta}_{m})|
and
\pi^{*}_{m}(s):=\operatornamewithlimits{argmax}_{a\in\mathcal{A}}\bm{\phi}(s,a)^{\top}\bm{\theta}_{m}
.

For any
m\in[M]
and
s\in\mathcal{S}
, we have

\displaystyle\bm{\phi}(s,\hat{\pi}_{m}(s))^{\top}\bm{\theta}_{m}\geq

\displaystyle\bm{\phi}(s,\hat{\pi}_{m}(s))^{\top}\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{m,N}-\beta_{m}(s)

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{(a)}}{\geq}

\displaystyle\bm{\phi}(s,\pi^{*}_{m}(s))^{\top}\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{m,N}-\beta_{m}(s)

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\bm{\phi}(s,\pi^{*}_{m}(s))^{\top}\bm{\theta}_{m}-2\beta_{m}(s),

where inequality (a) is due to that
\hat{\pi}_{m}(s)
is greedy with respect to
\hat{\bm{\theta}}_{m,N}
.

Rearranging the above equation and taking the expectation of
s
on both sides, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{s\sim\mathcal{D}}\left[\max_{a\in\mathcal{A}}\left(\bm{\phi}(s,a)-\bm{\phi}(s,\hat{\pi}_{m}(s))\right)^{\top}\bm{\theta}_{m}\right]\leq 2\mathbb{E}_{s\sim\mathcal{D}}[\beta_{m}(s)]\leq\varepsilon.

Now we prove the sample complexity.
Summing the number of samples used in the main algorithm of
\mathtt{C\mathchar 45\relax DouExpDes}
and subroutines
\mathtt{C\mathchar 45\relax FeatRecover}
and
\mathtt{EstLowRep}
(Line
in Algorithm
4
, Lines
4
-
5
in Algorithm
5
and Line
6
in Algorithm
6
), we have that the total number of samples is bounded by

\displaystyle T_{0}+2MTp+MN

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O\Bigg(\frac{L_{\phi}^{4}}{\nu^{2}}\log^{2}\left(\frac{d|\mathcal{A}|}{\delta}\right)+\frac{k^{4}L_{\phi}^{4}L_{\theta}^{2}L_{w}^{2}}{\nu^{2}\varepsilon^{2}}\log^{6}\left(\frac{kdL_{\phi}L_{\theta}L_{w}N}{\nu\delta\varepsilon}\right)\cdot\frac{L_{\phi}^{4}}{\nu^{2}}\log^{2}\left(\frac{dMT}{\delta}\right)

\displaystyle+M\cdot\frac{\left(k^{2}+k\gamma L_{\theta}^{2}\right)\log^{4}\big({\frac{k+\sqrt{k\gamma}L_{\theta}}{\varepsilon\delta}}\big)}{\varepsilon^{2}}\Bigg)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O\Bigg(\frac{k^{4}L_{\phi}^{4}L_{\theta}^{2}L_{w}^{2}}{\nu^{2}\varepsilon^{2}}\log^{6}\left(\frac{|\mathcal{A}|kdL_{\phi}L_{\theta}L_{w}N}{\nu\delta\varepsilon}\right)\cdot\frac{L_{\phi}^{4}}{\nu^{2}}\log^{2}\left(\frac{dMT}{\delta}\right)

\displaystyle+M\cdot\frac{\left(k^{2}+k\gamma L_{\theta}^{2}\right)\log^{4}\big({\frac{k+\sqrt{k\gamma}L_{\theta}}{\varepsilon\delta}}\big)}{\varepsilon^{2}}\Bigg)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\tilde{O}\Bigg(\frac{k^{4}L_{\phi}^{8}L_{\theta}^{2}L_{w}^{2}}{\nu^{4}\varepsilon^{2}}+\frac{M\left(k^{2}+k\gamma L_{\theta}^{2}\right)}{\varepsilon^{2}}\Bigg).

∎

Appendix E
Technical Tools

In this section, we provide some useful technical tools.

Lemma E.1

(Matrix Bernstern Inequality - Average, Lemma 31 in
(
Tripuraneni et al. 2021
)
)
.

Consider a truncation level
U>0
. If
\{\bm{Z}_{1},\dots,\bm{Z}_{n}\}
is a sequence of
d_{1}\times d_{2}
independent random matrices and

\bm{Z}^{\prime}_{i}=\bm{Z}_{i}\cdot\mathbbm{1}\left\{\|\bm{Z}_{i}\|\leq U\right\}
for any
i\in[n]
, then

\displaystyle\Pr\left[\left\|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(\bm{Z}_{i}-\mathbb{E}[\bm{Z}_{i}]\right)\right\|\geq t\right]\leq\Pr\left[\left\|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(\bm{Z}^{\prime}_{i}-\mathbb{E}[\bm{Z}^{\prime}_{i}]\right)\right\|\geq t-\Delta\right]+n\Pr\left[\|\bm{Z}_{i}\|\geq U\right],

where
\Delta\geq\|\mathbb{E}[\bm{Z}_{i}]-\mathbb{E}[\bm{Z}^{\prime}_{i}]\|
for any
i\in[n]
.

In addition, for
t\geq\Delta
, we have

\displaystyle\Pr\left[\left\|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(\bm{Z}^{\prime}_{i}-\mathbb{E}[\bm{Z}^{\prime}_{i}]\right)\right\|\geq t-\Delta\right]\leq(d_{1}+d_{2})\exp\left(-\frac{n^{2}(t-\Delta)^{2}}{2\sigma^{2}+\frac{2Un(t-\Delta)}{3}}\right),

where

\displaystyle\sigma^{2}=

\displaystyle\max\left\{\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}[(\bm{Z}^{\prime}_{i}-\mathbb{E}[\bm{Z}^{\prime}_{i}])^{\top}(\bm{Z}^{\prime}_{i}-\mathbb{E}[\bm{Z}^{\prime}_{i}])]\right\|,\ \left\|\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}[(\bm{Z}^{\prime}_{i}-\mathbb{E}[\bm{Z}^{\prime}_{i}])(\bm{Z}^{\prime}_{i}-\mathbb{E}[\bm{Z}^{\prime}_{i}])^{\top}]\right\|\right\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\max\left\{\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}[{\bm{Z}^{\prime}_{i}}^{\top}\bm{Z}^{\prime}_{i}]\right\|,\ \left\|\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}[\bm{Z}^{\prime}_{i}{\bm{Z}^{\prime}_{i}}^{\top}]\right\|\right\}.

Lemma 31 in
(
Tripuraneni et al. 2021
)
gives a truncated matrix Bernstern inequality for symmetric random matrices. Here we extend it to general random matrices.

Lemma
E.1
can be obtained by combining the truncation argument in the proof of Lemma 31 in
(
Tripuraneni et al. 2021
)
and Theorem 6.1.1 in
(
Tropp et al. 2015
)
(classic matrix Bernstern inequality for general random matrices).

Lemma E.2

(Matrix Bernstern Inequality - Summation)
.

Consider a truncation level
U>0
. If
\{\bm{Z}_{1},\dots,\bm{Z}_{n}\}
is a sequence of
d_{1}\times d_{2}
independent random matrices, and
\bm{Z}^{\prime}_{i}=\bm{Z}_{i}\cdot\mathbbm{1}\left\{\|\bm{Z}_{i}\|\leq U\right\}
and
\Delta\geq\|\mathbb{E}[\bm{Z}_{i}]-\mathbb{E}[\bm{Z}^{\prime}_{i}]\|
for any
i\in[n]
, then for
\tau\geq 2n\Delta
,

\displaystyle\Pr\left[\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(\bm{Z}_{i}-\mathbb{E}[\bm{Z}_{i}]\right)\right\|\geq\tau\right]\leq(d_{1}+d_{2})\exp\left(-\frac{1}{4}\cdot\frac{\tau^{2}}{2\sigma^{2}+\frac{U\tau}{3}}\right)+n\Pr\left[\|\bm{Z}_{i}\|\geq U\right],

where

\displaystyle\sigma^{2}=

\displaystyle\max\left\{\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}[(\bm{Z}^{\prime}_{i}-\mathbb{E}[\bm{Z}^{\prime}_{i}])^{\top}(\bm{Z}^{\prime}_{i}-\mathbb{E}[\bm{Z}^{\prime}_{i}])]\right\|,\ \left\|\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}[(\bm{Z}^{\prime}_{i}-\mathbb{E}[\bm{Z}^{\prime}_{i}])(\bm{Z}^{\prime}_{i}-\mathbb{E}[\bm{Z}^{\prime}_{i}])^{\top}]\right\|\right\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\max\left\{\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}[{\bm{Z}^{\prime}_{i}}^{\top}\bm{Z}^{\prime}_{i}]\right\|,\ \left\|\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}[\bm{Z}^{\prime}_{i}{\bm{Z}^{\prime}_{i}}^{\top}]\right\|\right\}.

Furthermore, we have

\displaystyle\Pr\left[\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(\bm{Z}_{i}-\mathbb{E}[\bm{Z}_{i}]\right)\right\|\geq 4\sqrt{\sigma^{2}\log\left(\frac{d_{1}+d_{2}}{\delta}\right)}+4U\log\left(\frac{d_{1}+d_{2}}{\delta}\right)\right]\leq\delta+n\Pr\left[\|\bm{Z}_{i}\|\geq U\right].

Proof of Lemma
E.2
.

Using Lemma
E.1
and defining
\tau:=nt
, we have that for
\tau>n\Delta
,

\displaystyle\Pr\left[\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(\bm{Z}_{i}-\mathbb{E}[\bm{Z}_{i}]\right)\right\|\geq\tau\right]\leq

\displaystyle(d_{1}+d_{2})\exp\left(-\frac{(\tau-n\Delta)^{2}}{2\sigma^{2}+\frac{2U(\tau-n\Delta)}{3}}\right)+n\Pr\left[\|\bm{Z}_{i}\|\geq U\right].

If
\tau>2n\Delta
, then
\tau-n\Delta>\frac{1}{2}\tau
and we have

\displaystyle\Pr\left[\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(\bm{Z}_{i}-\mathbb{E}[\bm{Z}_{i}]\right)\right\|\geq\tau\right]\leq

\displaystyle(d_{1}+d_{2})\exp\left(-\frac{\left(\frac{1}{2}\tau\right)^{2}}{2\sigma^{2}+\frac{2U\left(\frac{1}{2}\tau\right)}{3}}\right)+n\Pr\left[\|\bm{Z}_{i}\|\geq U\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(d_{1}+d_{2})\exp\left(-\frac{1}{4}\cdot\frac{\tau^{2}}{2\sigma^{2}+\frac{U\tau}{3}}\right)+n\Pr\left[\|\bm{Z}_{i}\|\geq U\right].

(37)

Plugging
\tau=4\sqrt{\sigma^{2}\log\left(\frac{d_{1}+d_{2}}{\delta}\right)}+4U\log\left(\frac{d_{1}+d_{2}}{\delta}\right)
into Eq. (
37
), we have

\displaystyle\Pr\left[\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(\bm{Z}_{i}-\mathbb{E}[\bm{Z}_{i}]\right)\right\|\geq 4\sqrt{\sigma^{2}\log\left(\frac{d_{1}+d_{2}}{\delta}\right)}+4U\log\left(\frac{d_{1}+d_{2}}{\delta}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(d_{1}+d_{2})\exp\left(-\frac{1}{4}\cdot\frac{16\sigma^{2}\log\left(\frac{d_{1}+d_{2}}{\delta}\right)+16U^{2}\log^{2}\left(\frac{d_{1}+d_{2}}{\delta}\right)+32U\log\left(\frac{d_{1}+d_{2}}{\delta}\right)\sqrt{\sigma^{2}\log\left(\frac{d_{1}+d_{2}}{\delta}\right)}}{2\sigma^{2}+\frac{1}{3}\left(4U\sqrt{\sigma^{2}\log\left(\frac{d_{1}+d_{2}}{\delta}\right)}+4U^{2}\log\left(\frac{d_{1}+d_{2}}{\delta}\right)\right)}\right)

\displaystyle+n\Pr\left[\|\bm{Z}_{i}\|\geq U\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(d_{1}+d_{2})\exp\left(-\frac{1}{4}\cdot 4\log\left(\frac{d_{1}+d_{2}}{\delta}\right)\right)+n\Pr\left[\|\bm{Z}_{i}\|\geq U\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\delta+n\Pr\left[\|\bm{Z}_{i}\|\geq U\right].

∎

Lemma E.3
.

For any
A,B>1
,
\kappa\in(0,1)
and
T>0
such that
\log\left(\frac{AB}{\kappa}\right)>1
and
\log(BT)>2
, if

T\geq\frac{68A^{2}\log^{2}\left(\frac{AB}{\kappa}\right)}{\kappa^{2}},

then

\frac{A}{\sqrt{T}}\log(BT)\leq\kappa.

Proof of Lemma
E.3
.

If
T=\frac{68A^{2}\log^{2}\left(\frac{AB}{\kappa}\right)}{\kappa^{2}}
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{A}{\sqrt{T}}\log(BT)=

\displaystyle\frac{A\kappa}{\sqrt{68}A\log\left(\frac{AB}{\kappa}\right)}\log\left(\frac{68A^{2}B\log^{2}\left(\frac{AB}{\kappa}\right)}{\kappa^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\kappa}{\sqrt{68}\log\left(\frac{AB}{\kappa}\right)}\left(\log\left(68\right)+\log\left(\frac{A^{2}B}{\kappa^{2}}\right)+\log\left(\log^{2}\left(\frac{AB}{\kappa}\right)\right)\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{\kappa}{\sqrt{68}\log\left(\frac{AB}{\kappa}\right)}\left(\log\left(68\right)+2\log\left(\frac{AB}{\kappa}\right)+2\log\left(\frac{AB}{\kappa}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{\kappa}{\sqrt{68}\log\left(\frac{AB}{\kappa}\right)}\left(\log\left(68\right)\log\left(\frac{AB}{\kappa}\right)+4\log\left(\frac{AB}{\kappa}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\kappa.

Let
f(T)=\frac{A}{\sqrt{T}}\log(BT)
. Then, the derivative of
f(T)
is

\displaystyle f^{\prime}(T)=\frac{2A-A\log(BT)}{2T\sqrt{T}}.

If
\log(BT)>2
, then
f^{\prime}(T)<0
, and thus
f(T)
is decreasing with respect to
T
.

Therefore, if
T\geq\frac{68A^{2}\log^{2}\left(\frac{AB}{\kappa}\right)}{\kappa^{2}}
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{A}{\sqrt{T}}\log(BT)\leq\kappa.

∎

Lemma E.4
.

For any
A,B>1
and
\kappa\in(0,1)
such that
\log(\frac{AB}{\kappa})>1
and
\log(BN)>4
, if

N\geq\frac{26^{4}A^{2}\log^{4}(\frac{AB}{\kappa})}{\kappa^{2}},

then

\frac{A\log^{2}\left(BN\right)}{\sqrt{N}}\leq\kappa.

Proof of Lemma
E.4
.

If
N=\frac{26^{4}A^{2}\log^{4}(\frac{AB}{\kappa})}{\kappa^{2}}
, we have

\kappa\sqrt{N}=26^{2}A\log^{2}(\frac{AB}{\kappa})
,
and

\displaystyle A\log^{2}\left(BN\right)=

\displaystyle A\log^{2}\left(\frac{26^{4}A^{2}B\log^{4}(\frac{AB}{\kappa})}{\kappa^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle A\log^{2}\left(\frac{26^{4}A^{2}B}{\kappa^{2}}\cdot\frac{A^{4}B^{4}}{\kappa^{4}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 36A\log^{2}\left(\frac{26AB}{\kappa}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 36A\left(\log\left(26\right)+\log\left(\frac{AB}{\kappa}\right)\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 36A\left(\log\left(26\right)\log\left(\frac{AB}{\kappa}\right)+\log\left(\frac{AB}{\kappa}\right)\right)^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 36\left(\log\left(26\right)+1\right)^{2}A\log^{2}\left(\frac{AB}{\kappa}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 26^{2}A\log^{2}\left(\frac{AB}{\kappa}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\kappa\sqrt{N},

and thus
\frac{A\log^{2}\left(BN\right)}{\sqrt{N}}\leq\kappa
.

Let
f(N)=\frac{A\log^{2}\left(BN\right)}{\sqrt{N}}
. Then, the derivative function of
f(N)
is

\displaystyle f^{\prime}(N)=\frac{4A\log(BN)-A\log^{2}(BN)}{2N\sqrt{N}}=\frac{A\log(BN)\cdot(4-\log(BN))}{2N\sqrt{N}}.

If
\log(BN)>4
, then
f^{\prime}(N)<0
, and thus
f(N)
is decreasing with respect to
N
.

Therefore, if
N\geq\frac{26^{4}A^{2}\log^{4}(\frac{AB}{\kappa})}{\kappa^{2}}
, we have

\frac{A\log^{2}\left(BN\right)}{\sqrt{N}}\leq\kappa
.
∎

Lemma E.5
.

For any
\bm{x}_{1},\dots,\bm{x}_{n}\in\mathbb{R}^{k}
, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{j=1}^{n}\|\bm{x}_{j}\|_{\left(\sum_{i=1}^{n}\bm{x}_{i}\bm{x}_{i}^{\top}\right)^{-1}}\leq\sqrt{nk}.

Proof of Lemma
E.5
.

It holds that

\displaystyle\sum_{j=1}^{n}\|\bm{x}_{j}\|_{\left(\sum_{i=1}^{n}\bm{x}_{i}\bm{x}_{i}^{\top}\right)^{-1}}=

\displaystyle\sum_{j=1}^{n}\sqrt{\bm{x}_{j}^{\top}\left(\sum_{i=1}^{n}\bm{x}_{i}\bm{x}_{i}^{\top}\right)^{-1}\bm{x}_{j}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sqrt{n\cdot\sum_{j=1}^{n}\bm{x}_{j}^{\top}\left(\sum_{i=1}^{n}\bm{x}_{i}\bm{x}_{i}^{\top}\right)^{-1}\bm{x}_{j}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sqrt{n\cdot\sum_{j=1}^{n}\textup{Trace}\left(\bm{x}_{j}^{\top}\left(\sum_{i=1}^{n}\bm{x}_{i}\bm{x}_{i}^{\top}\right)^{-1}\bm{x}_{j}\right)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sqrt{n\cdot\sum_{j=1}^{n}\textup{Trace}\left(\bm{x}_{j}\bm{x}_{j}^{\top}\left(\sum_{i=1}^{n}\bm{x}_{i}\bm{x}_{i}^{\top}\right)^{-1}\right)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sqrt{n\cdot\textup{Trace}\left(\sum_{j=1}^{n}\bm{x}_{j}\bm{x}_{j}^{\top}\left(\sum_{i=1}^{n}\bm{x}_{i}\bm{x}_{i}^{\top}\right)^{-1}\right)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sqrt{n\cdot\textup{Trace}\left(\bm{I}_{k}\right)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sqrt{nk}

∎

Lemma E.6
.

For any
\bm{x}_{1},\dots,\bm{x}_{n}\in\mathbb{R}^{k}
and
\gamma>0
, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{j=1}^{n}\|\bm{x}_{j}\|_{\left(\gamma I+\sum_{i=1}^{n}\bm{x}_{i}\bm{x}_{i}^{\top}\right)^{-1}}\leq\sqrt{nk}.

Proof of Lemma
E.6
.

It holds that

\displaystyle\sum_{j=1}^{n}\|\bm{x}_{j}\|_{\left(\gamma I+\sum_{i=1}^{n}\bm{x}_{i}\bm{x}_{i}^{\top}\right)^{-1}}=

\displaystyle\sum_{j=1}^{n}\sqrt{\bm{x}_{j}^{\top}\left(\gamma I+\sum_{i=1}^{n}\bm{x}_{i}\bm{x}_{i}^{\top}\right)^{-1}\bm{x}_{j}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sqrt{n\cdot\sum_{j=1}^{n}\bm{x}_{j}^{\top}\left(\gamma I+\sum_{i=1}^{n}\bm{x}_{i}\bm{x}_{i}^{\top}\right)^{-1}\bm{x}_{j}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sqrt{n\cdot\sum_{j=1}^{n}\textup{Trace}\left(\bm{x}_{j}^{\top}\left(\gamma I+\sum_{i=1}^{n}\bm{x}_{i}\bm{x}_{i}^{\top}\right)^{-1}\bm{x}_{j}\right)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sqrt{n\cdot\sum_{j=1}^{n}\textup{Trace}\left(\bm{x}_{j}\bm{x}_{j}^{\top}\left(\gamma I+\sum_{i=1}^{n}\bm{x}_{i}\bm{x}_{i}^{\top}\right)^{-1}\right)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sqrt{n\cdot\textup{Trace}\left(\sum_{j=1}^{n}\bm{x}_{j}\bm{x}_{j}^{\top}\left(\gamma I+\sum_{i=1}^{n}\bm{x}_{i}\bm{x}_{i}^{\top}\right)^{-1}\right)}

\displaystyle\overset{\textup{(a)}}{\leq}

\displaystyle\sqrt{n\!\cdot\!\left(\textup{Trace}\left(\sum_{j=1}^{n}\bm{x}_{j}\bm{x}_{j}^{\top}\left(\gamma I+\sum_{i=1}^{n}\bm{x}_{i}\bm{x}_{i}^{\top}\right)^{\!\!\!-1}\right)\!+\!\textup{Trace}\left(\gamma\left(\gamma I+\sum_{i=1}^{n}\bm{x}_{i}\bm{x}_{i}^{\top}\right)^{\!\!\!-1}\right)\right)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sqrt{n\cdot\textup{Trace}\left(\sum_{j=1}^{n}\bm{x}_{j}\bm{x}_{j}^{\top}\left(\gamma I+\sum_{i=1}^{n}\bm{x}_{i}\bm{x}_{i}^{\top}\right)^{-1}+\gamma\left(\gamma I+\sum_{i=1}^{n}\bm{x}_{i}\bm{x}_{i}^{\top}\right)^{-1}\right)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sqrt{n\cdot\textup{Trace}\left(\left(\gamma I+\sum_{j=1}^{n}\bm{x}_{j}\bm{x}_{j}^{\top}\right)\left(\gamma I+\sum_{i=1}^{n}\bm{x}_{i}\bm{x}_{i}^{\top}\right)^{-1}\right)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sqrt{n\cdot\textup{Trace}\left(\bm{I}_{k}\right)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sqrt{nk},

where inequality (a) is due to that
\left(\gamma I+\sum_{i=1}^{n}\bm{x}_{i}\bm{x}_{i}^{\top}\right)
is a positive definite matrix.
∎

Lemma E.7

(Self-normalized Concentration for Martingales, Theorem 1 in
(
Abbasi-Yadkori et al. 2011
)
)
.

Let
\{\mathcal{F}_{t}\}_{t=0}^{\infty}
be a filtration such that for any
t\geq 1
, the selected action
\bm{X}_{t}\in\mathbb{R}^{k}
is
\mathcal{F}_{t-1}
-measurable, the noise
\eta_{t}\in\mathbb{R}
is
\mathcal{F}_{t}
-measurable, and conditioning on
\mathcal{F}_{t-1}
,
\eta_{t}
is zero-mean and
R
-sub-Gaussian. Let
\bm{V}_{0}\in\mathbb{R}^{k\times k}
be a positive definite matrix and let
\bm{V}_{t}=\sum_{i=1}^{t}\bm{X}_{i}\bm{X}_{i}^{\top}
for any
t\geq 1
. Then, for any
\delta>0
, with probability at least
1-\delta
, for all
t\geq 1
,

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{t}\bm{X}_{i}\cdot\eta_{i}\right\|^{2}_{\left(\bm{V}_{0}+\bm{V}_{t}\right)^{-1}}\leq 2R^{2}\log\left(\frac{\det(\bm{V}_{t})^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\det(\bm{V}_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}\cdot\delta}\right).

Lemma E.8

(Reverse Bernstein Inequality for Martingales, Theorem 3 in
(
Zanette et al. 2021
)
)
.

Let
(\bm{\Sigma},\mathcal{F},\Pr[\cdot])
be a probability space and consider the stochastic process
\{\bm{X}_{t}\}
adapted to the filtration
\{\mathcal{F}_{t}\}
. Let
\mathbb{E}_{t}[\bm{X}_{t}]:=\mathbb{E}[\bm{X}_{t}|\mathcal{F}_{t-1}]
be the conditional expectation of
\bm{X}_{t}
given
\mathcal{F}_{t-1}
. If
0\leq\bm{X}_{t}\leq 1
then it holds that

\displaystyle\Pr\left[\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}_{t}[\bm{X}_{t}]\geq\frac{1}{4}\left(2\sqrt{\log\left(\frac{1}{\delta}\right)}+\sqrt{4\log\left(\frac{1}{\delta}\right)+4\left(\sum_{t=1}^{T}\bm{X}_{t}+2\log\left(\frac{1}{\delta}\right)\right)}\right)^{2}\right]\leq\delta.

Lemma E.9

(Elliptical Potential Lemma, Lemma 11 in
(
Abbasi-Yadkori et al. 2011
)
)
.

Let
\{\bm{X}_{t}\}_{t=1}^{\infty}
be a sequence in
\mathbb{R}^{k}
. Let
\bm{V}_{0}
be a
k\times k
positive definite matrix and let
\bm{V}_{t}=\bm{V}_{0}+\sum_{i=1}^{t}\bm{X}_{i}\bm{X}_{i}^{\top}
such that for any
t\geq 1
,
\|\bm{X}_{t}\|^{2}_{\bm{V}_{t-1}^{-1}}\leq 1
. Then, we have that

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{n}\left\|\bm{X}_{t}\right\|^{2}_{\bm{V}_{t-1}^{-1}}\leq 2\log\frac{\det(\bm{V}_{n})}{\det(\bm{V}_{0})}.

Lemma E.10

(Moments of Sub-Gaussian Random Variables, Proposition 3.2 in
(
Rivasplata 2012
)
)
.

For a
\sigma^{2}
-sub-Gaussian random variable
\bm{X}
which satisfies

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\exp\left(\mu\bm{X}\right)\right]\leq\exp\left(\frac{\sigma^{2}\mu^{2}}{2}\right),\ \forall\mu\in\mathbb{R},

we have that for any integer
n\geq 1
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[|\bm{X}|^{n}]\leq\left(2\sigma^{2}\right)^{\frac{n}{2}}n\cdot\Gamma\left(\frac{n}{2}\right),

where
\Gamma(n):=(n-1)!
for any integer
n\geq 1
.
